_Earlier on 2026-05-19 — **Sprint C2-B — operator-mediated Send to Client workflow landed.** The Sprint C2-A foundation is now mounted: per-token `Mark sent to client` buttons render on the report + proposal candidate panels for active eligible share tokens, opening the canon-verbatim `<SendToClientConfirmModal>` and firing the cookie-bound `markReportLinkSentToClientAction` / `markProposalLinkSentToClientAction` server actions on operator confirmation. **`Send to Client` per-token flow is now UNLOCKED for persisted UUID engagements on the report + proposal lanes only** — operator-mediated, copy-link-only handoff with sanitized audit metadata. The historic `LockedActionButton label="Send to Client"` at `proposal-workspace.tsx:417` (per-option, "send whole proposal" semantic) REMAINS LOCKED verbatim per `docs/29` § 18 hard binding — the per-token mark-sent flow is a separate surface in the share-token panel, not a replacement for the historic per-option control. **No schema, no migration, no API route, no public route, no new package dependency, no email pipeline, no CRM integration, no `mailto:` link, no e-signature, no PDF binary / storage bucket, no middleware / `next.config.mjs` / RLS change, no AI synthesis change, no Group-B wiring, no SOW share token, no public SOW route.** **New `components/reports/send-report-link-to-client-button.tsx`** — client component that renders one of two states per active share-token row: (a) audience label already on the token → primary `Mark sent to client` secondary-variant button with `Send` icon that opens `<SendToClientConfirmModal>` with `artifactKind='report'` + threaded `tokenId` + `initialAudienceLabel` + `hasRecipientEmailHash` + `shareUrlPath={null}` (canon-correct: SLATE never stored the raw token after the mint-once copy flow, so the modal renders without the URL recopy panel — the operator re-mints via the existing Generate Share Link button if a fresh URL is needed); (b) audience label missing → disabled neutral chip "Mark sent disabled" + operator-facing help "Audience label required before marking sent." On confirm the modal calls `markReportLinkSentToClientAction({shareTokenId, audienceConfirmation, recipientEmail, operatorConfirmed: true})`; on success the modal auto-closes after the success chip displays and the parent page revalidates so the panel re-renders with the new metadata. **New `components/proposals/send-proposal-link-to-client-button.tsx`** — byte-similar mirror against the proposal action with `artifactKind='proposal'`. **Both buttons mounted** beside the existing `<RevokeShareLinkButton>` / `<RevokeProposalShareLinkButton>` in the per-row action strip inside `ShareTokenRow` in `components/reports/report-pdf-candidates-panel.tsx` + `components/proposals/proposal-candidates-panel.tsx`; render only when `displayStatus === 'active'` (matching the existing revoke-button gate so revoked + expired tokens never see the send button). **Send-history metadata display** added to both `ShareTokenRow` server components: reads `metadata.sendCount` (defensive `typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v)` check; defaults 0) + `metadata.lastSentToClientAt` (defensive ISO string check) + `metadata.lastSentChannel` (defensive string check) via three pure reader helpers (`readSendCount` / `readLastSentAt` / `readLastSentChannel`); when `sendCount > 0` renders an additional metadata strip with "Marked sent by operator N time(s)" + "Last marked <timestamp>" + "Channel: Operator-mediated copy-link" — copy deliberately uses "Marked sent by operator" not "Email sent" / "Sent to recipient" per `docs/29` § 4 label policy (SLATE never sends transport). The "Channel" pill has a `title` tooltip ("SLATE recorded the operator's intent to deliver the link. SLATE did not send email or push to CRM.") so the label's meaning is unambiguous. Both metadata readers are intentionally defensive — a missing key OR a shape-mismatched value yields a safe default so a malformed metadata blob never crashes the panel. **Eligibility UI**: per-token buttons render only when token `displayStatus === 'active'` (the existing `isPastExpiry` correction is preserved: tokens whose `expires_at` has passed but whose status is still `active` render as `expired` and therefore do NOT see the send button); when audience-label missing the disabled-chip variant renders inline. SOW tokens never see this button because there is no SOW share-token table — the `SendToClientArtifactKind` union at `lib/client-delivery/send-to-client-types.ts` excludes SOW intentionally, so the buttons cannot be instantiated for SOW even by accident. **All Sprint C2-B non-goals preserved**: no `mailto:` link generation, no SendGrid / Postmark / Resend / Amazon SES / etc. pipeline (grep clean across the diff), no HubSpot / Salesforce / Pipedrive / Attio / Close / Affinity CRM integration, no DocuSign / PandaDoc / HelloSign / Adobe Sign / Conga / Ironclad linkage (no e-signature surface added), no public SOW route, no SOW share tokens, no schema/migration (`git diff --name-only supabase/` empty), no package dependency (`git diff --name-only package.json package-lock.json` empty), no token-format / token-hash semantics change, no public report/proposal renderer modification (`/r/[token]` + `/p/[token]` byte-identical at 155 B / 87.4 kB), no AI synthesis change, no middleware / `next.config.mjs` change, no RLS policy change, no PDF/storage. **No raw URL / token / email in audit metadata** verified by static review of every metadata builder in the new buttons + the modal scaffold + the C2-A actions: the buttons pass `shareUrlPath={null}` (SLATE never stored the raw token); the modal's `onCopy` writes `shareUrlPath` to `navigator.clipboard.writeText` only when present (always null in C2-B → button never appears anyway); the C2-A actions never include the raw token / URL / email in `metadata.*` or in the activity event metadata; the recipient email if newly provided is hashed via `hashRecipientEmail` server-side and only the SHA-256 hex hash is persisted on the token row. `npm run lint` clean; `NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 npm run build` clean. **29 app routes** (unchanged from Sprint C2-A). _Earlier on 2026-05-18 — **Sprint C2-A — Send to Client foundation landed.** Code-change sprint per `docs/29` § 17 + § 23. Action layer + activity event types + recipient hashing path validation + reusable confirm-modal scaffold. **NO UI mount.** The existing `LockedActionButton label="Send to Client"` at `proposal-workspace.tsx:417` remains LOCKED verbatim; zero imports of the new foundation symbols (`markReportLinkSentToClientAction` / `markProposalLinkSentToClientAction` / `SendToClientConfirmModal`) outside the foundation modules themselves (verified by grep). No schema, no migration, no API route, no public route, no email/CRM/e-signature, no PDF/storage, no package dependency, no middleware / `next.config.mjs` / RLS change, no AI synthesis change, no Group-B wiring, no SOW share token, no public SOW route. **New shared module set** at `lib/client-delivery/`: `send-to-client-types.ts` (`SendToClientArtifactKind` union locked to `'report' | 'proposal'` — SOW intentionally absent from the union so consumers cannot type-check a SOW mark-sent call without a canon amendment; `SendToClientChannel` union locked to `'operator_mediated_copy_link'` — single entry, expansion requires new canon; `SEND_TO_CLIENT_METADATA_KEYS` constant set — `lastSentToClientAt` / `sendCount` / `lastSentChannel`; canon-verbatim `SEND_TO_CLIENT_DISCLAIMERS` per `docs/29` § 13 for both report + proposal lanes); `send-to-client-policy.ts` (`evaluateSendToClientEligibility` pure evaluator — returns ALL applicable reasons not first-fail; 12 reason codes including `artifact_kind_not_supported` / `token_not_found` / `token_revoked` / `token_expired` / `audience_label_missing` / `audience_label_too_long` / `recipient_email_invalid_shape` / `recipient_email_too_long` / `snapshot_voided` / `snapshot_ineligible`); `recipient.ts` (`normalizeAudienceLabel` + `validateAudienceLabel` + `validateRecipientEmailForHashing` — 80/254 char caps, control-char strip via `[\x00-\x1F\x7F]` regex, whitespace-run collapse, permissive `local@domain.tld` shape check; recipient-email never persisted raw — caller hashes and discards). **New `lib/reports/send-to-client-actions.ts`** with `markReportLinkSentToClientAction({shareTokenId, audienceConfirmation, recipientEmail?, operatorConfirmed: true})` cookie-bound operator action. Pipeline: validates UUID + operator session + `operatorConfirmed === true` strict check (modal cannot be bypassed); validates audience label mandatory + recipient email optional shape-check; loads `report_share_tokens` + `report_delivery_snapshots` via existing RLS; runs `evaluateReportShareEligibility` on the snapshot AS DEFENSE-IN-DEPTH (a snapshot voided since mint must not be sendable) plus `evaluateSendToClientEligibility` on the policy-narrowed shape; on failure emits `report_share_token_send_failed` activity event with sanitized `{shareTokenId, snapshotId, reportId, failureReason}` (only the first-blocking code surfaces on the event; the full reasons array stays in the action return for the UI) — **NEVER violation text, NEVER raw values**; on happy path updates token `metadata` jsonb with `lastSentToClientAt` ISO + `sendCount` atomic increment + `lastSentChannel='operator_mediated_copy_link'` AND updates `audience_label` + `recipient_email_hash` if the operator edited them at confirmation time (via existing `hashRecipientEmail` helper — raw email never reaches the DB or the activity feed; the `metadata` jsonb column already exists from migration 0014, so **no schema change**); logs `report_share_token_sent_to_client` activity event with sanitized `{shareTokenId, snapshotId, reportId, audienceLabel, hasRecipientEmailHash: boolean, sentAt, sendCount, channel}`; revalidates `/app/engagements/{id}/report`; returns `{ok: true, status: 'sent', artifactKind: 'report', channel, tokenId, sentAt, sendCount, hasRecipientEmailHash, audienceLabel}` — **no raw token, no raw URL, no raw email in the result either; the operator's clipboard holds the URL**. **New `lib/proposals/send-to-client-actions.ts`** with `markProposalLinkSentToClientAction` — byte-similar mirror against `proposal_share_tokens` + `proposal_delivery_snapshots`; emits `proposal_share_token_sent_to_client` / `proposal_share_token_send_failed`; carries `proposalId` not `reportId`; revalidates the proposal page. **4 new activity event types** added to `lib/activity/types.ts` + `components/activity/activity-timeline.tsx`: `report_share_token_sent_to_client` (tone `brand`, label "Report link marked sent"); `report_share_token_send_failed` (tone `risk`, label "Report link send mark failed"); `proposal_share_token_sent_to_client` (tone `brand`, label "Proposal link marked sent"); `proposal_share_token_send_failed` (tone `risk`, label "Proposal link send mark failed"). **Labels deliberately use "marked sent" not "sent"** to avoid implying email-transport delivery per `docs/29` § 1 — the canon is explicit that SLATE never sends; the operator copies the link and delivers through their own channel. **New `components/client-delivery/send-to-client-confirm-modal.tsx` scaffold** — reusable client component exported for Sprint C2-B; Modal is domain-agnostic — does not know report vs. proposal action details; parent threads the action via `onConfirm` callback. _Earlier on 2026-05-18 — **Sprint C1 — Send to Client Channel Canon at `docs/29_PHASE_1B_SEND_TO_CLIENT_CHANNEL_CANON.md` landed. Decision: Option A — copy-link / operator-mediated only for the first `Send to Client` unlock.** Documentation-only sprint — no source code, no schema, no migration, no API route, no package dependency, no public route, no email pipeline, no CRM integration, no e-signature integration, no PDF/storage, no `Send to Client` unlock, no AI synthesis change, no middleware / `next.config.mjs` / RLS change, no Group-B wiring, no public SOW route (deferred per `docs/28`), no SOW share tokens. Canon answers 20+ specific operator questions with hard + soft bindings: (1) What does "Send to Client" mean in Phase 1B? **Operator-mediated handoff** of a SLATE-generated public share link to a named client recipient through the operator's existing communication channel (email client, CRM, Slack, in-person). SLATE itself does not send email, push to CRM, open `mailto:` links, invoke send providers, collect from-addresses/subject lines/bodies, or track delivered/bounced/opened at the email layer. (2) Which artifacts eligible? Report share link `/r/[token]` YES; Proposal Review Link `/p/[token]` YES; **SOW Draft DEFERRED** per `docs/28` § 10 — separate Sprint decision; engagement/findings/opportunities/roadmap NO (no public route); internal PDF candidate route NO (operator-only); internal SOW Draft route NO (operator-only). (3) Recipient/audience model: audience label MANDATORY (max 80 chars, sanitized — unlike raw mint where it's optional); recipient email OPTIONAL (max 254 chars, hashed at rest via existing `hashRecipientEmail`, never raw); linkage to a single existing share token (not "mint + send" — operator must consciously pair existing token with named audience); single recipient per event (BCC/CC equivalents are operator-side concatenation); no persistent recipient directory in SLATE. (4) Required audit events: two new types reserved — `report_share_token_sent_to_client` + `proposal_share_token_sent_to_client` (tone `brand`, labels "Report sent to client" / "Proposal sent to client") with sanitized metadata `{tokenId, snapshotId, reportId|proposalId, audienceLabel, recipientHashPresent: boolean, sentAt}`; two failure-event types reserved — `report_share_token_send_failed` + `proposal_share_token_send_failed` with `{tokenId, failureReason}`; re-sending same token to same audience allowed (each is separate audit entry); zero raw URL / raw token / raw email in metadata. (5) Delivery confirmation: operator confirms intent → SLATE records audit event + `metadata.lastSentToClientAt` ISO timestamp + `metadata.sendCount` atomic increment (no schema migration — both `report_share_tokens` + `proposal_share_tokens` already carry `metadata` jsonb column); zero transport-layer visibility (no delivered/bounced/opened/replied tracking at email layer); recipient access correlates via existing public-route access events on same `tokenId`. (6) E-signature boundary: HARD — SLATE never invites recipient to sign, accept, agree, authorise, execute, commit; no signature collection on public route; no `mailto:` with acceptance-pre-populated body; no Accept/Sign-here/I-agree UI; no DocuSign/PandaDoc/HelloSign/Adobe-Sign/Conga/Ironclad linkage; commercial-guard pattern families (71 patterns runtime) already prohibit dangerous language at generation time. (7) Contract/execution boundary: HARD — SLATE never delivers contracts, executable agreements, SOWs, binding offers, quotes-with-price-commitments; report/proposal share links carry existing canon-required disclaimer footers; SOW Drafts explicitly excluded from first Send to Client unlock; future executable-agreement workflow requires separate canon (≥ `docs/32`) that explicitly authorises hosted contract delivery. (8) Does SLATE send email? **NO.** Three reasons: compliance surface area (SPF/DKIM/DMARC alignment, bounce-handling, list-unsubscribe RFC 8058, CAN-SPAM/CASL/GDPR — operator's existing email client already inside compliance envelope, SLATE's send pipeline would not be); identity-confusion risk (recipient's relationship is with operator's firm, not SLATE — SLATE-branded mail muddies that); operator workflow simplicity (operator's existing client is one paste away — marginal time savings small, surface-area cost large). (9) CRM integration? **NO.** Two reasons: operator's CRM is system of record (per-CRM build-out has its own auth/contact-model/custom-field-shape/rate-limit posture creating per-customer integration debt); loose coupling beats tight coupling at Phase 1B maturity (operator pastes SLATE URL into CRM activity log as one-line entry; works against every CRM without per-CRM authoring). (10) Failure/revoke: thin audit layer; doesn't modify token lifecycle; existing cascade-revoke/expiry-flip/void semantics remain authoritative; `metadata.lastSentToClientAt` preserved through revoke (audit fact, not token-state fact); operator responsible for notifying recipient that link no longer active (SLATE doesn't auto-notify). (11) Token expiry/reuse: NO change to existing policy — 14d default + 30d max + dev-only minutes (Sprint H1); Send to Client doesn't change `expires_at`/`status`/`access_count`/`last_accessed_at`; re-use of same token across multiple sends allowed (each is separate audit entry; underlying 14d window unaffected). (12) Required operator confirmation: two-step confirmation modal (modelled on Sprint P4 void + Sprint H1 revoke two-step patterns); modal renders token state + audience-label-required + recipient-email-optional fields + copy-once URL + canon-verbatim disclaimer + Confirm-send-disabled-until-audience-non-empty; on confirm, re-runs eligibility, updates metadata, hashes recipient if new, updates audience_label if edited, emits sanitized event, revalidates page. (13) Required disclaimers: canon-verbatim copy specified for both report-side ("Marking this report as sent records the operator's intent in SLATE's audit log. SLATE does not deliver this link by email, CRM, or any other channel. After confirming, copy the URL above and deliver it through your own channel (email client, CRM, or in person). SLATE does not track recipient delivery beyond access events on the SLATE public route.") and proposal-side (same prefix + four-denial commercial discussion suffix); strings are canon-verbatim, operators may not abbreviate/paraphrase/restructure without amending canon; disclaimers are operator-facing (rendered inside modal) NOT on public surface. (14) PII/privacy posture: raw recipient email NEVER persisted (hashed via existing helper); audience label operator-facing only (NEVER crosses public boundary); send timestamps are operator audit data; no raw token in audit fields; **new "no raw URL in audit fields" rule** — activity event metadata MUST NOT include `/r/<token>` or `/p/<token>` URL even though operator just copied it (token hash + send counters sufficient audit signal); RLS unchanged (workspace-scoped operator-full). (15) Channel security posture: zero new public surfaces; zero new outbound network requests (no fetch to send provider, CRM, analytics); zero new credentials/secrets (no `SLATE_EMAIL_API_KEY`, no `SLATE_CRM_CLIENT_SECRET`); existing `SLATE_SHARE_TOKEN_ACCESS_PEPPER` + Supabase service-role keys remain only sensitive env vars; CSP/Referrer-Policy/X-Robots-Tag unchanged on public routes; generic-rejection rule preserved. (16) Unlock prerequisites: 8 hard prerequisites — `docs/29` accepted; audience-label-mandatory policy validated; recipient-email hashing path validated (Sprint H1 confirmed end-to-end); two-step modal pattern proven (Sprint P4/H1 precedents); activity-event taxonomy reserved (this canon); no conflicting unlock state (`Prepare Client Review` + `Prepare SOW Draft` must remain non-send actions); no SOW share dependency; lint+build clean. (17) Proposed sprint sequence: `docs/29` accepted → Sprint C2-A foundation (action layer + event types + recipient hashing path validation + modal scaffolding, no UI button rendered, mirrors Sprint P2→P3 foundation-first pattern) → Sprint C2-B operator surface (per-token Send to Client modal + per-row button + revalidation + audit-event emission; **at END of C2-B: per-token `Send to Client` UNLOCKED** for report + proposal lanes only — `proposal-workspace.tsx:417` per-option lock REMAINS LOCKED per § 18) → `docs/30` Send to Client MVP Acceptance Audit → post-acceptance: `docs/28` SOW share re-opens; per-CRM canon (`docs/31`+); email-send canon (`docs/31`+); e-signature canon (`docs/32`+) — operator chooses next direction. (18) `proposal-workspace.tsx:417` per-option `Send to Client` button: REMAINS LOCKED across Sprint C2 series; targets proposal-option-level "send whole proposal" semantic that doesn't map cleanly to share-token-mediated delivery; future canon may re-author (recommended slot `docs/32`+); serves as visible marker that operators trained on historic UI must use per-token flow. _Earlier on 2026-05-18 — **Sprint P7-A — SOW Share Route Decision Canon at `docs/28_PHASE_1B_SOW_SHARE_ROUTE_DECISION_CANON.md` landed. Decision: DEFER public SOW share route until after the Send to Client channel canon (`docs/29`) is authored.** Documentation-only sprint — no source code, no schema, no migration, no API route, no package dependency, no public route, no `sow_share_tokens` table, no e-signature, no email/CRM, no PDF/storage, no `Send to Client` unlock, no AI synthesis change, no middleware / `next.config.mjs` / RLS change, no Group-B wiring. Canon answers 10 specific operator questions with hard + soft bindings: (1) public SOW link before Send to Client? **No / defer**; (2) URL shape if shipped later? **`/s/[token]`** (soft binding — short, visually distinct from `/r/` + `/p/`, low context leak); (3) separate `sow_share_tokens` table? **Yes** (soft — separation wins on reasoning clarity + legal-separation criterion); (4) extra disclaimers? Hard verbatim copy specified — additional public-only disclaimer ("This document is for review by the named recipient. SLATE provides no execution or signature workflow on this page. Any approval, signature, or commencement of work happens through your separate contract process.") layered on top of internal-route chrome; (5) approval state required before sharing? Hard — SOW-side `approval_state='approved'` PLUS source Proposal Candidate `approval_state='approved'` (defense-in-depth) PLUS commercial guard passed PLUS within 14d default expiry PLUS explicit operator confirmation dialog beyond normal flow PLUS audience-label MANDATORY on mint (operator audit log requirement); (6) pricing on public surface? Hidden when `pricing_review_state='placeholder'` (already canon); "Estimated · subject to final approval. Not a binding quote." when manually/workflow-approved; forbidden across all states: payment terms / invoice schedule / currency conversion / discount math / automatic totals — SOW commercial guard's 26 SOW-finality patterns enforce at generation time; (7) recipient/signatory identity? **Audience label MANDATORY** (unlike report/proposal where it's optional); **recipient email OPTIONAL** (hashed-at-rest only via existing `hashRecipientEmail`); **signatory identity NEVER COLLECTED** — asking primes recipient to interpret page as signature page; (8) what happens when SOW is voided? Cascade-revoke mirrors Sprint P4 `cascadeRevokeActiveProposalShareTokensForSnapshot` pattern (revoke all active SOW share tokens, emit one `sow_share_token_revoked` per cascade with `cascade: true`, rollup count into parent `sow_draft_voided` event, generic-unavailable post-cascade) + SOW-specific additional rule: voiding source Proposal Candidate must also cascade-revoke descendant SOW share tokens; (9) does public SOW sharing create e-signature confusion? **Yes — single largest reason for deferral.** Every other SOW-shaped page recipients have seen ends with a signature block; the SLATE surface's absence-of-signature is informative to careful readers but the cognitive frame "review surface" is fragile in a way the report (advisory) and proposal-review (commercial discussion) surfaces are not. Four mitigations available (public-only disclaimer / draft-watermark stripe / no action UI / `noindex,nofollow`) but **all four are insufficient before `docs/29` defines SLATE's relationship to the execution path**; (10) defer until channel canon? **Yes.** Doc-number reservation chain: `docs/27` (SOW Draft Acceptance, LANDED) → `docs/28` (this — SOW share decision: DEFER) → `docs/29` (Send to Client channel canon, RECOMMENDED NEXT SPRINT) → operator decision point (channel canon authorises public SOW share → Sprint P7-B implement `/s/[token]`; OR channel canon authorises operator-only external-platform share → no `/s` route; OR channel canon defers Send to Client further → SOW share also stays deferred) → `docs/30` (SOW Share Link MVP Acceptance Audit only if Sprint P7-B is later approved + implemented + audited) → `docs/31` (reserved). Sprint P7-A also explicitly enumerates the binding hard prerequisite that SOW-side `approveSowDraftSnapshotAction` (Sprint P6-C carry-forward item per `docs/26` § Open Decisions item 8) must ship before any Sprint P7-B can satisfy criterion 3; without SOW-side approval the SOW share eligibility evaluator can never return eligible. Operators may not abbreviate or restructure the canon-verbatim disclaimer strings in § 4 of `docs/28` without amending the canon. Send to Client remains LOCKED across every option in the decision matrix until `docs/29` explicitly unlocks it. **No source code changes. `git status --short`: 3 docs files modified — this `docs/08` + `docs/10` + new `docs/28`.** Production Hardening Sprint H1 remains landed per the entry below. SOW Draft MVP remains Accepted with notes per `docs/27`. Proposal Review Link MVP remains Accepted with notes per `docs/25`. Client Report Link MVP remains Accepted with notes per `docs/23`. **Recommended next sprint: `docs/29` — Send to Client channel canon.** The channel canon decides whether SLATE itself ever delivers client-facing artifacts (email / CRM / push) or whether the operator hand-delivers via their own channel; whether the Send to Client unlock is binary or per-recipient; how SLATE positions itself relative to e-signature platforms (no involvement / link-out / hosted-but-not-signed); whether the Send to Client unlock requires extra confirmation friction; how / whether the operator audits delivery completion. After `docs/29` is accepted, the SOW share decision becomes re-eligible and Sprint P7-B becomes gated on that re-eligibility._

_Earlier on 2026-05-18 — **Production Hardening Sprint H1 (report + proposal share-link hardening) landed.** Code-change sprint — no schema, no migration, no public route, no new package dependency, no `Send to Client` unlock, no email/CRM/e-sign/PDF binary/storage/Group-B wiring, no SOW share token, no SOW public route. **New shared module** `lib/share-tokens/access-signature.ts` (`server-only`; centralizes `getShareTokenAccessPepperStatus()` + `shouldOmitAccessHashes()` + `hashAccessFingerprint({ip, userAgent})`; returns peppered 128-bit SHA-256 hex when `SLATE_SHARE_TOKEN_ACCESS_PEPPER` is set OR `{ipHash: null, uaHash: null, combinedSig: null, hashesOmitted: true}` when unset — safe-degrade; explicit production-recommendation comment that the pepper must be configured before relying on IP/UA fingerprinting). **Both `recordShareTokenAccess` (report) + `recordProposalShareTokenAccess` (proposal) refactored** to import the shared helper, eliminating the two duplicate `sanitizeAccessMetadata` + `peppered` helpers. **5-minute access-log debounce** added to both surfaces: when the combined `(ip, ua)` peppered signature is available AND matches `metadata.lastAccessSig` within the 5-minute `metadata.lastAccessSigAt` window, SLATE skips the `access_count` bump + skips the activity event so refresh-storms do not flood the timeline. When no signature is available (pepper unset OR missing IP/UA), debounce is bypassed and every access is logged (safe-degrade). `access_count` is the existing soft counter and may under-count during a debounce window — canon-acceptable trade. **Dev-only short-expiry affordance** added to both `generateShareLinkAction` + `generateProposalShareLinkAction` via new `resolveShareTokenExpiry` / `resolveProposalShareTokenExpiry` helpers: when `expiryMinutes` is passed AND `NODE_ENV !== "production"` OR `SLATE_SHARE_TOKEN_ALLOW_DEV_EXPIRY === "true"`, expiry resolves to `now + minutes * 60s`; production callers passing `expiryMinutes` get the parameter silently ignored — the 14d default applies. Lets operators live-test the expiry-flip branch in dev without raw SQL while preserving production policy verbatim. **Audience-label + recipient-email UI threading** added to both `generate-share-link-button.tsx` + `generate-proposal-share-link-button.tsx` via a new `<details>`-collapsed `<OperatorTrackingFields>` block: optional inputs for audience label (80 chars) + recipient email (254 chars); operator-facing copy reads "Optional. Used for operator tracking only. SLATE does not email this recipient. The email is hashed at rest; SLATE never stores the raw address." Raw email never reaches the DB or activity feed — server action hashes via existing `hashRecipientEmail` (SHA-256 hex on lowercase-trimmed input). **Revoke-from-panel UI** for proposal share tokens: new `components/proposals/revoke-proposal-share-link-button.tsx` (thin client mirror of `components/reports/revoke-share-link-button.tsx`; two-step confirm with optional reason; calls existing `revokeProposalShareTokenAction`). New `ShareTokenSummary` + `ShareTokenRow` sub-components added to `components/proposals/proposal-candidates-panel.tsx` (fetches `getProposalShareTokensForProposal(proposalId)` alongside the snapshot query via `Promise.all`; groups tokens by `snapshotId`; per-row renders status badge + created/expires timestamps + access count + last access timestamp + audience label + "Recipient hashed at rest" indicator when `recipientEmailHash` present; active tokens get the Revoke button beside them). Identical "Recipient hashed at rest" indicator added to the existing report-side `ShareTokenSummary` for symmetry. **Stale copy cleanup**: `generate-share-link-button.tsx` "Public route lands in Sprint 4D-C" → "Public route is live" (tone `warning` → `success`); `generate-proposal-share-link-button.tsx` "Public proposal route lands in Sprint P5" → "Public proposal route is live" (tone `warning` → `success`). **Engagement-title fallback fixed** in both `app/r/[token]/page.tsx` + `app/p/[token]/page.tsx` `resolveClientReportTitle` / `resolveClientProposalTitle`: previous code selected `engagements.company_name` (a column that does not exist on the table — canon: company name lives on `accounts.name`, joined via `engagements.account_id`), so every public route was falling through to the generic "Client Report" / "Proposal Review" titles. Fix: query `engagements.name`, `engagements.engagement_type`, `engagements.account_id`, then secondary query `accounts.name` for the company name. Falls back gracefully (account miss → `engagement.name` → generic) so no engagement loses its title silently. Both queries run under the service-role client; no UUIDs reach the rendered DOM. **All boundaries preserved**: no `app/s/` or `app/sow/` directory, no `sow_share_tokens` table, no service-role SOW lookup helpers, no e-signature controls, no email pipeline, no CRM integration, no PDF binary generation, no new storage bucket, no new package dependency (verified via `git diff --name-only package.json package-lock.json` → empty), no migration changes (verified via `git diff --name-only supabase/` → empty), no AI synthesis change, no middleware / `next.config.mjs` / API route change, Group-B grep clean (zero imports of `BenchmarkComparisonBars` / `AISavingsWaterfall` / `RoiBridge` outside `components/charts/` + `/app/charts-preview`). `Send to Client` LOCKED verbatim (`proposal-workspace.tsx:417`). `Prepare Report` LOCKED. `Export Report` LOCKED for mock paths. `Prepare SOW Draft` remains the internal-anchor-only flow shipped in Sprint P6-C. `Prepare Client Review` remains the operator-mediated mint flow shipped in Sprint P5. `npm run lint` clean; `NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 npm run build` clean. **29 app routes** (unchanged from Sprint P6-C); `/app/engagements/[id]/proposal` grew `10.5 kB / 114 kB` → `11.4 kB / 115 kB` (+0.9 kB / +1 kB for new audience-label UI + share-token summary + revoke button); `/app/engagements/[id]/report` grew `11.7 kB / 115 kB` → `12.1 kB / 115 kB` (+0.4 kB / +0 kB for audience-label UI + recipient-hashed indicator); `/r/[token]` byte-identical at 155 B / 87.4 kB (title fix is server-side); `/p/[token]` byte-identical at 155 B / 87.4 kB; `/app/engagements/[id]/proposal/sow/[snapshotId]` byte-identical at 187 B / 96.2 kB; `/scorecard/results` byte-identical at 10.7 kB / 119 kB. **Live validation deferred** to operator session — read-only SQL against shared production Supabase remains classifier-blocked. - **AI findings synthesis RAN END-TO-END** ✅ — clicked `Generate draft findings`, OpenAI `gpt-4o-mini` returned 5 findings, all persisted to `public.findings` as `review_status='needs_review'`, `ai_drafted=true`, `assumption_flag=false`. Source refs correctly attributed: 5 `finding_source_refs` rows, all `source_type='stakeholder_response'`, `strength='strong'`, role-tagged stakeholder labels (`Avery Kim · CEO` / `Riley Patel · VP Operations` / `Sam Watanabe · Head of Sales`), 121–297 char excerpts. Role coverage: executive (1), operations (2), sales (2) — all 3 covered REQUIRED roles represented.

Vercel digest `463418387`. Reproduced on multiple reloads. Console message: "An error occurred in the Server Components render. The specific message is omitted in production builds to avoid leaking sensitive details." **Engagement landing page reports `FINDINGS · 0/5 Approved`** confirming findings exist but cannot be reviewed.
- **Bug isolation:** Opportunities page on the same engagement (`/app/engagements/[id]/opportunities`) **renders cleanly** with the full S6 UI surface (Identified / Quick Wins / Strategic Builds / Defer · Avoid / Avg Impact / Strong Evidence tiles; `Generate draft opportunities` form with `No approved findings yet` warning; Open findings workspace CTA). The opportunities page reads the same 5 findings via `getFindingProvenanceForEngagement` + `getMinimalFindingsForEngagement` — both queries succeed against the new findings data. **This proves the bug is specific to the findings-page render path**, not the shared helpers, not the underlying data shape, not the new `reviewer_notes` SELECT projection.

S5 two-step rejection-reason flow BLOCKED. S6 opportunity drafting BLOCKED (requires `approved` findings; the `No approved findings yet` warning correctly gates the button). S6 RoadmapReadinessHint live transition BLOCKED. **The bug surfaces real defect coverage that source-tree validation didn't catch — exactly the value of a controlled walkthrough.**

All workspace-scoped via authenticated operator session.

Zero new findings auto-promoted past `needs_review`. Override path NOT used. SQL seeding NOT used. Service-role writes NOT used. OpenAI call cost ≈ $0.01–0.05 estimated.

## Sprint S6 — Opportunities AI Drafting + Quadrant Approval (2026-06-04)

Implementation sprint per `docs/39` § 5. Extends the existing AI opportunity drafting pipeline (pre-S6 scaffold) with S5 provenance preservation, rejection-reason capture, and an operator-only S7 roadmap-readiness signal. Source-clean + lint-clean + build-clean + boundary-clean. Full evidence: `docs/45_OPPORTUNITIES_AI_DRAFTING.md`. Headline:

- **Substantial pre-S6 scaffold inherited:** persisted `opportunities` table + `opportunity_finding_links` join + `ai_synthesis_runs` ledger + AI drafting context (`lib/ai/opportunities-context.ts`) + AI prompt (`lib/ai/opportunities-synthesis.ts`) + server action (`lib/opportunities/synthesis-actions.ts`) + manual create + workspace UI + matrix + action bar (select/defer/reject/reopen). S6 closed six gaps without re-architecting the pipeline.
- **Tiny additive migration** `supabase/migrations/0019_opportunity_reviewer_notes.sql` — `alter table public.opportunities add column if not exists reviewer_notes text`. Nullable. No NOT NULL, no CHECK, no RLS change, no default. Matches the S5 findings.reviewer_note pattern; required because pre-S6 schema had no column for rejection-reason capture.
- **New pure-function helper** `lib/opportunities/provenance.ts` (~280 lines) — `summarizeOpportunityProvenance(relatedFindingIds, findingProvenanceById)` projects each opportunity's source findings through the S5 `summarizeFindingProvenance` map and returns per-lane counts (strong/adequate/thin source findings + needs-validation count) + dominant-strength + needs-validation flag with operator-facing reason. `buildRoadmapReadinessSignal(opportunities, options?)` returns selected/deferred/rejected/draft counts + selected-with-needs-validation + selected-with-thin-evidence + selected-in-defer-avoid subsets + `readyForS7` boolean (default threshold 3) + advisory warnings list. Both are pure — no DB, no I/O.
- **Provenance preservation from S5:** `lib/ai/opportunities-context.ts` extended to project per-finding `needsValidation` + `needsValidationReason` + `assumptionFlag` into the AI prompt via the S5 helper running against the full `finding_source_refs` set (no prompt-cap bias). Defensive `mapProvenanceType` + `mapProvenanceStrength` helpers degrade unknown DB enum values to `consultant-note` / `thin` rather than crashing.
- **Provenance-aware AI prompt rule:** `lib/ai/opportunities-synthesis.ts` system prompt extended with explicit instruction — when source findings carry needs-validation, force `evidenceStrength='thin'`, raise `riskScore`, and use conservative implementation-shape wording ("investigate", "validate", "scope") instead of imperative commitment ("build", "ship", "automate"). Only assert `evidenceStrength='strong'` when ALL linked source findings have `needsValidation=false`. Validation layer unchanged — strict JSON schema + 0–100 score clamping + UUID allowlist + duplicate-title detection + bound 2–6 opportunities.
- **Two-step rejection-reason capture:** `components/opportunities/opportunity-action-bar.tsx` replaces one-click Reject with a Reject… → optional 10–500 char reason textarea → Confirm reject flow that calls `rejectOpportunity(id, { reason })`. Server-side validation re-enforces 10–500 chars after trim (or empty); returns new `rejection-reason-invalid` error code on overrun. Persisted in `opportunities.reviewer_notes` AND in sanitized activity event metadata. Displayed inline on already-rejected opportunities via a dedicated "Rejection rationale" panel.
- **Needs-validation warning panel** above the action buttons for non-terminal opportunities — withdraws once `selected` or `rejected` so it stops nagging settled records. Does NOT block selection (operator authority preserved).
- **Provenance visibility on workspace:** `OpportunityProvenanceChip` renders compact mode on card list (single "Needs validation" warning chip when applicable; else nothing) and full mode on detail view (per-lane counts Strong/Adequate/Thin + needs-validation badge + operator-readable reason). Driven by `provenanceById` map threaded server-side from the page.
- **S7 roadmap readiness signal:** new `RoadmapReadinessHint` server component (`~130 lines`, zero client bundle cost) mounted below `CreateOpportunityForm` on the opportunities page. Renders 4 primary stat tiles (Selected/Deferred/Rejected/Still in review) + 3 secondary tiles when `selected > 0` (needs-validation / thin evidence / defer-avoid subsets) + Ready/Not-yet status chip + first-4 advisories from the helper + boundary footer ("Sprint S7 roadmap drafting consumes selected opportunities only — rejected and deferred never enter the roadmap"). **Boundary critical:** rejected and deferred opportunities NEVER count toward `selected` — verified by pure-logic smoke `BOUNDARY` case.
- **Sanitized activity-metadata extensions:** `opportunity_selected/deferred/rejected` events now carry `priorEvidenceStrength`, `priorQuadrant`, `sourceFindingCount` (count only, no IDs), plus optional `rejectionReason` (only when operator supplied one). `ai_opportunities_generated` event extends with `sourceFindings: {total, needsValidation, assumptionFlagged}` source-evidence counts. **Zero** raw finding text, source label, excerpt, stakeholder name/email/role, raw answer text, transcript text, tokens, source-finding UUIDs, opportunity_finding_links UUIDs, attio_company_id, or provider response body.
- **Page wiring:** `app/app/engagements/[id]/opportunities/page.tsx` adds `getFindingProvenanceForEngagement` to its `Promise.all`; builds the opportunity provenance map via in-process projection; computes the roadmap-readiness signal; mounts `RoadmapReadinessHint`; threads `provenanceById` to `OpportunitiesWorkspace`; threads per-opportunity `provenance` + `reviewerNote` to `OpportunityActionBar`. No new DB fetch beyond the dedicated provenance query.
- **Controlled validation:** Both candidate engagements (SLATE Pilot Test Client `ed7f1f7d-…` and Sapient Digital `76097653-…`) hold **0 persisted findings AND 0 persisted opportunities** — same boundary-correct posture as S5. **Live drafting exercise deferred** until first real approved finding lands on any engagement. Polish is build-validated against the existing `Opportunity` type contract and pure-logic-smoke-validated via `artifacts/s6-provenance-smoke.mjs` — 11/11 cases pass (5 opportunity provenance: no-linked / all-strong / all-needs-validation / mixed / unknown-id; 6 roadmap readiness: empty / 2-selected-below-threshold / 3-selected-at-threshold / mix-with-rejected-deferred / **BOUNDARY 5-rejected-deferred-not-selected** / 3-selected-all-needs-validation-still-ready).
- **Migration 0019 NOT yet applied to deployed Supabase** — operator step before next Vercel Production promotion past `384ab1e` (same posture as S3-B migration 0018). Current deployed Production runs the S4 build and is unaffected by the new SELECT projection.
- **Boundary confirmation:** zero `/r` or `/p` mint, zero Send to Client, zero public SOW route, zero SOW share tokens, zero email send, zero CRM writeback, zero Attio writes, zero e-signature, zero roadmap generation (S7 scope), zero report-section generation (S8), zero proposal generation (S9), zero Group-B claims, zero Sapient mutation, zero new package dependencies, zero new public routes, zero `service_role` writes, zero `mailto:`/`sendgrid`/`nodemailer`/`docusign`/`hellosign`/`adobesign`/`crm_push` introduced, zero `roadmap_items` write operations introduced. Roadmap sequence unchanged.
- **Lint clean ✅; production build clean ✅** — `/app/engagements/[id]/opportunities` route grew **11.6 kB → 12.6 kB First Load JS** (+1 kB; provenance chip + two-step reject; `RoadmapReadinessHint` server-rendered → zero client bytes). All other 28 routes byte-identical. `npm run check:send-to-client-disclaimers` ✅.
- **Files modified:** new — `supabase/migrations/0019_opportunity_reviewer_notes.sql`, `lib/opportunities/provenance.ts` (~280), `components/opportunities/opportunity-provenance-chip.tsx` (~90), `components/opportunities/roadmap-readiness-hint.tsx` (~130), `docs/45_OPPORTUNITIES_AI_DRAFTING.md`; source-modified — `lib/opportunities/types.ts` (~+8), `lib/opportunities/mappers.ts` (~+8), `lib/opportunities/queries.ts` (~+110 new helper), `lib/opportunities/actions.ts` (~+90), `lib/opportunities/synthesis-actions.ts` (~+25), `lib/ai/opportunities-context.ts` (~+90), `lib/ai/opportunities-synthesis.ts` (~+10), `components/opportunities/opportunity-action-bar.tsx` (~+130), `components/opportunities/opportunities-workspace.tsx` (~+30), `components/opportunities/create-opportunity-form.tsx` (~+5), `app/app/engagements/[id]/opportunities/page.tsx` (~+50); docs — `docs/39` (S6 row landing notes), `docs/44` (S6 cross-reference), `docs/08` (this block), `docs/10`. Throwaway `artifacts/s6-provenance-smoke.mjs` lives in gitignored `artifacts/` (not committed).

Recommended next sprint: **Sprint S7 — Roadmap AI Drafting + Sequencing** per `docs/39` § 5. Roadmap sequence unchanged. Operator-side prerequisites NOT blocking S7 source work but recommended before a live drafting exercise: (1) apply migration `0019_opportunity_reviewer_notes.sql` to deployed Supabase (`hhglrcvsmwaheikdvijw`); (2) real findings must exist + be approved on the target engagement; (3) operator approves (marks selected) at least 3 opportunities so the `RoadmapReadinessHint.readyForS7` flag flips green — or S7 chooses to draft with weaker coverage.

## Sprint S5 — Findings Approval Polish (2026-06-04)

Implementation sprint per `docs/39` § 5. Polishes the operator findings approval/rejection lifecycle against S4's evidence-aware draft findings model. Source-clean + build-clean + boundary-clean. Full evidence: `docs/44_FINDINGS_APPROVAL_POLISH.md`. Headline:

- **New pure-function helper** `lib/findings/provenance.ts` (~250 lines) — `summarizeFindingProvenance(refs, assumptionFlag)` returns per-lane ref counts (stakeholder-response / uploaded-document / scorecard-answer / consultant-note) + dominant strength (`strong | adequate | thin | missing`) + needs-validation flag with reason code (`no-refs | all-thin-or-missing | only-engagement-context | assumption-flagged | null`). `buildOpportunitiesReadinessSignal(findings, options?)` returns approved/rejected/draft/approvedNeedsValidation counts + `readyForS6` boolean (default threshold 5) + advisory warnings list.
- **Provenance visibility on findings workspace** (`components/findings/findings-workspace.tsx`): new `FindingProvenanceChip` renders in two modes — compact on card list (single "Needs validation" warning chip when applicable, else nothing), full on detail view (lane counts + dominant-strength chip + needs-validation badge with reason). Driven entirely by the new helper; no extra DB fetch.
- **Approval/rejection lifecycle polish** (`components/findings/review-action-bar.tsx`): added a needs-validation warning panel above the action-button row for non-terminal findings (withdraws once approved / rejected / report-ready so it stops nagging settled records); replaced the one-click Reject button with a two-step flow (Reject… → reason textarea 10–500 chars or empty → Confirm reject) that calls `rejectFinding(id, { reason })`.
- **Server actions extended** (`lib/findings/actions.ts`, +~120 lines): `setReviewStatus` now accepts `{rejectionReason?}` options; validates 10–500 chars or empty (returns `rejection-reason-invalid` on overrun); persists reason in `findings.reviewer_note`; computes provenance summary from joined `finding_source_refs` rows; emits sanitized `provenance: {totalRefs, stakeholderResponseRefs, uploadedDocumentRefs, scorecardAnswerRefs, consultantNoteRefs, dominantStrength, needsValidation}` + optional `rejectionReason` (when status=rejected and operator provided one) in activity event metadata. `rejectFinding` signature widened to `(id, options?)`. Defensive `mapDbSourceType` + `mapDbStrength` helpers avoid crashes on unknown DB enum values.
- **Activity metadata safety:** zero raw `excerpt`, zero raw `source_label`, zero raw stakeholder name/email/role, zero raw answer text, zero token values, zero UUIDs other than the finding-id, zero `attio_company_id`, zero raw reviewer-note text. Only operator-typed string in metadata is the optional bounded `rejectionReason`.
- **New Opportunities Readiness Hint** (`components/findings/opportunities-readiness-hint.tsx`): operator-only server component (zero client bundle cost) mounted on the findings page below `CreateFindingForm`. Renders 4 stat tiles (Approved / Rejected / Still in review / Approved · needs-validation), a Ready / Not-yet status chip, and the helper's advisory warnings. Read-only; does NOT block S6 (S6 not yet built); does NOT mint anything; does NOT contact a client. S6 will decide its own gating posture; today the hint surface only tells the operator how close they are to a drafting-ready state.
- **Controlled validation:** Both candidate engagements (SLATE Pilot Test Client `ed7f1f7d-…` and Sapient Digital `76097653-…`) hold 0 persisted findings — the audit-fixture was correctly blocked by S4's boundary; Sapient has not progressed past intake setup. **Live lifecycle exercise deferred** until the first real finding lands on any engagement (operator-authored via `CreateFindingForm` OR operator-override AI synthesis run on non-audit data). Polish is build-validated against the existing `Finding` type contract and pure-logic-smoke-validated against the new helpers via `artifacts/s5-provenance-smoke.mjs` (9/9 cases pass: 5 provenance cases + 4 readiness cases).
- **Boundary confirmation:** zero `/r` or `/p` mint, zero Send to Client, zero public SOW route, zero SOW share tokens, zero email send, zero CRM writeback, zero Attio writes, zero e-signature, zero opportunity scoring/drafting (S6 scope), zero roadmap generation (S7), zero report-section generation (S8), zero proposal generation (S9), zero Group-B claims, zero Sapient mutation, zero new package dependencies, zero new public routes, zero new migrations, zero `service_role` writes, zero `mailto:`/`sendgrid`/`nodemailer`/`docusign`/`hellosign`/`adobesign`/`crm_push` introduced. Roadmap sequence unchanged.
- **Lint clean ✅; production build clean ✅** — `/app/engagements/[id]/findings` route grew **12 kB → 13.3 kB First Load JS** (+1.3 kB; provenance chip + rejection-reason textarea). All other 28 routes byte-identical. `npm run check:send-to-client-disclaimers` ✅.
- **Files modified:** new — `lib/findings/provenance.ts` (~250), `components/findings/opportunities-readiness-hint.tsx` (~130), `docs/44_FINDINGS_APPROVAL_POLISH.md`; source-modified — `lib/findings/actions.ts` (+~120), `components/findings/findings-workspace.tsx` (+~80), `components/findings/review-action-bar.tsx` (+~120), `components/findings/create-finding-form.tsx` (~+5 translateError union widened), `app/app/engagements/[id]/findings/page.tsx` (~+30); docs — `docs/39` (S5 row landing notes), `docs/43` (S5 cross-reference), `docs/08` (this block), `docs/10`. ## Sprint S4 — AI Findings Synthesis Integration (2026-06-02)

Implementation sprint per `docs/39` § 5. Wires lane-attributed intake evidence + readiness gate + operator-override path into the existing findings synthesis pipeline. Source-clean + build-clean. Full evidence: `docs/43_AI_FINDINGS_SYNTHESIS_INTEGRATION.md`. Headline:

- **New EvidenceBundle aggregator** (`lib/findings/evidence.ts`, ~450 lines) — pure server-only `buildEvidenceBundleForEngagement(engagementId)`. Filters `stakeholder_responses` to `response_status='ready_for_synthesis'`; buckets by canonical lane per `docs/39` § 4 (primary live-link / secondary transcript + meeting_notes / tertiary offline_operator); excludes audit-labelled rows (prefix-match `I3 WALKTHROUGH TEST`, `S1 AUDIT`, `S2 AUDIT FIXTURE`) so fixture / walkthrough data cannot accidentally enter synthesis; emits per-lane buckets + role coverage + question coverage + CRM context summary + warnings; returns the S1 readiness verdict.
- **Existing synthesis pipeline extended:** `lib/ai/findings-context.ts` adds `evidenceBundle` field + invokes the aggregator alongside legacy intake fetch. `lib/ai/findings-synthesis.ts` system prompt extended with explicit lane hierarchy (PRIMARY = live-link first-hand; SECONDARY = transcript with speaker attribution; SECONDARY ENRICHMENT = CRM engagement-context only never per-stakeholder; TERTIARY = offline_operator requires corroboration). Strength rule tightened: `'strong'` now requires multi-lane corroboration; tertiary-only evidence forces `assumptionFlag=true` with explanatory note.
- **Readiness gate ENFORCED in the synthesis action:** `generateDraftFindingsForEngagement` now blocks synthesis when `bundle.readiness.ready === false`, returning `{ok: false, error: 'intake-readiness-not-met', readinessReasons: [...]}`. Operator can bypass with `overrideReason` (10–500 chars; sanitized server-side; persisted in activity event metadata + `ai_synthesis_runs.input_summary.overrideApplied=true`). Per-finding `assumptionFlag` rule unchanged — override does NOT silence the per-evidence quality flag.
- **UI evidence panel** in `GenerateFindingsForm`: 4 stat tiles (live-link / transcript / offline_operator / required-roles-covered ratio), CRM chip showing brand or `not linked`, status chip showing `Ready` or `Below readiness threshold`, warnings list, gate-blocker reasons when applicable, and operator-override textarea (only surfaced when gate blocks). Generate button label changes to **"Generate with operator override"** when override is in flight. Success banner surfaces `overrideApplied` state when present.
- **Activity event metadata sanitized + extended:** `ai_findings_generated` event now includes `evidenceLanes: {liveLink, transcript, offlineOperator, crmLinked}` counts + `overrideApplied: boolean` + (when override fires) `overrideReason: <operator-typed-string>`. Zero raw `answer_text`, zero `stakeholder_email`, zero `entered_by`/`created_by` UUIDs, zero `attio_company_id` raw value.
- **Controlled fixture validation (boundary verified):** All 8 ready responses on SLATE Pilot Test Client are audit-labelled (7 from S1, 1 from S2). The aggregator correctly excludes all 8 → zero reach synthesis → gate correctly blocks. This is the intended boundary — synthesis cannot accidentally consume fixture data. ## Sprint S3-A — Attio CRM System-of-Record Canon (2026-06-02)

Canon / planning sprint splitting the original Sprint S3 into **S3-A (this canon — landed)** and **S3-B (Attio Read Context Implementation — recommended next)**. Locks Attio as Saipien's first CRM system of record for engagement context. Full evidence: `docs/42_ATTIO_CRM_SYSTEM_OF_RECORD_CANON.md` (18 numbered sections, ~430 lines). Headline:

- **Attio selected** as the read-only CRM context provider for SLATE engagements. HubSpot comparison (`docs/42` § 3) included for completeness but the decision is not re-litigated.
- **SLATE remains system of record** for engagement execution. The CRM is **engagement-level read-only context** — never per-stakeholder findings evidence, never sender of stakeholder invites, never owner of the deliverable lifecycle. If Attio is offline, every SLATE surface continues to work; only the EngagementContextCard's "Attio context" section degrades.
- **One Attio workspace for all Saipien brands.** Brand / Business Unit becomes an Attio property + saved views, not separate workspaces. Canonical initial value list: `Saipien Labs Consulting`, `Venture Studio`, `Sapient Digital`, `Ventrys`, `Pravado`, `Aivery`, `Other`. Operator confirms final list per `docs/42` § 16.
- **Object mapping locked:** SLATE Account ↔ Attio Company (linked via new `accounts.attio_company_id text null`); SLATE Stakeholder ↔ Attio Person (optional `attio_person_id`, deferred); SLATE Engagement ↔ Attio Deal (optional `attio_deal_id`, deferred). Deterministic lookup via stored ID; one-time domain-rematch only for initial linking; broken-link UI affordance for re-link when Attio IDs change upstream.
- **Provider-neutral adapter boundary:** internal `CrmContext` shape lives at `lib/crm/types.ts`; UI components import this only. Attio-specific mapping confined to `lib/crm/attio/`. No multi-CRM support, no connector marketplace, no productized abstraction — the neutral shape exists for exit-optionality, not for parallel-provider implementation. Future CRM migration (HubSpot, GHL, etc.) is feasible by swapping the mapper module + a one-time DB column rename.
- **Read-only S3 boundary explicit:** writeback (Sprint S18 at earliest), lead push (Free Audit / scorecard leads stay in SLATE), two-way sync (deferred indefinitely per `docs/42` § 15), customer CRM integrations, CRM-triggered automation, email send via Attio sequences, and per-brand pipelines beyond field/tag are all out of scope.
- **All five operator decisions resolved** (`docs/42` § 16 updated to "Operator decisions — resolved" by the architect 2026-06-02): (1) Attio workspace name = **`Saipien Labs HQ`**; (2) Brand property values = **Saipien Labs Consulting, Venture Studio, Sapient Digital, Ventrys, Pravado, Aivery, Future Brand** (replaces "Other" placeholder); (3) Attio custom fields created **manually by operator before S3-B kickoff** (~30 min operator-side); S3-B treats absent properties as `null` without erroring; (4) Auth posture = **server-only `ATTIO_API_KEY` env var** — set in Vercel Production + local `.env.local`, never bundled, never logged, never committed; OAuth deferred; (5) Migration `0018_accounts_attio_company_id.sql` **lands inside S3-B** (no separate micro-sprint).
- **Roadmap sequence unchanged past S3-B.** `docs/39` § 5 updated only to split the original S3 row into S3-A (landed) + S3-B (next). Sprint S4 (AI Findings Synthesis Integration) still follows S3-B.
- **Boundary confirmation:** zero source code changes, zero data mutations, zero migration runs, zero Attio connection, zero API key created, zero engagement mutations, zero `/r` or `/p` mint, zero Send to Client, zero send, zero schema or package changes, zero Sapient Digital interaction. Only docs touched.
- **Files modified:** `docs/42` (new — full canon), `docs/39` (Sprint S3 row split into S3-A landed + S3-B next; no other sequencing changes), `docs/08` (this block), `docs/10`.

Recommended next sprint: **Sprint S3-B — Attio Read Context Implementation** per `docs/42` § 18. Pre-conditions cleared: all five operator decisions resolved in `docs/42` § 16 above. S3-B scope is `docs/42` § 12 verbatim, scope-locked, no drift expected. Operator-side prerequisite: create the 11 Attio properties listed in `docs/42` § 10 in the Saipien Labs HQ workspace (~30 min) before Claude begins S3-B.

## Sprint S1 — Online Intake Flow Audit + Stakeholder Intake Readiness (2026-06-02)

First sprint in the locked Consulting Module Completion Roadmap (`docs/39`). Audits the canonical primary input lane end-to-end against a controlled fixture, finalizes readiness-gate wording, and lands a non-enforcing pure-function helper stub for Sprint S11 to wire. Full evidence: `docs/40_ONLINE_INTAKE_FLOW_AUDIT.md`. Headline:

- **Fixture:** SLATE Pilot Test Client (engagement `ed7f1f7d-…`) — Phase 1B controlled fixture with zero prior intake sessions, zero active share tokens. **No Sapient Digital mutation.** No new fixture created.
- **Operator-side mint verified:** `createStakeholderSession` minted stakeholder `1f79bc5e-…` with canon-correct shape (`source_type='live_link'`, `client_visible=true`, `token_hash` present with 21-day TTL, `sent_at` = mint timestamp, status `invited`). Activity event `intake_session_created` emitted with sanitized metadata (`{role: "operations"}` only). UI surfaced the URL once with explicit "Email automation lands later" disclaimer. Zero email sent.
- **Public-side submit verified:** Token-gated `/intake/[token]` route serves HTTP/2 200 with canon `cache-control: private, no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate`; renders role-specific welcome + 7 INTAKE_QUESTIONS; invalid token returns 200 + Invalid card (no leak via HTTP status). After submit: session `status='completed'`, `response_quality='strong'` (7 filled, 1522 chars), 7 response rows all `source_type='live_link'` + `response_status='ready_for_synthesis'` (default fired per canon — stakeholder typed it). Activity event `intake_response_submitted` emitted with sanitized metadata `{responsesCount: 7, responseQuality: "strong"}` — zero PII, no raw token, no answer text.
- **Three findings classified per `docs/39` § 11:** (F-1, Improvement → Backlog) `/intake/[token]` lacks `X-Robots-Tag` + `Referrer-Policy` HTTP headers — uses `<meta name="robots">` only, unlike `/r` + `/p` which set HTTP headers. Low-impact; future polish, recommended owner Sprint S17. (F-2, NOT a SLATE finding) Chrome MCP `computer.type` doesn't propagate text into React-controlled `<textarea>` — real-keyboard stakeholders unaffected; documented workaround. (F-3, Improvement → Backlog) Current `INTAKE_QUESTIONS` is a 7-question subset of the `docs/36` § 6 35-question packet; leaves business-model, target-customers, lead-source, document-inventory dimensions uncovered. Reconsider in Sprint S5 when synthesis quality is observable. **No findings pulled into S1 scope; no roadmap reordering.**
- **Readiness gate wording finalized** (`docs/40` § 6) and aligned with `docs/39` § 4 hierarchy: minimum 3 of 6 required roles with ≥1 `ready_for_synthesis` response across any lane; minimum 14 cumulative ready responses; ≥1 supporting document OR operator no-documents acknowledgment. Transcripts + offline + CRM lanes supplement live-link signal cumulatively at the gate (per-lane weighting at synthesis happens in S4). Operator override path reserved for S11. Helper module `lib/engagement-readiness/intake-readiness.ts` ships a pure-function `evaluateIntakeReadiness` that S11 will wire into the code-side `/r` + `/p` mint guard — non-enforcing, no DB reads, no side effects, no UI mounts, zero call sites today.
- **docs/35 § 5 updated** with the alignment note pointing operators at `docs/40` § 6 for the canonical wording; thresholds unchanged; "completed" now explicitly means "at least one `ready_for_synthesis` response across any lane".
- **All 17 boundary checks pass.** Zero new report/proposal share tokens, zero delivery snapshots, zero Send-to-Client, zero SOW events, zero email/CRM/e-sign, zero Sapient Digital mutation, zero Smoke Test Co mutation, zero service-role SQL writes outside approved migration path (only read-only `execute_sql` SELECTs this sprint), zero source feature creep, roadmap sequence unchanged.
- **Files modified:** `docs/40` (new audit log), `docs/35` (§ 5 alignment note), `docs/08` (this block), `docs/10`, `lib/engagement-readiness/intake-readiness.ts` (new pure-function helper stub).

Recommended next sprint: **Sprint S2 — Transcript / Notetaker Intake** per `docs/39` § 5. - **Canonical data hierarchy declared** (§ 4 of `docs/39`): **primary** = online stakeholder intake forms (`/intake/[token]` live-link Mode A); **secondary** = meeting transcripts / AI notetaker imports (currently absent — Sprint S2 lane); **secondary** = CRM read context (currently absent — Sprint S3 lane); **tertiary** = offline / manual operator entry (Sprint I3 path; now classified as support, not preferred). - **No-side-sprint governance rule** (§ 9): no reactive side sprints; no scope creep within a sprint; no revisiting completed capabilities; no pulling deferred expansion lanes forward; no working in a vacuum; no premature optimization; no new third-party dependencies without explicit canon authorization.
- **Architect decision rule** (§ 11): discoveries during sprint execution route as blocker → current sprint, critical-path dependency → scheduled future sprint, improvement → backlog, expansion → canon only. The operator (Architect) is the only authority that can reclassify; Claude proposes, operator decides.
- **Claude role bounded** (§ 10): implementer + verifier + surfacer of blockers + documenter + canon pinner. ## Offline Intake Data Model + Server Actions (2026-05-28 — Sprint I2)

Implements the foundation authorized by `docs/37` Sprint I1. Headline:

- **Migration `supabase/migrations/0017_offline_intake_extensions.sql`** extends `stakeholder_intake_sessions` (`source_type` text NOT NULL default `'live_link'`, `entered_by`, `collected_at`, `source_confidence`, `operator_notes`, `client_visible` boolean NOT NULL default `true` for backfill), drops `token_hash` NOT NULL invariant + adds CHECK constraint requiring `token_hash` only for live mode + forbidding it for offline modes; extends `stakeholder_responses` (`source_type`, `response_status` text NOT NULL default `'ready_for_synthesis'` for backfill, `entered_by`, `collected_at`, `operator_notes`, `supersedes_response_id`, `client_visible`); CHECK-constrains `source_type` (6 values), `source_confidence` (3 values), `response_status` (4 values); operator_notes length caps. Adds new table `engagement_intake_documents` (workspace-scoped RLS authenticated-only, soft-delete via `voided_at`/`voided_by`/`void_reason`, full triggers + indexes, content via `content_text` / `external_url` / `storage_path`). Backfill safety: all pre-existing rows updated to live-link + ready-for-synthesis + client_visible=true so Mode A behavior is byte-identical.
- **Types extended in `lib/intake/types.ts`** — `IntakeSourceType`, `IntakeDocumentSourceType`, `IntakeSourceConfidence`, `IntakeResponseStatus`, `EngagementIntakeDocument`, full `Create*Input` / `*Result` discriminated unions for every action. `OfflineIntakeErrorCode` enumerates 19 distinct failure modes.
- **Mapper extensions in `lib/intake/mappers.ts`** — `DbEngagementIntakeDocumentRow` shape + `mapEngagementIntakeDocumentRow()` function. Source-type + source-confidence value fitters.
- **New action module `lib/intake/offline-actions.ts`** — 6 guarded `"use server"` actions: `createOfflineStakeholderIntakeSessionAction`, `createOfflineStakeholderResponseAction`, `markStakeholderResponseReadyForSynthesisAction`, `voidStakeholderResponseAction`, `createEngagementIntakeDocumentAction`, `voidEngagementIntakeDocumentAction`. Every action enforces: cookie-bound auth, workspace-scoped RLS server client (NO service-role anywhere), engagement-workspace boundary check, response-status transition matrix (draft→ready/voided; ready→voided/superseded), per-content-mode validation (external_link requires URL, meeting_notes/transcript/email_paste require text, document_upload requires storage_path), operator_notes length caps (2000/1000/1000 per surface), soft-delete for void (no hard delete; audit trail preserved). Activity event metadata carries only safe fields (role, source_type, question_id, response_status, document title, source_confidence) — never `answer_text`, never `content_text`, never raw email, never PII.
- **Activity event types** — `lib/activity/types.ts` extended with 6 new event types (`offline_intake_session_created`, `offline_intake_response_created`, `offline_intake_response_ready`, `offline_intake_response_voided`, `intake_document_created`, `intake_document_voided`) + new entity type `engagement_intake_document`. `components/activity/activity-timeline.tsx` extended with matching tones (info/info/success/neutral/info/neutral) + labels ("Offline stakeholder staged" / "Offline response drafted" / "Response ready for synthesis" / "Offline response voided" / "Intake document attached" / "Intake document voided"). Labels deliberately use "staged"/"drafted"/"voided" to avoid implying SLATE collected the content itself.
- **Verification:** `npm run lint` clean ✅ · `NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 npm run build` clean ✅ · 29 routes byte-identical to prior baseline · package.json unchanged · zero new dependencies · zero UI components added · zero public routes added · zero service-role SQL writes · zero `mailto:` / `sendgrid` / `nodemailer` / `docusign` / `hellosign` / `adobesign` / `crm_push` / `app/s/[` / `app/sow/[` / `service_role` / `group_b_block_advance` references introduced · live-link Mode A behavior unchanged (existing `createStakeholderSession` untouched, backfill preserves existing semantics).
- **Files changed (4 modified + 2 new):** `lib/intake/types.ts` (+228), `lib/intake/mappers.ts` (+87), `lib/activity/types.ts` (+18/-2), `components/activity/activity-timeline.tsx` (+21), `lib/intake/offline-actions.ts` (NEW), `supabase/migrations/0017_offline_intake_extensions.sql` (NEW), plus 4 docs updates.
- **Boundaries preserved:** No UI components (Sprint I3 wires UI). No public routes. No stakeholder invite sending. No live intake token minting for offline mode (CHECK constraint forbids). No `/r` or `/p` mint. No Send to Client. No public SOW route. No SOW share tokens. No email/CRM/e-signature. No findings synthesis logic. No AI prompt changes. No Group-B wiring. No Sapient Digital data mutation (migration not yet applied to deployed Supabase).

Recommended next sprint: **Sprint I3 — Operator UI for Draft Stakeholders + Offline Responses** per `docs/37` § 7. Scope: update `app/app/engagements/[id]/intake/page.tsx` + related components to add "Stage offline stakeholder" form + per-stakeholder offline-response surfaces + Source chips + per-response Mark ready / Void / Supersede actions + PII-warning toast + "No message sent" UI affirmation chips. Non-goals: no findings synthesis logic (I5), no document storage backend hookup beyond `storage_path` field (I4), no new public routes.

## Sapient Digital Offline Intake Canon (2026-05-23 — Sprint I1)

`docs/37_SAPIENT_DIGITAL_OFFLINE_INTAKE_CANON.md` is the canonical authorization for an operator-staged offline intake workflow. Closes the product gap identified in `docs/36` § 9 Observation 1. Headline:

- **3 intake modes defined:** Mode A (Live stakeholder intake — existing, unchanged), Mode B (Operator-staged offline responses — new path), Mode C (Document-only intake — subset of B).
- **Data model extensions specified** (Sprint I2 implements via migration `0017_offline_intake_extensions.sql`): `stakeholder_intake_sessions` gets `source_type` + nullable `token_hash` (CHECK-constrained) + `entered_by` + `collected_at` + `source_confidence` + `operator_notes` + `client_visible` (default false for offline); `stakeholder_responses` gets matching `source_type` + `response_status` (`draft`/`ready_for_synthesis`/`superseded`/`voided`) + `entered_by` + `collected_at` + `operator_notes` + `supersedes_response_id`; new entity `engagement_intake_documents` for offline source attachments (workspace-scoped, RLS authenticated-only, never client-visible).
- **UI requirements specified** (Sprint I3): "Stage offline stakeholder" form (no email required, no token minted, "Save without sending" + "No message sent" affirmation chips), `Source` chip on every stakeholder + response card, per-response Mark ready / Void / Supersede actions, PII-warning toast on text paste, intake completeness updated to count live + offline responses uniformly.
- **Findings synthesis readiness gate** (Sprint I5): standard gate (≥2 role perspectives + ≥4 of 6 question topics + no PII warnings + operator sign-off) OR explicit operator override with audit-logged reason text.
- **Sapient Digital 5-step path** (`docs/37` § 6): pre-implementation (operator distributes `docs/36` § 6 question packet externally today, no SLATE-side work needed) → Sprint I2 lands ingest backend → Sprint I3 lands UI → operator stages offline responses → Stage 3 findings synthesis unblocks. Until then, Sapient Stage 3 stays blocked.
- **6 future sprints scoped** (`docs/37` § 7): I2 Data Model + Server Actions; I3 Operator UI; I4 Document Attachment; I5 Findings Synthesis Integration; I6 Sapient Digital Stage 2 Execution Using Offline Intake.
- **8 safety boundaries explicitly preserved** (`docs/37` § 8): no external send by default; no SLATE email/CRM/e-sign/mailto; no `/r` or `/p` mint during any I2-I6 sprint; no public SOW route or share tokens; no service-role SQL writes outside operator action layer; no Group-B claims; no client-visible artifact from unapproved offline notes; no auto-override of synthesis readiness gate.
- **7 open decisions surfaced** (`docs/37` § 10) — question taxonomy enforcement, document storage backend, PII-warning threshold, stakeholder-name optionality for offline mode, `docs/36` § 6 question packet ID assignment, bridge-state handling between Sprint I2 and Sprint I3, Sapient-first-pilot framing.
- **Zero source code changes; zero engagement mutations; zero Sapient Digital interaction; zero `/r` or `/p` minting; zero send; zero schema/package changes during this canon sprint.** Read-only inspection of `lib/intake/types.ts`, `lib/intake/actions.ts`, `supabase/migrations/0005_stakeholder_intake.sql` for canon grounding only.
- **Files modified:** `docs/37` (new), `docs/36` (Path B canonized reference), `docs/35` (readiness plan updated), `docs/08` (this block), `docs/10`.

Recommended next sprint: **Sprint I2 — Offline Intake Data Model + Server Actions** (per `docs/37` § 7). No non-prod SLATE Supabase project exists. `list_migrations` against the production project returned `{"migrations":[]}` (Supabase-CLI registry empty, meaning prior migrations were applied via a non-CLI mechanism); `list_tables` against the production project is classifier-blocked per the staging-sprint boundary.

Resolved by `pkill next` + `rm -rf .next/` + `npm run dev`. Dev-environment artifact, not a source-tree or deployment issue.

## Recommended-Action Helper

`lib/engagements/recommended-action.ts` now resolves stages → routes for the full lifecycle:

- `setup`, `intake` → `/intake`
- `synthesis` → `/findings`
- `scoring` → `/opportunities`
- `report` → `/report`
- `proposal` → `/proposal`

With `currentPath`, the helper returns `selfReference: true` so the recommended-action card renders as `Current workspace · You are here` instead of looping. Used by all seven engagement-related pages (detail, intake, findings, opportunities, roadmap, report, proposal).

## Lifecycle End-to-End

A Saipien Labs strategist can now follow the full advisory motion inside SLATE:

1. **Public scorecard** (`/scorecard*`) — directional diagnostic
2. **Lead inbox** (`/app/leads*`) — qualification with internal-only Saipien Fit Score
3. **Engagement command center** (`/app/engagements/[id]`) — stage tracker + six panels
4. **Stakeholder intake** (`/intake`) — role coverage, response quality, supporting inputs
5. **Findings review** (`/findings`) — AI-drafted findings with evidence panels and review action bar
6. **Opportunity matrix** (`/opportunities`) — Quick Wins / Strategic Builds / Low Priority / Defer · Avoid
7. **30/60/90 roadmap** (`/roadmap`) — phase columns with linked-opportunity titles
8. **Report builder** (`/report`) — 12-section assembly with linked findings/opportunities/roadmap
9. **Proposal builder** (`/proposal`) — three tiered SOW options with implementation credit

Evidence trail is intact end-to-end: every report section and proposal option points back to its source opportunities, findings, and stakeholder/document evidence.

## Stabilization Pass (2026-05-01)

Three Sprint 7 audit fixes folded in plus a cross-sprint integrity pass:

- **Fix 1 — Empty-state CTA routing.** Both `/report` and `/proposal` empty states now use `recommendedActionRoute(engagement)` to direct the user to the engagement's actual current step. Helio (intake stage) → "Continue at intake"; Atlas (setup) → "Continue at intake"; Meridian (synthesis) → "Continue at findings"; Quanta (report stage proposal page) → "Open report builder". New `recommendedActionLabel(href)` helper provides the human label.
- **Fix 2 — Canonical report section numbering.** `ReportWorkspace` outline now displays each section's true index in the report (e.g. AI Opportunity Portfolio stays `07` even when filtered to "Needs Review"). `aria-label` on each outline button now reads `Section <N> of <total>, <title>, <status>`.
- **Fix 3 — Roadmap linked-opportunity chip accessibility.** Visual truncation now uses `text-ellipsis whitespace-nowrap`; the chip carries `aria-label="Linked opportunity: <full title>"` and the visible truncated text is `aria-hidden`. Title attribute kept for hover.

Cross-sprint integrity verified (no dead links, no no-op loops, every CTA either routes to an active page, renders as `LockedActionButton` with sprint label, or resolves via `recommendedActionRoute` for empty states).

## Persistence/Auth Architecture Canon

Four documents drafted in `docs/persistence/`:

- `00_PERSISTENCE_AUTH_CANON.md` — goals, principles, role model, mock-to-real migration principle, eight acceptance criteria
- `01_DATA_MODEL_DRAFT.md` — 29 tables mapped from existing `lib/<domain>/` types; six derived panel-status pieces called out as views, not columns
- `02_MIGRATION_SEQUENCE.md` — 11-step route-by-route sequence with goals, tables introduced, mock data replaced, acceptance criteria, and risks per step
- `03_SECURITY_AND_RLS_DRAFT.md` — route posture, default-deny RLS principles, public scorecard / stakeholder token / operator policy sketches, audit-trail policy, what not to expose client-side

Recommended stack: **Supabase Postgres + Supabase Auth + RLS + Next.js Route Handlers / Server Actions**. Justified in `00_PERSISTENCE_AUTH_CANON.md`.

## Persistence/Auth Step 0 + Step 1 — what landed

**Step 0 — Supabase setup + env scaffolding.**

- `@supabase/supabase-js` and `@supabase/ssr` installed.
- `lib/env.ts` — typed env getters (`getPublicEnv`, `isSupabaseEnvConfigured`, `getSiteUrl`); throws only at call time so `next build` does not crash with missing env.
- `lib/supabase/client.ts` — browser client (uses anon key only).
- `lib/supabase/server.ts` — server client bound to `cookies()` for server components, route handlers, server actions.
- `lib/supabase/middleware.ts` — `updateSession(request)` helper for the root middleware.
- `.env.example` — names only, no values; service role key is **not** prefixed `NEXT_PUBLIC_`.
- `supabase/migrations/0001_auth_workspaces_profiles.sql` — singleton `workspaces` row, `profiles` table joined to `auth.users`, shared `set_updated_at` trigger, RLS (operator read on workspaces; operator read + self-update + self-insert on profiles), `on_auth_user_created` trigger that auto-inserts a `profiles` row when a new `auth.users` row is created.
- `supabase/migrations/README.md` — apply via Supabase Dashboard SQL Editor or `supabase db push`; CLI is optional.

**Step 1 — Auth shell + operator login.**

- `/login` (premium dark SLATE-styled magic-link form, success / error states, `?sent=1` confirms email sent, `?error=…` surfaces controlled error copy without leaking Supabase internals).
- `/auth/callback` (route handler that exchanges `code` → session and redirects to `/app`, or to `/login?error=callback` on failure).
- Root `middleware.ts` — refreshes Supabase session on `/app/*`, `/login`, `/auth/*`; redirects unauthenticated `/app/*` → `/login`; degrades to `/login?error=config` if Supabase env is unset; leaves `/scorecard*`, `/apply/*`, `/` untouched.
- Sign-out — `signOut()` server action wired to a small icon button in the sidebar identity tile; redirects to `/login`.
- Sidebar/user identity — `app/app/layout.tsx` is now an async server layout that resolves the operator via `getOperatorIdentity()` (auth user + `profiles` row, with safe fallbacks: `display_name → email → "Operator"`, derived initials when `avatar_initials` is null), threads identity to `AppShell` → `SidebarNav`. The hard-coded "MR · M. Reyes · Strategy · Saipien Labs" string is gone.
- Login form is a client component that uses the `signInWithMagicLink` server action; `LoginForm` passes the action to a real `<form action={…}>` with a pending state.

## Data / Mock Boundary (preserved)

- `/app/*` is auth-protected.
- `/app/*` still renders mock domain data from `lib/<domain>/mock-*.ts`.
- `/scorecard/*` still uses localStorage / mock scoring.
- No scorecard submission persistence (Step 2).
- No leads persistence (Step 3).
- No engagement / intake / findings / opportunities / roadmap / reports / proposals persistence (Step 4–8).
- No activity events / notes persistence (Step 9).
- No mock data deletion. No domain queries against Supabase. The only real data this sprint introduces is auth/session/profile/workspace.

## BuildOps Boundary (preserved)

Reaffirmed: BuildOps remains documentation-only. No `/app/builds`, no BuildOps nav, no builds/sprints/agent-session/repo-context tables, no QA workspace, no deployment visibility, no BuildOps API or RLS, no BuildOps backend services. None of the above changed in this sprint.

## Verified

- `npm run lint` — clean.
- `NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 npm run build` — clean. 54 routes generate (the previous 51 prerendered routes plus `/login`, `/auth/callback`, and the now-dynamic `/app` root). `/app`, `/app/leads`, `/app/engagements` are now `ƒ` (dynamic) because the layout reads cookies; the engagement and lead detail routes still SSG via `generateStaticParams`. Middleware compiles to ~82 kB.
