- Updates the run row to `completed` (or `failed` if zero findings persisted) with `output_summary` containing only counts + provider/model labels.
- Emits exactly one activity event per call (`ai_findings_generated` or `ai_synthesis_failed`). Metadata is restricted to `{ runType, generatedCount, skippedDuplicateCount, provider, model }` (no statements, excerpts, or stakeholder content).
- Revalidates `/app/engagements/<id>/findings` and `/app/engagements/<id>`. Returns `{ ok, generatedCount, skippedDuplicateCount, provider, model }` to the caller — never a raw model response.
- Partial-failure behavior: if a finding row inserts but its source refs fail, the finding row is left in `needs_review` and the operator can attach evidence manually. The error count is recorded on `output_summary.insertErrorCount`.

**UI integration.**

- `components/findings/generate-findings-form.tsx` (`"use client"`) — shows `Generate draft findings` CTA with subdued "Draft only · operator review required" badge. Three states:
  - `aiConfigured = true` and intake responses present → enabled CTA, copy explains scorecard + intake + metadata are used and uploaded files are not parsed.
  - `aiConfigured = true` and no intake responses → CTA enabled but a warning banner says synthesis will run on scorecard context only and most candidates will be flagged as assumptions.
  - `aiConfigured = false` → CTA disabled, controlled message: *"AI synthesis is not configured for this environment. Add the provider key server-side (`OPENAI_API_KEY` in `.env.local`) to enable draft generation."*
- Component uses `useTransition`. On success it shows generated count + skipped duplicates. On failure it shows a translated error code (e.g., `ai-rate-limited` → "AI provider rate-limit reached. Wait a minute and try again.").
- `app/app/engagements/[id]/findings/page.tsx` — calls `isAiConfigured()` server-side and threads it plus `hasIntakeEvidence = candidates.length > 0` into `<GenerateFindingsForm>`. The form renders only when `loaded.kind === "real"`. Mock slug engagements never see the AI synthesis surface. Page meta line now reads "AI Synthesis Step 1 · Live" / "AI draft + operator-authored · human approval required".
- `components/findings/findings-workspace.tsx` — finding detail panel now branches its category badges: AI-drafted findings keep the existing `Sparkles · AI-drafted` badge; operator-authored findings (`aiDrafted === false`) render a neutral `Operator-authored` badge instead. List items also gain a subtle `Sparkles · AI` badge when AI-drafted. Existing mock findings (where `aiDrafted` is undefined) keep the AI-drafted treatment so seeded screenshots don't regress.
- `lib/findings/types.ts` + `lib/findings/mappers.ts` — `Finding` type now carries an optional `aiDrafted: boolean`; the DB → TS mapper sets `aiDrafted: Boolean(row.ai_drafted)` so persisted findings carry the real flag.

**Activity events.**

- `lib/activity/types.ts` — added `ai_findings_generated` and `ai_synthesis_failed` to `ActivityEventType`; added `ai_synthesis_run` to `ActivityEntityType`.
- `components/activity/activity-timeline.tsx` — added tone + label entries: `ai_findings_generated → ai / "AI findings generated"`, `ai_synthesis_failed → risk / "AI synthesis failed"`.
- Activity metadata restricted to `{ runType, generatedCount, skippedDuplicateCount, provider, model }`. The existing `sanitizeMetadata` (Step 9) still drops keys matching `/token|secret|password|apikey|authorization|cookie|email|body|raw|excerpt/i` defensively even though the synthesis caller never includes those.

**Public/internal boundary.**

- Only authenticated operators can trigger synthesis. `generateDraftFindingsForEngagement` short-circuits on `auth.getUser()` before any context is built or any provider call is made.
- Synthesis output is stored as operator-only `findings` rows under existing `findings_operator_full` RLS. Public scorecard / public intake routes never read findings, source refs, synthesis runs, or activity events.
- The OpenAI key is read only in `lib/ai/provider.ts` (server-only) and never leaves the server. The browser bundle never receives any synthesis code (the `server-only` import enforces this at build time).
- The page server component reads `isAiConfigured()` once and passes only the boolean down — the key is never serialized into the rendered HTML.

**Mock boundary preserved.**

- Legacy mock slug engagements continue rendering the seeded mock findings from `lib/findings/mock-findings.ts` — no AI synthesis surface, no `GenerateFindingsForm`, no `ManualFindingPlaceholder` regression.
- The CTA appears only when `loaded.kind === "real"` AND `aiConfigured === true`.

**Validation guardrails.**

- Categories: 8-value allowlist matching `findings.category` DB vocabulary. Unknown → reject candidate.
- Confidence: 4-value allowlist. Unknown → default to `needs_evidence`.
- Source types: 4-value allowlist. Unknown → drop the source ref.
- Source strength: 4 values (`strong`, `adequate`, `thin`, `missing`). `missing` is recorded on the candidate but persisted as `thin` (the DB strength vocabulary is 3-valued).
- Statement required and non-empty; clipped to 240 chars.
- Source IDs are validated against the canonical UUID regex; invalid IDs are dropped (the source ref still persists with the label/excerpt only).
- Generated count clamped to 7 maximum even if the model returns more.
- Every finding must have ≥ 1 source ref OR `assumptionFlag = true`.
- Statement deduplication is case-insensitive and whitespace-collapsed against existing findings on the engagement.

**Verification.**

- `npm run lint` — clean.
- `NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 npm run build` — clean. 25 routes. `/app/engagements/[id]/findings` First Load grew from 113 kB to 114 kB (one new client component bundled).
- Build also succeeds with `OPENAI_API_KEY` unset — `getAiProviderConfig()` returns `null`, the page renders the controlled "AI synthesis is not configured" state, no provider calls are issued.
- Secret handling: `.env.local` was not read, modified, or staged; no provider keys, no prompt bodies, no raw model responses, no stakeholder content, no Supabase keys printed during this sprint. `.env.example` updated with names only (`SLATE_AI_PROVIDER`, `OPENAI_API_KEY`, `SLATE_AI_FINDINGS_MODEL`).

## AI Synthesis Step 1.1 — what landed

**Goal.** Make AI findings synthesis discoverable from the engagement workflow without encouraging premature low-evidence generation. No persistence changes, no provider behavior changes, no migrations.

**Engagement detail discoverability.**

- `app/app/engagements/[id]/page.tsx` reads `isAiConfigured()` server-side and computes `aiAvailable = isPersistedEngagement && isAiConfigured()` plus `hasIntakeEvidence` (true when ≥ 1 stakeholder is in-progress or completed). Both are passed down to the panel + tracker.
- `components/engagements/findings-status-panel.tsx` accepts new optional `aiAvailable` and `hasIntakeEvidence` props. When `aiAvailable === true`, the header renders a subtle `Sparkles · AI draft available` outline badge next to the existing status badge, plus a one-line readiness footnote between the description and progress bar:
  - With intake evidence: *"AI draft findings is ready to run."*
  - Without intake evidence: *"AI draft available, but intake evidence is thin. Capture stakeholder input first."*
- The Findings panel CTA (`Review Findings`) is now linked whenever `aiAvailable === true`, so operators can reach the synthesis CTA on a brand-new engagement without first having to advance the stage or accumulate findings.
- `components/engagements/engagement-status-panel.tsx` gained two new optional slots (`headerAccessory` and `footnote`). All existing call sites continue to render unchanged because the slots are undefined by default.

**Stage tracker indicator.**

- `components/engagements/engagement-stage-tracker.tsx` accepts an optional `aiAvailableStages?: EngagementStage[]` prop. When the synthesis stage is included, both layouts (desktop horizontal + mobile vertical) render a small `Sparkles` icon next to the stage label. Hover/title text and aria-label both read "AI draft findings available."
- Empty/undefined `aiAvailableStages` means no indicator renders — the prior visual is preserved exactly for legacy mock slug engagements and for environments without an AI provider key.
- The engagement detail page passes `aiAvailableStages={aiAvailable ? ["synthesis"] : undefined}`. No mock slug engagement renders the indicator because mock-engagement detail pages 404 (the engagement detail route only resolves UUIDs).

**Findings page guidance — `components/findings/generate-findings-form.tsx`.**

- Description copy gained one trailing sentence: *"Best results come after at least one stakeholder completes intake."*
- The "Limited evidence" warning text was rewritten:
  - Before: *"No stakeholder intake responses are attached yet. Synthesis will run on scorecard context only — most candidates will be flagged as assumptions until intake responses land."*
  - After: *"No stakeholder intake responses are attached yet. You can generate scorecard-only draft findings, but they will be assumption-heavy. For a stronger AI pass, capture stakeholder input first."*
- A new secondary `Manage intake first ↗` link sits inside the warning, deep-linking to `/app/engagements/<engagementId>/intake`. The primary `Generate draft findings` CTA remains enabled — the choice stays with the operator.

**Recommended action behavior.**

- Unchanged. `lib/engagements/recommended-action.ts` still routes by stage (`setup | intake → /intake`, `synthesis → /findings`, etc). When stakeholder evidence is missing the engagement is in `setup` or `intake`, so the recommended action stays `Manage Intake`. The new AI affordances are subordinate ambient signals, not primary CTAs.

**Mock + public boundary.**

- AI affordances render only when the engagement is a real persisted UUID AND `OPENAI_API_KEY` is configured. Mock slug engagements (`atlas-aios-q2`, `helio-aios-q2`, `meridian-aios-q2`, `quanta-aios-q1`, `caldera-aios-q1`) do not show the affordances.
- The public `/scorecard*` and `/intake/[token]` routes are unaffected — none of them import `isAiConfigured()` or render the engagement workflow surfaces.

**Verification.**

- `npm run lint` — clean.
- `NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 npm run build` — clean. 25 routes. `/app/engagements/[id]` First Load unchanged at 107 kB; `/app/engagements/[id]/findings` grew from 10.6 kB to 10.8 kB (the new `Manage intake first` link).
- Manual browser run via Playwright: signed in via service-role-minted magic link, navigated to `/app/engagements/76097653-fedb-42e5-9ef6-e89a0e97f802`, confirmed Sparkles next to "Synthesis" in stage tracker, `AI draft available` badge + "AI draft available, but intake evidence is thin. Capture stakeholder input first." footnote on the Findings panel, recommended action remains "Manage Intake". Navigated to `/findings`, confirmed updated copy + `Manage intake first` link inside the warning. Navigated to `quanta-aios-q1/findings` (mock slug), confirmed no AI affordance (page meta correctly shows "Sprint 5 · Mock data").

## AI Synthesis Step 2 — what landed

**Goal.** Operator-triggered AI draft opportunity generation from consultant-approved findings. AI output is stored as `status = draft` opportunities tied to the source findings via `opportunity_finding_links`. The operator must select, defer, or reject each draft using the existing review action bar — drafts do not auto-promote into the roadmap.

**No migration.** The Step 1 `ai_synthesis_runs` table accepts arbitrary `run_type` text; Step 2 reuses it with `run_type = 'opportunity_draft'`. The Step 7 `opportunities` and `opportunity_finding_links` tables already carry the score columns, status vocabulary, and join shape needed. Zero schema changes ship with this step.

**AI provider layer additions — `lib/ai/{types,provider,opportunities-context,opportunities-synthesis}.ts`.**

- `lib/ai/types.ts` — adds `DraftOpportunityCandidate`, `DraftOpportunityEvidenceStrength`, `OpportunityProviderInvocationOk`, and `OpportunityProviderInvocationResult` (a discriminated union mirroring the findings shape).
- `lib/ai/provider.ts` — adds `getAiOpportunityProviderConfig()`. Reads `SLATE_AI_OPPORTUNITIES_MODEL` first, falls back to `SLATE_AI_FINDINGS_MODEL`, then to the default model. Returns `null` when the provider key is absent. `isAiConfigured()` continues to gate the UI.
- `lib/ai/opportunities-context.ts` — `buildOpportunitySynthesisContext(engagementId)`. Server-only, `auth.getUser()`-gated. Loads engagement + linked account + scorecard summary (when present) + approved/report-ready findings (with up to 5 source refs each, label + role only — no excerpt) + input asset metadata + existing opportunities (with linked finding IDs). Caps: ≤ 20 findings, ≤ 5 source refs per finding, ≤ 20 existing opportunities, ≤ 20 scorecard answers, ≤ 20 input assets. Per-string clip 240–360 chars depending on field. Returns `error: 'no-approved-findings'` if zero eligible findings exist — short-circuiting before any provider call.
- `lib/ai/opportunities-synthesis.ts` — `synthesizeDraftOpportunities(context)` shapes the system + schema-instruction + user prompt and calls the provider with `temperature = 0.2`, `maxTokens = 2200`. Strict validator drops candidates without a title, candidates whose `linkedFindingIds` do not match the eligible set, candidates with unknown categories, and duplicate titles within the batch. Scores clamped to 0–100 integers. Arrays clamped to ≤ 6 items × ≤ 200 chars each. Generated count clamped to 6 maximum.

**Server action — `lib/opportunities/synthesis-actions.ts`.**

- `generateDraftOpportunitiesForEngagement(engagementId)` — operator-only. Auth-gates first, returns `ai-not-configured` immediately when the key is absent. Returns `no-approved-findings` immediately when the context builder returns no eligible findings — no `ai_synthesis_runs` row is opened in this case (the table records actual provider invocations, not pre-checks).
- Opens an `ai_synthesis_runs` row in `started` state with `input_summary` containing only safe counts (`findings`, `inputAssets`, `scorecardAnswers`, `existingOpportunities`, `min/maxOpportunities`). No prompt body, no excerpt, no model response is ever written to this row.
- Calls `synthesizeDraftOpportunities` and inspects the result. On provider error: marks the run `failed`, emits `ai_synthesis_failed`, revalidates routes, returns the typed error.
- On success: deduplicates against existing opportunity titles (case-insensitive, whitespace-collapsed) and within the batch. For each surviving candidate: derives quadrant via `pickQuadrant(impact, complexity, risk)` (preserves the high-risk `risk >= 85 → defer-avoid` override from `createOpportunity`), derives priority from quadrant via `priorityFromQuadrant`, inserts the opportunity row with `status = draft`. Then inserts `opportunity_finding_links` rows for every linked finding ID with `strength` mirroring the validated `evidenceStrength`.
- Updates the run row to `completed` (or `failed` if zero opportunities persisted) with `output_summary` containing only counts + provider/model labels + `linkErrorCount`.
- Emits exactly one activity event per call (`ai_opportunities_generated` or `ai_synthesis_failed`). Metadata is restricted to `{ runType, generatedCount, skippedDuplicateCount, provider, model }` (no titles, descriptions, or finding statements).
- Revalidates `/app/engagements/<id>/opportunities`, `/app/engagements/<id>/roadmap`, and `/app/engagements/<id>`. Returns `{ ok, generatedCount, skippedDuplicateCount, provider, model }` to the caller — never a raw model response.
- Partial-failure behavior: if an opportunity inserts but its links fail, the opportunity row is left in `draft` and the operator can attach evidence manually. The link error count is recorded on `output_summary.linkErrorCount`.

**UI integration.**

- `components/opportunities/generate-opportunities-form.tsx` (`"use client"`) — shows `Generate draft opportunities` CTA with a subdued "Draft only · operator review required" badge. Three states:
  - `aiConfigured = true` and `hasApprovedFindings = true` → enabled CTA, copy explains approved/report-ready findings are used as evidence and that drafts must still be selected/deferred/rejected.
  - `aiConfigured = true` and `hasApprovedFindings = false` → CTA disabled, controlled warning: *"Approve or mark findings report-ready before drafting opportunities."* with a deep link to `/app/engagements/<id>/findings`.
  - `aiConfigured = false` → CTA disabled, controlled message: *"AI opportunity drafting is not configured for this environment. Add the provider key server-side (`OPENAI_API_KEY` in `.env.local`) to enable draft generation."*
- Component uses `useTransition`. On success it shows generated count + skipped duplicates. On failure it shows a translated error code (e.g., `ai-rate-limited` → "AI provider rate-limit reached. Wait a minute and try again.").
- `app/app/engagements/[id]/opportunities/page.tsx` — calls `isAiConfigured()` server-side and threads `aiConfigured = isPersisted && isAiConfigured()` plus `hasApprovedFindings = findingCandidates.length > 0` (the existing Step 7 candidate query already filters `review_status in ('approved', 'report_ready')`) into `<GenerateOpportunitiesForm>`. The form renders only inside the `isPersisted` branch — mock slug engagements never see the AI drafting CTA. Page meta line now reads "AI Synthesis Step 2 · Live" when AI is configured on a real engagement.
- Existing manual `<CreateOpportunityForm>` continues to render below the AI form. `OpportunityActionBar` (selected / deferred / rejected / reopen) drives the lifecycle on AI-drafted opportunities exactly as it does on manually-scored ones.

**Activity events.**

- `lib/activity/types.ts` — added `ai_opportunities_generated` to `ActivityEventType` (no new entity type — `ai_synthesis_run` from Step 1 covers it).
- `components/activity/activity-timeline.tsx` — added tone + label entries: `ai_opportunities_generated → ai / "AI opportunities generated"`. `ai_synthesis_failed` continues to handle failures.
- Activity metadata restricted to `{ runType, generatedCount, skippedDuplicateCount, provider, model }`. The Step 9 `sanitizeMetadata` continues to drop forbidden keys defensively.

**Public/internal boundary.**

- Only authenticated operators can trigger opportunity synthesis. `generateDraftOpportunitiesForEngagement` short-circuits on `auth.getUser()` before any context is built or any provider call is made.
- Synthesis output is stored as operator-only `opportunities` rows under existing `opportunities_operator_full` RLS plus `opportunity_finding_links` rows under `opportunity_finding_links_operator_full`. Public scorecard / public intake routes never read opportunities, links, synthesis runs, or activity events.
- The OpenAI key is read only in `lib/ai/provider.ts` (server-only) and never leaves the server. The browser bundle never receives any synthesis code (the `server-only` import enforces this at build time).
- The page server component reads `isAiConfigured()` once and passes only the boolean down — the key is never serialized into the rendered HTML.

**Mock boundary preserved.**

- Legacy mock slug engagements continue rendering the seeded mock opportunities from `lib/opportunities/mock-opportunities.ts` — no AI synthesis surface, no `GenerateOpportunitiesForm`, no `CreateOpportunityForm` regression.
- The CTA appears only when `loaded.kind === "real"` AND `aiConfigured === true`.

**Validation guardrails.**

- Categories: 9-value allowlist matching the existing `OpportunityCategory` vocabulary. Unknown → reject candidate.
- Evidence strength: 3-value allowlist (`strong`, `adequate`, `thin`). Unknown → default to `adequate`.
- `linkedFindingIds`: must be valid UUIDs AND must be in the eligible set returned by the context builder (approved/report-ready findings only). Otherwise dropped.
- Title required and non-empty; clipped to 160 chars.
- Description / source summary / recommended action / implementation shape clipped to 800 / 600 / 400 / 500 chars.
- Arrays (`dependencies`, `risks`, `successSignals`): max 6 items × max 200 chars per item.
- All scores clamped to 0–100 integers (default 50 if missing or NaN).
- Generated count clamped to 6 max even if the model returns more.
- Title-based dedup is case-insensitive and whitespace-collapsed; runs against existing engagement opportunities AND within the same batch.
- Server-derives priority + quadrant from impact + complexity + risk via the existing `computeQuadrant` helper, then applies the high-risk override (`risk >= 85 → defer-avoid`). Model-provided priority/quadrant is ignored.

**Verification.**

- `npm run lint` — clean.
- `NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 npm run build` — clean. Route count unchanged at 25; `/app/engagements/[id]/opportunities` First Load grew slightly to accommodate the new client component.
- Build also succeeds with `OPENAI_API_KEY` unset — `getAiProviderConfig()` returns `null`, the page renders the controlled "AI opportunity drafting is not configured" state, no provider calls are issued.
- Secret handling: `.env.local` was not read, modified, or staged; no provider keys, no prompt bodies, no raw model responses, no stakeholder content, no opportunity descriptions, no finding excerpts, no Supabase keys printed during this sprint. `.env.example` updated with names only (`SLATE_AI_OPPORTUNITIES_MODEL`).

## Phase 1A Acceptance Audit (2026-05-07)

`docs/12_PHASE_1A_ACCEPTANCE_AUDIT.md` is the canonical audit. Headline:

- **Accept Phase 1A with Minor Fixes.** AdvisoryOps OS foundation is functionally complete, persisted, secured, and build-clean.
- **Two minor fixes applied during the audit:** stale `Mock — not wired` badges were removed from `components/proposals/proposal-workspace.tsx` (`ProposalOptionDetail`) and `components/intake/follow-up-queue.tsx` (`FollowUpQueue`). Both badges had been bleeding the `mock` label onto persisted Step 5 / Step 8 engagements where they were incorrect; the page meta line ("Persistence Step N · Live" vs "Sprint N · Mock data") already differentiates persisted vs mock.
- **Phase 1B (Consulting-Grade Deliverable Engine) is required and not yet scoped.** The current report and proposal builders are operationally complete but are **not** McKinsey/BCG/Bain-caliber output. There is no charting layer, no consulting exhibit library (waterfall, bridge, bubble, heatmap, swimlane), no rich-text section bodies, no PDF / DOCX export, no AI section drafting for reports, no AI option drafting for proposals, no AI roadmap drafting, no document parsing, no industry benchmark dataset.

## Phase 1B Chart & Exhibit Canon (2026-05-07)

`docs/13_PHASE_1B_CHART_EXHIBIT_CANON.md` is the next governance artifact in the Phase 1B sequence and has now landed. It approves Visx as the underlying charting layer, formalizes the SLATE chart-vocabulary architecture (only chart primitives may import `@visx/*`; exhibits compose primitives; app pages consume exhibits only), defines the eight required exhibits with purpose / inputs / structure / source-note / dependencies / data-readiness honesty, and sets the recommended implementation order. **The canon does not authorize building the remaining seven exhibits — that requires a separate sprint approval.**

## Phase 1B Exhibit Sprint 1 — Risk-Adjusted Priority Quadrant

The first real Phase 1B exhibit ships at `components/charts/exhibits/risk-adjusted-priority-quadrant.tsx`. It is an analytical 2×2 (impact × complexity) with bubble size = business impact and color = risk band (4 deterministic thresholds mapped to the existing `success / info / warning / risk` chart-tone vocabulary — no new tone added). Quadrant midlines are at 50/50 (analytical-view convention; the operator editing matrix continues to use the 70/60 thresholds in `lib/opportunities/helpers.ts`). The exhibit is a pure server component, accepts a narrow `RiskAdjustedQuadrantPoint[]` shape, and ships with an exported `opportunityToRiskQuadrantPoint` adapter that the future report-wiring sprint will consume. The unlinked `/app/charts-preview` route now renders both the proof-of-fit Executive Summary 2×2 and the Sprint 1 Risk-Adjusted Priority Quadrant. **The exhibit is not yet wired into the report or proposal builders — Sprint 1 proves the exhibit component and preview rendering only.** No package dependencies were added; no new Visx packages were introduced.

## Phase 1B Exhibit Sprint 2 — Capability Maturity Heatmap

Sprint 2 adds the second real exhibit and its supporting primitive. The exhibit ships at `components/charts/exhibits/capability-maturity-heatmap.tsx`; the primitive at `components/charts/primitives/chart-heatmap-cell.tsx`. The heatmap renders a capability × dimension grid with cell color encoding a 4-band maturity scale (0–39 risk · 40–59 warning · 60–79 info · 80–100 success — same chart-tone vocabulary as Sprint 1) and a tabular-mono numeric overlay for the score. Row and column labels render in `JetBrains Mono` uppercase tracking-1.4. The cell primitive is plain SVG `rect` + `text` — **`@visx/heatmap` was deliberately not installed**; the primitive is reusable for the Stakeholder Coverage Matrix in a later sprint without code changes. The exhibit is data-shape-pure (`CapabilityMaturityCell[]` plus `capabilities` + `dimensions` row/column arrays) and renders only on the unlinked `/app/charts-preview` route. **Sprint 2 ships the preview component only — not yet wired into the report or proposal builders.** No package dependencies added.

## Phase 1B Exhibit Sprint 3 — Stakeholder Coverage Matrix

Sprint 3 adds the third real exhibit at `components/charts/exhibits/stakeholder-coverage-matrix.tsx`. It is a role × topic intake-coverage heatmap that surfaces evidence gaps before a report is treated as fully grounded. Cell color encodes a 4-tone evidence-strength scale (`missing → neutral`, `thin → warning`, `adequate → info`, `strong → success` — same chart-tone vocabulary as Sprints 1 + 2). Cell label shows the supporting `responseCount` for present cells and an em-dash for missing cells; missing cells additionally render at low fill opacity with a subtler stroke and a dimmer label color so a coverage gap visibly differs from a low-strength response. **The exhibit reuses the Sprint 2 `ChartHeatmapCell` primitive without modification** — the canon's promise that one primitive backs both heatmap exhibits is now realized; the primitive is unchanged. The exhibit is data-shape-pure (`StakeholderCoverageCell[]` plus `roles` + `topics` row/column arrays) and renders as the fourth exhibit on `/app/charts-preview`. **Sprint 3 ships the preview component only — not yet wired into the report or proposal builders.** No package dependencies added.

## Phase 1B Exhibit Sprint 4 — Roadmap Gantt with Dependencies

Sprint 4 adds the fourth real exhibit at `components/charts/exhibits/roadmap-gantt-with-dependencies.tsx` and two new generic primitives: `components/charts/primitives/chart-gantt-bar.tsx` and `components/charts/primitives/chart-dependency-arrow.tsx` (the latter also exports `ChartDependencyArrowheadMarker` for the SVG `<marker>` arrowhead). The exhibit renders a fixed 0–90 day timeline with phase headers (Days 0–30 / 31–60 / 61–90), dashed phase boundaries, a `--color-brand-primary` Today marker, and right-angle dependency arrows routed source-bar-right → vertical drop → target-bar-left. Bar color follows the existing chart-tone vocabulary mapped to `RoadmapStatus`: `planned → info`, `in_progress → brand`, `blocked → risk`, `complete → neutral` (and rendered muted via the bar primitive's `muted` flag so completed work recedes). Item titles sit in the left margin in `JetBrains Mono` uppercase tracking-1.4; the bars themselves are clean colored blocks with no internal text. Defensive clamping: items past day 90 are visually clipped to the chart edge; negative or zero durations are normalized to a minimum visible width; dependency edges referencing unknown ids are silently dropped. The two new primitives are deliberately generic — `ChartGanttBar` is reusable by any horizontal-bar exhibit, `ChartDependencyArrow` is reusable by any "A → B" connector. The exhibit is data-shape-pure (`RoadmapGanttItem[]`) and renders as the fifth exhibit on `/app/charts-preview`. **Sprint 4 ships the preview component only — not yet wired into the report or proposal builders, and no roadmap AI drafting was added.** No package dependencies added; no new Visx packages.

## Phase 1B Benchmark Data Canon (2026-05-08)

`docs/14_PHASE_1B_BENCHMARK_DATA_CANON.md` is the canonical governance doc for any future benchmark / peer-comparison work. It defines three benchmark-data categories — `illustrative`, `internal_directional`, `validated` — with crisp boundaries; specifies the exact source-note string for each tier; defines the future TS data shape (`BenchmarkComparisonPoint`, `BenchmarkComparisonDataset`) plus validation rules (`p25 ≤ p50 ≤ p75`, `methodology` required for validated, etc.); enumerates prohibited and allowed claim language ("industry benchmark" / "above average" / "in the top quartile" are gated on validated data); and defines three readiness gates (Gate 0 → preview only · Gate 1 → operator-internal · Gate 2 → client-facing). It also proposes six initial dimensions grounded in SLATE's existing context (AI readiness, workflow friction, systems readiness, data readiness, governance maturity, adoption capacity). **The canon does not authorize building Benchmark Comparison Bars or the `ChartPercentileBand` primitive — that requires a separate sprint approval.** No benchmark dataset, schema, migration, or seed file ships with this canon.

## Phase 1B Exhibit Sprint 5 — Benchmark Comparison Bars (Gate 0 illustrative only)

Sprint 5 ships the fifth real exhibit at `components/charts/exhibits/benchmark-comparison-bars.tsx` plus a new generic primitive at `components/charts/primitives/chart-percentile-band.tsx` (rail + IQR box + median line + diamond marker, value-agnostic — accepts pre-computed SVG x-positions). The exhibit renders one row per dimension with a 0–100 axis, a faint background rail, an info-tone IQR box from p25 to p75, a median line at p50, and a brand-primary diamond marker at the client score. Bottom axis emits ticks only at 0/25/50/75/100 with a neutral `SCORE · 0–100` caption (the canon prohibits "better"/"stronger" axis framing because `Workflow friction` is inverted). The exhibit ships an exported `validateAndClampPoint` helper that clamps scores to 0–100 and **rejects** rows where `p25 > p50` or `p50 > p75` rather than silently sorting; if every row is invalid it renders an inline empty-state. An exported `defaultBenchmarkSourceNote` derives the canonical source-note string from `dataset.status` per `docs/14`. **Sprint 5 ships under Gate 0 — illustrative preview data only.** The exhibit is rendered as the sixth exhibit on `/app/charts-preview` with a clearly-labeled illustrative dataset; it carries an "Illustrative · Gate 0" status pill in the legend, a conservative default takeaway ("Illustrative comparison structure only; validated benchmark data is required before client-facing use."), and the exact illustrative source note ("Illustrative sample data · not a benchmark"). **The exhibit MUST NOT be wired into reports, proposals, public-scorecard surfaces, or PDF exports** until a Gate 1 / Gate 2 dataset exists. No package dependencies added; no benchmark data files; no schema; no AI synthesis changes.

## Phase 1B Financial Assumptions Canon (2026-05-08)

`docs/15_PHASE_1B_FINANCIAL_ASSUMPTIONS_CANON.md` is the canonical governance doc for any future financial / ROI / savings claim in any SLATE chart, exhibit, report, proposal, or PDF artifact. It defines four financial-assumption tiers — `illustrative`, `operator_estimated`, `client_validated`, `finance_approved` — with crisp boundaries; specifies the exact source-note string for each tier; enumerates 13 required financial inputs (baseline cost / hours, hourly cost, implementation cost, recurring cost, automation rate, adoption rate, risk adjustment, time-to-value, confidence, owner, validation status, last-reviewed); defines the future TS data shapes (`FinancialAssumptionSet`, `SavingsWaterfallContribution`, `RoiBridgePoint`) plus 12 validation rules (rates clamped 0–1, `lowEstimate ≤ expectedValue ≤ highEstimate`, `finance_approved` requires owner + reviewedAt, etc.); enumerates prohibited claim language ("guaranteed savings", "payback in X months", "will save $X", "board-ready ROI" all gated on Gate 3 finance approval); defines four readiness gates (Gate 0 → preview only · Gate 1 → operator-internal · Gate 2 → draft client surfaces with caveats · Gate 3 → final client-facing); and adds dedicated rule blocks for AI-Savings Waterfall and ROI Bridge. **The canon does not authorize building AI-Savings Waterfall, ROI Bridge, or any of the financial-exhibit primitives (`chart-waterfall-bar`, `chart-projection-line`, `chart-band-area`) — each requires a separate sprint approval.** No financial-model dataset, schema, migration, or seed file ships with this canon.

## Phase 1B Exhibit Sprint 6 — AI-Savings Waterfall (Gate 0 illustrative only)

Sprint 6 ships the sixth real exhibit at `components/charts/exhibits/ai-savings-waterfall.tsx` plus a new generic primitive at `components/charts/primitives/chart-waterfall-bar.tsx` (stepped SVG bar with optional `modeled` / `muted` flags; value-agnostic — accepts pre-computed SVG geometry; zero financial wording in the primitive). The exhibit walks the viewer from a baseline cost bar through per-contribution savings / cost bars to a modeled-state bar with a dashed-outline "modeled" treatment. Connectors render as dashed segments at the running cost stack between adjacent bars. **Y-axis is COST, not savings** — bars represent cost levels; savings reduce the cost stack (descending bars in `success` tone), costs increase it (ascending bars in `warning` tone). The exhibit never labels a single figure as "net savings"; the canon prohibits "guaranteed savings", "payback", "ROI", and similar firm-claim language. Exports `validateAssumptionSet`, `validateContribution`, `defaultFinancialSourceNote`, and `deriveBaselineCost` — pure helpers a future report-wiring sprint can reuse without re-implementing the canon's invariants. **Sprint 6 ships under Gate 0 — illustrative preview data only.** The exhibit is rendered as the seventh exhibit on `/app/charts-preview` with a clearly-labeled illustrative dataset; it carries an "Illustrative · Gate 0" status pill in the legend, a conservative default takeaway ("Illustrative savings structure only; validated financial assumptions are required before client-facing use."), and the exact illustrative source note ("Source: Illustrative sample data · not a financial model"). **The exhibit MUST NOT be wired into reports, proposals, public-scorecard surfaces, or PDF exports** until a Gate 1 / Gate 2 / Gate 3 assumption set exists. ROI Bridge is NOT implemented in this sprint. No package dependencies added; no financial data files; no schema; no AI synthesis changes.

## Phase 1B Exhibit Sprint 7 — ROI Bridge (Gate 0 illustrative only)

Sprint 7 ships the seventh and final Phase 1B exhibit at `components/charts/exhibits/roi-bridge.tsx` plus two new generic primitives at `components/charts/primitives/chart-projection-line.tsx` (polyline with optional marker dots; zero ROI / financial wording) and `components/charts/primitives/chart-band-area.tsx` (closed-area band traced upper L→R then lower R→L; zero domain wording). The exhibit walks the viewer through a multi-period (Y1 / Y2 / Y3) modeled-return range. **It deliberately renders a SENSITIVITY BAND (low / expected / high) rather than a single deterministic line**, so the cone-of-uncertainty stays visible at every period — the canon's no-collapsed-band invariant is enforced at the validation layer (`validateRoiBridgePoint` rejects `lowEstimate === expectedValue === highEstimate`, plus `lowEstimate > expectedValue` and `expectedValue > highEstimate`). Y-axis caption reads `MODELED RANGE · %`; per-marker labels are plain percentages — never "ROI", "return", "payback", or "break-even". The exhibit deliberately rejects "guaranteed ROI" / "payback in X months" / "break-even" / "cash-flow positive by" / "will return X%" / "board-ready ROI" framing. Exports `validateAssumptionSet`, `validateRoiBridgePoint`, and `defaultRoiBridgeSourceNote`. The financial-assumption types (`FinancialAssumptionSet`, `FinancialConfidence`, `FinancialAssumptionStatus`) are intentionally duplicated from Sprint 6's `ai-savings-waterfall.tsx` so each exhibit stays data-shape-pure and free of cross-exhibit imports; both type sets match `docs/15` byte-for-byte and a future shared-types extraction sprint will lift them into `lib/charts/financial-types.ts` once a third financial exhibit exists. **Sprint 7 ships under Gate 0 — illustrative preview data only.** The exhibit is rendered as the eighth exhibit on `/app/charts-preview` with a clearly-labeled illustrative dataset (Y1 60% [30–90], Y2 140% [80–220], Y3 220% [140–320]); it carries an "Illustrative · Gate 0" status pill in the legend, a conservative default takeaway ("Illustrative ROI structure only; finance-approved assumptions are required before client-facing return claims."), and the exact illustrative source note ("Source: Illustrative sample data · not a financial model"). **The exhibit MUST NOT be wired into reports, proposals, public-scorecard surfaces, or PDF exports** until a Gate 1 / Gate 2 / Gate 3 assumption set exists. **The Phase 1B preview library is now complete (eight of eight exhibits).** No package dependencies added; no financial data files; no schema; no AI synthesis changes.

## Phase 1B Preview Library Acceptance Audit (2026-05-11)

`docs/16_PHASE_1B_PREVIEW_LIBRARY_ACCEPTANCE_AUDIT.md` is the formal acceptance audit of the eight-exhibit Phase 1B preview library before any report / proposal / public-scorecard / PDF wiring begins. Outcome: **Accepted with notes** for downstream report-wiring preparation. Audit verifies inventory (8 exhibits + 11 primitives + README + preview page + 3 canon docs), architecture compliance (SVG-only, zero canvas, zero `"use client"` in chart code, zero `@visx/*` imports outside `components/charts/`, zero primitive imports outside `components/charts/exhibits/`, no new package deps, static sample data only at the preview-page call site, 0 KB client JS for `/app/charts-preview`), and canon / gating compliance (the three Gate-0 exhibits — Benchmark Comparison Bars, AI-Savings Waterfall, ROI Bridge — render the exact canonical illustrative source notes byte-for-byte and are confined to the preview route; no firm benchmark / ROI / savings / payback / break-even / financial-return claims appear anywhere outside canon prohibition text). Build + lint clean; every route is within 0 KB of the Sprint 7 baseline. Five polish items recorded in a backlog (label/bubble overlap on the Risk Quadrant; row-label gutter crowding on the Capability Maturity Heatmap; long category labels on the AI-Savings Waterfall; `modeled` context on the ROI Bridge numeric labels; full framed-card review owed before report integration) — none blocks downstream wiring preparation. **No exhibit, primitive, or preview-page source code was changed for this audit.** The audit explicitly classifies the five non-financial / non-benchmark exhibits as Group A (eligible for the next report-wiring sprint, given persisted data already exists) and the three Gate-0 financial / benchmark exhibits as Group B (gated on `docs/14` and `docs/15` reaching their next data tier before any wiring touches client-facing surfaces). The audit recommends **Phase 1B Report Exhibit Wiring Sprint 0 — adapter and slot mapping** as the next sprint: author a wiring canon doc, scaffold the five Group-A adapters under `lib/charts/adapters/`, and add an operator-only diagnostic surface — explicitly stopping short of editing `ReportWorkspace`, `report_sections`, or any client-facing surface in this sprint. Visual artifacts for all eight exhibits land in `artifacts/phase-1b-preview-library/` (gitignored; not committed).

## Phase 1B SOW Draft Sprint P6-B — SOW Draft foundation (2026-05-17)

Sprint P6-B ships the operator-only SOW Draft foundation per `docs/26` § Proposed Sprint Sequence. **Foundation-only sprint** — no UI mounted, no internal SOW Draft route (Sprint P6-C scope), no public `/s/[token]` route (Sprint P7-A scope, recommended defer), no `Prepare SOW Draft` unlock (Sprint P6-C scope at the END of P6-C). All five `LockedActionButton` instances remain unchanged from post-Sprint-P5 state (4 active for persisted UUID engagements: `Prepare Report` on roadmap + `Prepare SOW Draft` + `Send to Client` on proposal-workspace + `Export Report` on report mock paths; the Sprint P5 `Prepare Client Review` unlock and its mock-path fallback both stay in place). No email/CRM/e-sign/PDF binary/storage/Group-B wiring. **NO migration in P6-B** per `docs/26` § SOW Snapshot Model — the Sprint P2 migration `0015_proposal_delivery_snapshots.sql`'s `delivery_surface` CHECK constraint already accepts the `'sow_draft_candidate'` value, so SOW Draft snapshots land in the existing `proposal_delivery_snapshots` table with no schema change. **Type extension** in `lib/proposals/delivery-snapshot-types.ts` — `ProposalCommercialGuardResult.patternsApplied` and `.violations[].patternFamily` both gain a new `"sow-draft-finality"` family member; `delivery-snapshot-mappers.ts` defensive predicates updated to accept the new value (unknown family values fall through to the canon-safe default per the existing mapper pattern). **New `lib/proposals/sow-draft-eligibility.ts`** — pure `evaluateSowDraftEligibility(input)` returning `{eligible, reasons[], includedOptionIds, omittedOptionIds, omittedContent[], pricingReviewState, draftWatermark, sowDraftNotice: {pricingNotice, legalBoundaryNotice}}`. Returns ALL applicable reasons (not first-fail) with severity tags. **15 reason codes** matching `docs/26` § SOW Eligibility Rules verbatim: `engagement_not_persisted` / `source_snapshot_missing` / `source_snapshot_voided` / `source_surface_not_proposal_candidate` / `source_not_approved` / `source_guard_failed` / `no_included_options` / `selected_option_missing` / `group_b_content_blocked` / `sow_guard_failed` / `pricing_pending` (warning) / `implementation_credit_pending_approval` (warning) / `public_sow_not_authorized` (warning) / `reviewer_notes_excluded` (warning) / `unsupported_surface`. Warning-severity reasons do not block; they surface as operator-facing notes the future Sprint P6-C UI can render alongside an eligible verdict. Canon-derived `sowDraftNotice` strings (pricing-pending notice when `placeholder`, "Estimated · subject to final approval. Not a binding quote." when manually/workflow-approved; legal-boundary notice constant). **Extended `lib/proposals/commercial-guard.ts`** with **`SOW_DRAFT_FINALITY_PATTERNS`** — **26 new patterns** per `docs/26` § SOW Commercial Guard verbatim: `binding_agreement`, `executed_agreement`, `signature_block`, `sign_below`, `accepted_by`, `authorized_representative`, `payment_due`, `invoice_due`, `payment_terms_explicit` (single alternation pattern covering `net 30` / `net-30` / `due upon receipt` per canon's 26-distinct-phrase enumeration), `start_date_guaranteed`, `delivery_guaranteed`, `sla_guaranteed`, `liquidated_damages`, `termination_for_convenience`, `governing_law`, `indemnification`, `limitation_of_liability`, `warranty`, `auto_renewal`, `cancellation_fee`, `change_order_accepted`, `legally_binding_sow` (canon-explicit re-check of the proposal-side `legally_binding` pattern for SOW-specific family attribution; produces 2 violations on a single match, one per family, per the canon's intent), `statement_is_binding`, `this_sow_is_effective`, `work_shall_commence`, `client_hereby_agrees`. **New `runSowDraftCommercialGuard({optionSnapshot, sourceContextSnapshot, sowDraft, extras?})`** wrapper scans the combined **70-pattern set** (44 proposal base + 26 SOW; `COMBINED_RULES_WITH_SOW` constant) over: per-included-option fields (same shape as the proposal guard for byte-identical proposal-side behaviour) + proposal-level implementation-credit copy + SOW-specific structured fields (`sow.scopeStatement`, `sow.deliverables`, `sow.exclusions`, `sow.assumptions`, `sow.dependencies`, `sow.proposedTimeline`, `sow.responsibilities.client`, `sow.responsibilities.operator`, `sow.openQuestions`, `sow.pricingNotice`, `sow.legalBoundaryNotice`) + the existing Sprint P4 `extras.sowDraftText` + `extras.footerText` hooks. Returns `ProposalCommercialGuardResult` with `patternsApplied: ['financial', 'commercial-finality', 'roadmap-commitment', 'proposal-finality', 'sow-draft-finality']` and `patternCount: 70`. **Existing `runProposalCommercialGuard` for the proposal-candidate surface is UNCHANGED** — its `patternsApplied` still reports the four base families and its `patternCount` remains 44; proposal-candidate snapshots generated after Sprint P6-B have byte-identical commercial-guard scan behaviour. **New `lib/proposals/sow-draft-actions.ts`** — `generateSowDraftCandidateAction({engagementId, proposalId?, sourceProposalSnapshotId, selectedOptionIds?})` cookie-bound operator action. Validates UUIDs + auth + engagement existence. Loads source Proposal Candidate snapshot via direct `select(SNAPSHOT_SELECT)` + `mapProposalDeliverySnapshotRow`. Defense-in-depth gates BEFORE wasting cycles on guard / eligibility: `source.engagementId === engagementRow.id`, `source.status !== 'voided'`, `source.deliverySurface === 'client_proposal_candidate'`, `source.approvalState === 'approved'`, `args.proposalId` matches `source.proposalId` if passed. Resolves included-options set per canon (`selectedOptionIds[]` precedence; recommended-from-source fallback; all-included-from-source fallback if no recommended). Builds `sowOptionSnapshot` (every source option captured for audit; `includedInArtifact` flag per the SOW selection — may be a subset of the source's included options). Builds `sowDraft` jsonb under `source_context_snapshot.sowDraft`: `scopeStatement` (from first included option's `scopeSummary`), `deliverables` (deduped flat list across all included options), `exclusions: []` (SOW-specific, operator fills via future editor), `assumptions` (deduped flat), `dependencies` (deduped flat), `proposedTimeline` (from first included option's `timeline`), `responsibilities: {client: [], operator: []}` (SOW-specific, operator fills), `openQuestions: []`, `pricingNotice` + `legalBoundaryNotice` from canon-derived constants based on `pricing_review_state`. Runs commercial guard BEFORE eligibility (eligibility consumes guard's `passed` flag). On guard failure → logs `sow_draft_failed` with metadata `{proposalId, sourceProposalSnapshotId, failureReason: 'sow_commercial_guard_violation', violationCount}` (NEVER violation text — would smuggle banned phrases into the activity feed) + returns `{ok: false, error: 'commercial-guard-violation', violations: [{field, code}]}`. On eligibility blocker → logs `sow_draft_failed` with `failureReason: 'sow_eligibility_blocked'` + blockerCount. Happy path → inserts `proposal_delivery_snapshots` row with `status='candidate'`, `delivery_surface='sow_draft_candidate'`, `approval_state='unreviewed'`, `pricing_review_state` copied from source, `draft_watermark=true`, `proposal_status_at_generation` copied from source, prepended `PROPOSAL_GROUP_B_OMISSION_ENTRY` in `omitted_content` + per-option SOW-omission entries (`sow_option_not_selected` reason code for options the operator chose not to include); logs `sow_draft_generated` activity event with sanitized metadata `{proposalId, sourceProposalSnapshotId, deliverySurface, draftWatermark, approvalState, pricingReviewState, includedOptionCount, omittedOptionCount}`; revalidates proposal + engagement detail routes; returns `{ok: true, snapshotId, deliverySurface: 'sow_draft_candidate', draftWatermark, approvalState: 'unreviewed', pricingReviewState, includedOptionCount, omittedOptionCount}`. **3 new activity event types** added to `lib/activity/types.ts` (`sow_draft_generated` tone `success`, `sow_draft_failed` tone `risk`, `sow_draft_voided` tone `neutral`); timeline labels added to `components/activity/activity-timeline.tsx` (`SOW Draft generated` / `SOW Draft generation failed` / `SOW Draft voided`). The new entity type continues to be `proposal_delivery_snapshot` (same table; no new entity type needed since SOW Draft snapshots live in the existing table). Sprint P6-B emits `sow_draft_generated` + `sow_draft_failed` from the action; `sow_draft_voided` is reserved for the existing `voidProposalDeliverySnapshotAction` to emit when the operator voids a `sow_draft_candidate` row in Sprint P6-C (the action filters by id not surface, so it transparently handles SOW snapshots — but the event type should swap from `proposal_snapshot_voided` to `sow_draft_voided` based on the row's `delivery_surface` in Sprint P6-C; deferred to P6-C). **No UI mounted** in Sprint P6-B — the action is callable from server-action contexts only; Sprint P6-C will wire it into the proposal page's new `<PastSowDraftsPanel>`. **No public route** — `app/s/` and `app/sow/` directories do not exist (`ls app/` confirms only `app/p/` from Sprint P5). **No share token table** — `sow_share_tokens` is Sprint P7-A scope IF approved. **Live validation deferred to Sprint P6-C** per the Sprint P2 → P3 precedent — no safe operator-mediated invocation path without a UI button. The action is statically verified: all 4 modules type-check + lint clean; the new TS types compose correctly with the existing `proposal_delivery_snapshots` jsonb mappers; the SOW guard's pattern union math (44 + 26 = 70) is exact. `npm run lint` clean; `NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 npm run build` clean. **28 app routes** (unchanged from post-Sprint-P5); every route's First Load JS byte-identical to the Sprint P5 baseline (no client-bundle delta because the new modules are server-only). **No package dependency.** Group-B grep clean (confined to the canonical 4 files: `components/charts/exhibits/{benchmark-comparison-bars,ai-savings-waterfall,roi-bridge}.tsx` + `app/app/charts-preview/page.tsx`; the prohibition strings inside `commercial-guard.ts` are `RegExp` patterns — the canonical allowed shape, same as `lib/ai/claim-guard.ts`'s existing 26 patterns). All 5 LockedActionButton sites unchanged. No middleware / `next.config.mjs` / API route / public route / storage / PDF library / AI synthesis change. **Backlog carried forward to Sprint P6-C**: live `generateSowDraftCandidateAction` walkthrough via the new UI button against a fresh approved Proposal Candidate snapshot; the swap of `voidProposalDeliverySnapshotAction`'s emitted event type from `proposal_snapshot_voided` → `sow_draft_voided` when the row's `delivery_surface='sow_draft_candidate'` (Sprint P6-C decision — could alternatively emit BOTH events for audit clarity); optional `approveSowDraftSnapshotAction` if SOW-side approval ships as a separate state per `docs/26` § Open Decisions item 8; SOW share-token cascade-revoke wiring (Sprint P4's cascade is proposal-token-specific; SOW share tokens are Sprint P7-A scope and would need their own cascade helper). **Recommended next sprint: Sprint P6-C — internal SOW Draft route + `<PastSowDraftsPanel>` + Generate / Open / Void controls + Prepare SOW Draft unlock at end** per `docs/26` § Prepare SOW Draft Unlock Policy. **`Send to Client`, `Prepare SOW Draft`, `Prepare Report`, mock-route `Export Report` all remain locked throughout Sprint P6-C until the unlock at the end.**

## Phase 1B SOW Draft Canon — Sprint P6-A (2026-05-17)

`docs/26_PHASE_1B_SOW_DRAFT_CANON.md` is the canonical governance doc for moving from the operator-mediated Proposal Review Link surface (Sprint P5) toward an internal SOW Draft artifact. **Canon-only sprint.** No source code, schema, migration, API route, package dep, public route, storage bucket, PDF library, email pipeline, CRM integration, SOW snapshot generator, SOW renderer, SOW share token, or e-sign integration was created or modified. `Prepare SOW Draft`, `Send to Client`, `Prepare Report`, and mock-route `Export Report` remain locked verbatim. `Prepare Client Review` unlock from Sprint P5 (in-page anchor to the Past Proposal Candidates panel) is unchanged. **Canon defines**: the SOW Draft artifact definition (internal operator-generated implementation-scope artifact based on an approved Proposal Candidate; must include draft label + non-binding markings + selected option(s) + scope summary + deliverables + exclusions + assumptions + dependencies + proposed timeline + client/operator responsibilities + open commercial questions + pricing-state notice + approval/signature boundary; must NOT include final binding quote / payment terms / signature blocks / acceptance buttons / legally binding language / automatic start date / guaranteed delivery date / e-signature language / final SLA-legal clauses / Group-B claims); **15 SOW Eligibility Rules** (persisted UUID + proposal exists + non-voided Proposal Candidate + Proposal Candidate `approval_state='approved'` + Proposal Candidate `commercial_guard_result.passed=true` + delivery_surface client_proposal_candidate + pricing_review_state any of the three + included options non-empty + no voided backing snapshot + no stale share-token dependency + no Group-B + SOW guard passes + no e-sign language + no final payment terms + no raw reviewer notes); **pricing/terms policy** (MVP default pricing hidden; `placeholder` state must surface the canon-mandated pending notice; no fixed price unless approved workflow exists; no taxes / payment due dates / auto-renewal / cancellation / late fees / refund / legal terms in the SOW Draft surface; implementation credit hidden until commercial-approval workflow exists); **SOW commercial guard policy** (extends the existing 44-pattern proposal commercial guard with **26 new SOW-finality patterns** — `binding agreement` / `executed agreement` / `signature block` / `sign below` / `accepted by` / `authorized representative` / `payment due` / `invoice due` / `net 30` variants / `start date guaranteed` / `delivery guaranteed` / `SLA guaranteed` / `liquidated damages` / `termination for convenience` / `governing law` / `indemnification` / `limitation of liability` / `warranty` / `auto-renewal` / `cancellation fee` / `change order accepted` / `legally binding` / `statement is binding` / `this SOW is effective` / `work shall commence` / `client hereby agrees` — total 70 patterns scanned at SOW generation time; activity metadata never carries violation text; client artifact never exposes guard internals; export-time scan required); **required markings / disclaimers** (mandatory "Draft SOW · not executed" header on every page distinct from Proposal Candidate's banner; mandatory body note "This draft is for review and planning only. It is not binding until reviewed, approved, and executed by authorized parties."; mandatory footer "Not a contract. Not an executed SOW. Not a binding quote. Not authorization to begin work. Final scope, pricing, timeline, and terms require written approval."; conditional pricing-state notice "Pricing is pending manual review and is intentionally omitted from this draft." OR "Estimated · subject to final approval. Not a binding quote." depending on `pricing_review_state`; operator-only internal banner "Operator-only SOW Draft · NOT SENT BY SLATE · No client delivery" on the internal route only); **SOW snapshot model** — recommended **reuse of `proposal_delivery_snapshots`** with `delivery_surface='sow_draft_candidate'` (Sprint P2 schema designed for this; the CHECK already accepts the value); SOW-specific content lands under `source_context_snapshot.sowDraft` (extensible jsonb shape with `scopeStatement` / `deliverables` / `exclusions` / `assumptions` / `dependencies` / `proposedTimeline` / `responsibilities: {client[], operator[]}` / `openQuestions` / `pricingNotice` / `legalBoundaryNotice` / `sourceProposalSnapshotId` / `sourceProposalReviewTokenId?` / `generatedAt`); no migration in P6 unless an implementation gap is discovered; **SOW internal route** at recommended path `/app/engagements/[id]/proposal/sow/[snapshotId]` (operator-only, `/app/*`-gated, snapshot-pure render via new `<SowDraftDocument>` extending Sprint P3's `<ProposalCandidateDocument>` patterns with SOW-specific chrome, `dynamic="force-dynamic"` + `revalidate=0` + `fetchCache="force-no-store"` defeating Next 14 fetch cache, voided banner, no public access, no e-signature controls, no Send to Client, no PDF binary, print-friendly light surface via `.slate-print-light`); **`Prepare SOW Draft` Unlock Policy** (11 prerequisites; earliest unlock at END of Sprint P6-C; non-send unlock — replaces the `LockedActionButton` with a primary in-page anchor labeled `Generate SOW Draft` pointing at a new `<PastSowDraftsPanel>` mirror of `<ProposalCandidatesPanel>`; mock paths keep the locked button); **SOW share route policy** (NOT part of Sprint P6 — Sprint P7-A decision; if approved, recommended URL `/s/[token]` with separate `sow_share_tokens` table mirroring the report-vs-proposal separation rationale; stricter public disclaimers; e-signature still separate; recommended default: **defer** until after Sprint P6-D acceptance audit); **Send to Client policy** (remains locked through Sprint P6 + P7; Sprint P8 earliest with separate channel canon — recommended doc number `docs/27_PHASE_1B_SLATE_DELIVERY_CHANNEL_CANON.md`; first version operator-mediated copy-link); future activity event types defined but not implemented (`sow_draft_generated`/`_failed`/`_voided`, optional `sow_draft_approved`, optional Sprint P7 `sow_share_token_*`, optional Sprint P8 `sow_sent_to_client`); **proposed sprint sequence** P6-A (canon, this sprint) → P6-B (SOW foundation: eligibility evaluator + commercial guard extension + `generateSowDraftCandidateAction` + 3 new activity event types; **no migration**) → P6-C (internal SOW route + `<PastSowDraftsPanel>` + Generate/Open/Void controls + optional `approveSowDraftSnapshotAction`; **`Prepare SOW Draft` unlocks at end**) → **audit gate** (SOW Draft MVP Acceptance Audit) → P7-A (SOW share route canon OR implementation decision; recommended defer) → P8 (optional Send to Client unlock after separate channel canon); parallel non-blocker work: production hardening backlog from `docs/23` + `docs/25` (pepper config + access debounce + dev short-expiry affordance + audience-label UI + revoke-from-panel UI + engagement-title fallback investigation + Sprint P4 copy-once chip wording fix) applies to both report + proposal lanes and runs independently; Benchmark Gate 1 / Financial Gate 1 advancement remains separate; **12 open decisions** enumerated with committed recommended defaults — reuse `proposal_delivery_snapshots` (no migration); `/s/[token]` for any future public SOW route; pricing hidden when placeholder; implementation credit hidden; client/operator responsibilities included with structured shape; change-order block omitted (single footer line covers); `approval_state='approved'` required for SOW generation; SOW approval state separate from Proposal Candidate approval; generate from operator-selected with recommended-option default; legal terms omitted with single boundary notice; public SOW share deferred to P7-A operator decision; e-signature provider undecided and out of Phase 1B scope. **Sprint P6-A authorises only Sprint P6-B's scope if accepted; nothing else.** Acceptance criteria: 16 conditions met (docs/26 exists with every required section, SOW Draft definition clear, eligibility rules defined, pricing/terms policy defined, commercial guard policy defined, disclaimer copy defined, snapshot model recommendation defined, internal route requirements defined, Prepare SOW Draft unlock policy defined, SOW share route policy defined, Send to Client policy defined, activity events defined, sprint sequence defined, open decisions enumerated with defaults, docs/08 + docs/10 updated, no source/schema/API/package/middleware/next.config/public/storage/PDF/email/CRM/e-sign changes). `git status --short` after this sprint: only `docs/08`, `docs/10`, `docs/26` modified (`docs/26` added). **No source code touched.** Visx plan file remains parked. Existing report-link + proposal-link routes untouched. The recommended next sprint conditional on operator approval is **Sprint P6-B — SOW Draft foundation** (`lib/proposals/sow-draft-eligibility.ts` + commercial-guard extension for `SOW_DRAFT_FINALITY_PATTERNS` + `generateSowDraftCandidateAction` + 3 new activity event types; operator-only with NO public route, NO `Prepare SOW Draft` unlock, NO email/CRM, NO PDF binary, NO Group-B wiring, NO migration).
