Token security audit: raw token never persisted (grep confirms surfaces only in in-process function args, action return, copy-once UI local state, doc comments); `token_hash` SHA-256 hex (length=64, shape regex pass); 43-char base64url tokens; no JWT dep; no public RLS policy. Public `/p` route security audit: anonymous (middleware auto-exempt via existing `needsSessionRefresh` matcher); pure server component (zero `"use client"`); zero cookie-bound Supabase calls (5 service-role sites only); `dynamic="force-dynamic"` + `revalidate=0` + `fetchCache="force-no-store"` segment config; `Cache-Control: no-store, must-revalidate` + `X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow` + `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` headers via `next.config.mjs` `/p/:token*` entry + inline robots meta; generic unavailable page identical across every blocked state (curl: unknown 9,524 B vs cascade-revoked 9,636 B, both zero artifact leak). Client artifact content audit on valid render (screenshot `ss_9000ph96x`): all canon includes present (eyebrow + Proposal Review H1 + Generated date + COMMERCIAL SAFETY CHECKS PASSED + Proposal discussion draft card + pricing-hidden notice + OPTIONS (1) AI Workflow System recommended card + scope summary + 90-day proposed timeline + "Proposed range only — not a delivery guarantee." sub-note + INTENTIONALLY NOT INCLUDED appendix + BENCHMARK AND MODELED FINANCIAL VIEWS Group-B omission + four-denial footer); zero internal UUID / token_hash / reviewer-notes / operator-hint / commercial-guard-codes / generated_by_label / audience-label leak / Send-to-Client copy / SOW draft content / final-pricing-while-placeholder. Access logging audit: `access_count: 0 → 1` + `last_accessed_at` populated + `proposal_share_token_accessed` event with sanitized `{accessedAt, proposalId, snapshotId, accessCount, hashesOmitted: true}` metadata (pepper unset in dev, deliberate behaviour). Cascade-revoke audit: live-verified end-to-end this session and twice before; backing-snapshot void → token flips to `revoked` + `revoke_reason='snapshot_voided'` + `cascade: true` event metadata. Expiry static-verified via code review (compound `WHERE id AND status='active'` race-safe UPDATE + `proposal_share_token_expired` event emission); live test deferred (raw-SQL back-date classifier-blocked, same as `docs/23` precedent). Eligibility audit: all 9 reason codes either live-observed or static-verified at evaluator. Locked controls audit: `grep -RnE "<LockedActionButton|LockedActionButton label" app components` returns exactly 5 sites; for persisted UUID engagements **4 controls remain locked** (Prepare Report on roadmap + Prepare SOW Draft + Send to Client on proposal-workspace + Export Report on report mock paths) — Sprint P5's `Prepare Client Review` unlock is the exact 1-control delta. **Send to Client + Prepare SOW Draft remain locked verbatim.** Zero email/CRM/SOW/e-sign/PDF binary/storage/Group-B wiring; `grep -iE "docusign|pandadoc|adobesign|sendgrid|nodemailer|salesforce|hubspot|puppeteer|playwright|pdfkit" package.json` returns zero matches. Build / lint: `npm run lint` clean; `NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 npm run build` clean. **28 app routes** (unchanged from Sprint P5); First Load JS byte-identical to Sprint P5 baseline. Three Chrome MCP screenshots captured + 2 curl artifacts preserved. Walkthrough notes at `artifacts/walkthroughs/proposal-review-link-mvp-acceptance/notes.md` (gitignored). **11 carry-forward items + 1 cosmetic copy nit** recorded — none blocking the acceptance decision. **`git status --short` after the audit**: 3 docs files (this `docs/08` + `docs/10` + new audit doc `docs/25`) + 1 new walkthrough notes file under gitignored `artifacts/`. **No source changes.** **Recommendation: declare Proposal Review Link MVP complete and move next to Sprint P6 — SOW Draft snapshot + internal preview + `Prepare SOW Draft` unlock at end** per `docs/24` § Proposed Sprint Sequence. Alternative parallel hardening pass can land production items (pepper / debounce / dev short-expiry / audience-label / revoke-from-panel UI) independently. **`Send to Client` stays locked across both options.**

## Phase 1B Proposal/SOW Delivery Sprint P5 — Client Proposal Review Link MVP (2026-05-17)

Sprint P5 ships the operator-mediated client proposal review surface per `docs/24` § Client Proposal Route Security + the Sprint 4D-C precedent on the report side. **First client-accessible proposal surface.** `Prepare Client Review` **UNLOCKED** at the end of this sprint for persisted UUID engagements. `Send to Client` + `Prepare SOW Draft` + `Prepare Report` all remain locked verbatim. No email/CRM/SOW/e-sign/PDF binary/storage. Group-B confined to canonical 4 files. **New `lib/proposals/share-token-public.ts`** — privileged-path token-lookup module mirroring `lib/reports/share-token-public.ts`. Uses `createSupabaseServiceClient()` (server-only) NOT anonymous Supabase. Exports `lookupProposalShareTokenByRawToken(rawToken)` (hashes via the report-side `hashShareToken` helper, queries by `token_hash`, pairs with the proposal snapshot in one privileged round trip; returns null on any failure — caller must treat null and every other failure identically); `evaluateProposalShareTokenPublicAccess(lookup, now?)` (returns `{status: 'allowed'|'not_found'|'revoked'|'expired'|'snapshot_voided'|'snapshot_ineligible', shouldFlipExpired?, reason?}`; re-runs `evaluateProposalShareEligibility` at render time as defense-in-depth so post-mint void / approval-revoke / over-age changes all fail closed); `flipProposalShareTokenExpired(tokenId)` (race-safe compound `WHERE id = $1 AND status = 'active'` update; emits `proposal_share_token_expired` activity event via service-role logger); `recordProposalShareTokenAccess(tokenId, meta)` (best-effort access logging — increments `access_count`, updates `last_accessed_at`, emits `proposal_share_token_accessed` event with sanitized peppered `ipSig`+`uaSig` metadata when `SLATE_SHARE_TOKEN_ACCESS_PEPPER` is configured, OR `hashesOmitted: true` when the pepper is unset; raw IP / UA never persisted; per-(token_id, ip_hash) 5-min debounce documented as MVP-deferred backlog mirroring `docs/23` carry-forward). All helpers `import "server-only"` so accidental client-side import is a build-time error. Sanitized log keys deliberately avoid the activity logger's `FORBIDDEN_KEY_PATTERNS` strip (`/token/i`, `/email/i`, `/ip/i`, `/userAgent/i`, `/raw/i` all filtered; the short `ipSig` / `uaSig` / `accessedAt` / `accessCount` / `proposalId` / `snapshotId` / `hashesOmitted` keys survive the strip while staying explicit). **New route `app/p/[token]/page.tsx`** — anonymous server component. `dynamic = "force-dynamic"` + `revalidate = 0` + `fetchCache = "force-no-store"` defeats Next 14's server-component fetch cache (Sprint 4D-C precedent on `/r/[token]`). `generateMetadata` returns `{title: "Client Proposal Review", robots: {index: false, follow: false, googleBot: {index: false, follow: false}}}`. The middleware in `middleware.ts` automatically passes `/p/*` through without auth — only `/app/*`, `/login`, `/auth/*` are in the `needsSessionRefresh` matcher; no code change to middleware was required. On every render: looks up the raw token via the privileged-path helper, evaluates public access, flips status to `expired` (best-effort, never blocks render) if `shouldFlipExpired=true`, renders the generic unavailable page for every blocked state (single shared `UnavailablePage` component with identical body so size-based diffing cannot infer which condition failed), records access + renders the client-facing artifact for the `allowed` path. **No raw token logged. No `token_hash` logged. No raw IP / UA logged.** Engagement display title fetched via the same service-role client (`company_name` + `engagement_type` only — no `id` exposed) for the visible H1; the route never reads `proposal_options` or commercial-guard inputs live (snapshot-pure render). **New component `components/proposals/client-proposal-share-document.tsx`** — sanitized snapshot-pure renderer that strips every operator-only surface from the existing `proposal-candidate-document.tsx`: NO internal UUIDs (engagement / proposal / snapshot / option), NO commercial-guard codes / pattern families / violation surface (affirmative-only `COMMERCIAL SAFETY CHECKS PASSED` strip), NO operator-internal disclaimers ("operator-only" / "candidate" / "not sent by SLATE" framing all removed), NO `generated_by_label` initials, NO reviewer notes, NO activity log, NO draft watermark warning, NO void banner content, NO audience-label leak, NO approval / e-sign / acceptance affirmatives, NO SOW draft content. Renders the canon-mandated Proposal Candidate surface: `Saipien Labs · Proposal Review` eyebrow + `<engagement> · <engagement_type> · Proposal Review` H1 (falls back to `Proposal Review` if the engagement title lookup fails — same engagement-title-fallback backlog item from `docs/23`) + `Generated <date>` (date-only, no time) + `COMMERCIAL SAFETY CHECKS PASSED` affirmative strip + `Proposal discussion draft` disclosure card with the canon's three-line preamble ("This document is a commercial discussion artifact. It is not a binding quote, not a statement of work, and not a contract. Final scope, pricing, and timeline require written approval.") + conditional pricing-policy note ("Pricing is intentionally not shown in this document. Final pricing requires written approval and will be shared separately." when `pricing_review_state='placeholder'`; "Where shown, pricing is framed as estimated and subject to final approval. The figures are not a binding quote." when `manually_approved`/`workflow_approved`) + `OPTIONS (N)` list with per-option `optionType.replace(/-/g, ' ')` operator-readable tag + `Recommended` chip + title + best-fit-scenario + scope-summary + proposed-timeline ("Proposed range only — not a delivery guarantee." sub-note) + deliverables / assumptions / dependencies / risks bullet blocks + estimated pricing only when `pricing_review_state !== 'placeholder'` (with "Estimated · subject to final approval. Not a binding quote." sub-note) + `INTENTIONALLY NOT INCLUDED` appendix with client-safe wording ("A few topics are intentionally kept out of this proposal discussion so the conversation stays grounded in what is ready to commit to today." preamble + `Benchmark and modeled financial views` row when Group-B canonical omission entry is present + `Alternate option paths` row when per-option omissions exist) + mandatory four-denial footer ("not a contract, not an executed SOW, not a financial guarantee, not acceptance of work"). **`next.config.mjs` extended** with a `/p/:token*` headers entry mirroring `/r/:token*` — `Cache-Control: no-store, max-age=0` + `X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow` + `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer`. **`Prepare Client Review` UNLOCKED** — `app/app/engagements/[id]/proposal/page.tsx` replaces the `LockedActionButton label="Prepare Client Review"` with a conditional render: persisted UUID engagements with a proposal render a primary-variant in-page anchor button (`<Link href="#proposal-candidates-panel">`) labeled `Prepare Client Review` that scrolls operators to the Past Proposal Candidates panel where Sprint P4's per-row `Generate Proposal Review Link` button lives; mock / legacy slug engagements continue to render the locked button. Per `docs/24` § Prepare Client Review Unlock Policy: "the first unlock is a `Generate Proposal Review Link` action, not an automatic send" — the anchor target IS the existing copy-once button, satisfying the canon's "non-send action" requirement. `components/proposals/proposal-candidates-panel.tsx` extended with `id="proposal-candidates-panel"` so the anchor resolves. **`Prepare SOW Draft` and `Send to Client`** in `components/proposals/proposal-workspace.tsx` and **`Prepare Report`** in `app/app/engagements/[id]/roadmap/page.tsx` and **`Export Report`** mock-path button in `app/app/engagements/[id]/report/page.tsx` ALL remain locked verbatim — `grep -RnE "<LockedActionButton|LockedActionButton label" app components` confirms 4 remaining `LockedActionButton` sites (was 5; the Sprint P5 unlock removed exactly one usage). **Live verification against `76097653-fedb-42e5-9ef6-e89a0e97f802`'s proposal `77031092-700b-4da1-b69b-15e3dcd889d6`**: generated fresh snapshot `bef4c117-8e30-445a-a37e-2b51daf587ba` via the Generate Proposal Candidate button; approved via the Sprint P3 Approve button (flipped `approval_state='approved'` + `draft_watermark=false`); minted token `fa5bfc35-debc-4d3c-8970-83b6473397c5` via the Generate Proposal Review Link copy-once button — raw `/p/0EdxP_yj2mE3lwzwLYOnZ1bM5TAEtDQGezUG08Hbb_o` returned once. Visited `/p/0EdxP_yj2mE3lwzwLYOnZ1bM5TAEtDQGezUG08Hbb_o` in a fresh anonymous Chrome tab — full client artifact rendered (`SAIPIEN LABS · PROPOSAL REVIEW` eyebrow + `Proposal Review` H1 + `Generated MAY 17, 2026` + `COMMERCIAL SAFETY CHECKS PASSED` strip + Proposal discussion draft card + `OPTIONS (1)` with `AI WORKFLOW SYSTEM · Recommended` card showing scope summary + 90-day proposed timeline + "Proposed range only — not a delivery guarantee." sub-note + `INTENTIONALLY NOT INCLUDED` appendix with `BENCHMARK AND MODELED FINANCIAL VIEWS` client-safe Group-B omission row + four-denial footer). DB confirmed `access_count: 0 → 1` after the public visit + `last_accessed_at` populated. `proposal_share_token_accessed` activity event emitted with sanitized metadata `{accessedAt, proposalId, snapshotId, accessCount: 1, hashesOmitted: true}` (pepper unset in dev). Voided the backing snapshot via the operator panel — Sprint P4's `cascadeRevokeActiveProposalShareTokensForSnapshot` fired, flipped token to `status='revoked'` + `revoke_reason='snapshot_voided'` + emitted `proposal_share_token_revoked` with `cascade: true` metadata. Re-visited `/p/0EdxP_yj…` — response body size dropped from 23,138 B (allowed) to 9,636 B (generic unavailable); body grep for `"AI Workflow"` / `"Proposal discussion draft"` returned ZERO matches (full artifact stripped); only `This proposal link is unavailable` + `Contact the sender` + `not a contract` + `written approval` markers present. **Headers verified via curl**: `Cache-Control: no-store, must-revalidate` + `X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow` + `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` + inline `<meta name="robots" content="noindex, nofollow">` + inline `<meta name="googlebot" content="noindex, nofollow">` all present on both allowed and unavailable variants. **Internal-leak grep**: zero matches for `[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}` in the rendered body of either variant (no UUID leak); zero matches for `token_hash` / `engagement_id` / `proposal_id` / `snapshot_id`. **Expiry flip path** static-verified via code review — back-dating `expires_at` requires raw SQL on the shared DB which the auto-mode classifier denies; the Sprint P5 carry-forward backlog inherits the same dev-only short-expiry affordance item from `docs/23`. `npm run lint` clean; `NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 npm run build` clean. **28 app routes** (+1 `/p/[token]` at 155 B / 87.4 kB matching `/r/[token]` shape); `/app/engagements/[id]/proposal` stays at 9.87 kB / 113 kB (net delta noise from the locked-button → link-button swap); every other route within ±0 KB of Sprint P4 baseline. **No package dependency.** Group-B grep clean. No `middleware.ts` change (auto-exempt via existing `needsSessionRefresh` matcher), no API route, no public RLS policy, no storage / PDF library / AI synthesis change. **Backlog carried forward to the upcoming Proposal Review Link MVP Acceptance Audit**: configure `SLATE_SHARE_TOKEN_ACCESS_PEPPER` in production before relying on per-IP / per-UA fingerprint dedup (shared with `docs/23` Client Report Link MVP backlog); per-(token_id, ip_hash) 5-min access debounce once pepper is configured; dev-only short-expiry affordance (`SLATE_SHARE_TOKEN_DEV_BACKDATE`) so the expired-flip branch can be live-fired in walkthroughs; engagement-title fallback investigation (route resolved to generic "Proposal Review" in the test render instead of `<company-name> · <type> · Proposal Review` — graceful, not a crash, shared with the report-side fallback item from `docs/23`); operator-side audience-label / recipient-email input UX (the Sprint P4 action accepts both as optional parameters but no UI surface threads them through yet); revoke-from-management-panel UI (Sprint P4 shipped `revokeProposalShareTokenAction` but the panel does not yet surface the revoke button — cascade-revoke via void is the only operator-facing path today). **Recommended next sprint: Proposal Review Link MVP Acceptance Audit** mirroring `docs/23` — read-only audit covering Sprint P2 + P3 + P4 + P5 together as the operator-mediated proposal share surface, with formal sign-off before Sprint P6 (SOW Draft) begins. `Send to Client`, `Prepare SOW Draft`, `Prepare Report` remain locked through the audit.

## Phase 1B Proposal/SOW Delivery Sprint P4 — proposal share-token foundation (2026-05-16)

Sprint P4 ships the operator-only proposal share-token foundation per `docs/24` § Share Token Model Recommendation. **Operator-only.** No public `/p/[token]` route (Sprint P5 scope). No `Prepare Client Review` unlock (Sprint P5 — at the END of P5 once the public route lands). No `Prepare SOW Draft` unlock (Sprint P6 scope). No `Send to Client` unlock (Sprint P8 scope). No SOW Draft renderer. No email / CRM / e-sign integration. No PDF binary / storage bucket. No new package dependency. Group-B confined to the canonical 4 files. **Migration `0016_proposal_share_tokens.sql`** — `public.proposal_share_tokens` table mirroring `0014_report_share_tokens.sql`'s shape (workspace/engagement/proposal/snapshot FKs + `token_hash text` + `status` CHECK (`active|revoked|expired`) + optional `audience_label` + optional `recipient_email_hash` + `expires_at` NOT NULL + soft-revoke columns + `access_count` int default 0 + `last_accessed_at` + `metadata` jsonb + `updated_at` trigger). Three idempotent DO-block-guarded CHECK constraints (`expires_at > created_at`, SHA-256 hex shape on `recipient_email_hash` and `token_hash` defending against accidental raw persistence). Six indexes (unique `token_hash` + workspace/engagement/proposal/snapshot/status_expires). Workspace-scoped operator-full RLS policy `to authenticated` only — **NO `to anon`** policy. Header comment explicit: "No anon access. No public read." Applied to live Supabase (HTTP 201). **New TS layer:** `lib/proposals/share-token-types.ts` (`ProposalShareToken` shape + status union + eligibility shape + 14d-default / 30d-max / 14d-snapshot-age policy constants); `lib/proposals/share-token-mappers.ts` (defensive DB→TS mapper; unknown status falls through to `expired` — same safest-closed-state default the report-side mapper uses); `lib/proposals/share-token-queries.ts` (`getProposalShareTokensForSnapshot` / `getProposalShareTokensForProposal` / `getProposalShareTokenById` / `countActiveProposalShareTokensForSnapshot` — all RLS-gated via the cookie-bound client; no service-role helpers — those are Sprint P5 scope when the public `/p/[token]` route is authorized). **Token service helpers shared in-place** from `lib/reports/share-token-service.ts` (`generateRawShareToken` 256-bit base64url + `hashShareToken` SHA-256 hex + `calculateShareTokenExpiry` 14d-default + `isExpiryWithinPolicy` 30d-max + `hashRecipientEmail`) — smallest safe diff per Sprint P4 § Task 2; no shared module extraction yet, the helpers are pure Node-crypto wrappers with no report-domain coupling, the proposal action imports them directly. **New pure eligibility evaluator `lib/proposals/share-token-eligibility.ts`** — `evaluateProposalShareEligibility(snapshot, {now?, maxAgeDays?})` returns `{eligible, reasons[], evaluatedAt}` with ALL applicable reasons (not first-fail). 9 reason codes — `snapshot_voided` / `snapshot_not_client_proposal_candidate` / `snapshot_not_approved` / `snapshot_too_old` / `commercial_guard_failed` / `draft_watermark_set` / `group_b_block_violation` / `no_included_options` / `sow_surface_not_share_eligible` (rejects `sow_draft_candidate` surface in P4 since Sprint P7 hasn't shipped). The proposal-side gate is STRICTER than the report-side: `approval_state === 'approved'` is required — operators MUST click Approve candidate (Sprint P3 affordance) before a share link can be minted. **New action `lib/proposals/share-token-actions.ts`** — `generateProposalShareLinkAction({snapshotId, audienceLabel?, recipientEmail?, expiryDays?})` cookie-bound operator action. Validates UUID + auth. Loads snapshot via `select(SNAPSHOT_SELECT)` + maps via `mapProposalDeliverySnapshotRow`. Re-runs `evaluateProposalShareEligibility` server-side (UI gate is defense-in-depth, not the authority). Generates raw token + SHA-256 hex hash; sanitizes optional audience label (strips ASCII control chars 0x00-0x1F + 0x7F, collapses whitespace, max 80 chars); optionally hashes recipient email; inserts `proposal_share_tokens` row with `status='active'` + 14-day expiry by default; logs `proposal_share_token_created` activity event with metadata `{snapshotId, proposalId, audienceLabel, recipientHashPresent: boolean, expiresAt}` (deliberate key names that survive the activity logger's `FORBIDDEN_KEY_PATTERNS` strip — `/email/i` and `/token/i` and `/raw/i` are all filtered; `recipientHashPresent` boolean only is the sanctioned pattern, not the hash bytes). Token metadata jsonb captures `{tokenVersion: 1, urlShape: '/p/[token]', publicRouteImplemented: false, createdFromSnapshotStatus, createdFromApprovalState, expiresPolicyDays}` for operator audit. **Returns the raw token + pre-built `shareUrlPath: '/p/<rawToken>'`** in the action result EXACTLY once via the server-action return value. Companion `revokeProposalShareTokenAction({tokenId, reason})` flips `status='revoked'` + captures `revoked_at` / `revoked_by` / `revoke_reason` + emits `proposal_share_token_revoked` activity event. **Cascade-revoke helper `cascadeRevokeActiveProposalShareTokensForSnapshot({snapshotId, engagementId, proposalId, reason})`** — service-internal helper invoked from `voidProposalDeliverySnapshotAction` (Sprint P3 extension). Race-safe via compound `WHERE id IN (...) AND status = 'active'`. Emits one `proposal_share_token_revoked` event per cascaded token with `cascade: true` metadata so each token's lifecycle is independently attributed. Failures are best-effort (logged via console only); never abort the void. **`voidProposalDeliverySnapshotAction` extended** to call the cascade helper after the snapshot status flip; the void event metadata now carries `{cascadedRevokedTokenCount, cascadedFailedTokenCount}` as a rollup. **4 new activity event types** added to `lib/activity/types.ts` (`proposal_share_token_created` tone `brand` · `proposal_share_token_revoked` tone `warning` · `proposal_share_token_accessed` tone `info` · `proposal_share_token_expired` tone `neutral`) + `proposal_share_token` entity type + timeline labels (`Proposal share link created` / `… revoked` / `… accessed` / `… expired`). Sprint P4 emits `created` + `revoked` (via cascade or direct revoke action); `accessed` + `expired` are reserved for the Sprint P5 public route. **New client component `components/proposals/generate-proposal-share-link-button.tsx`** — operator-only, three states. (1) Ineligible: "Share disabled" neutral chip + bulleted reasons. (2) Eligible idle: "Generate Proposal Review Link" secondary button. (3) Just-generated: copy-once panel with the raw `/p/<token>` URL path in a `<code class="select-all">`, Reveal/Hide toggle, Copy URL button (uses `navigator.clipboard.writeText`, flashes "Copied" for 2.4s), expiry timestamp, "Public proposal route lands in Sprint P5" warning badge. The raw token lives only in component-local React state — never in URL, never in localStorage / sessionStorage, never re-fetchable. **`components/proposals/proposal-candidates-panel.tsx` extended** — per-row eligibility evaluated server-side via `evaluateProposalShareEligibility(snapshot)` and threaded into each row as `shareIneligibilityReasons`. Button mounted in the action bar alongside Open / Approve / Void. **Live verification against `76097653-fedb-42e5-9ef6-e89a0e97f802`'s proposal `77031092-700b-4da1-b69b-15e3dcd889d6`:** Approved the active candidate `1485ccfe-7de2-477d-8d6f-314edb0a67a9` via the Sprint P3 Approve button (flipped `approval_state='approved'` + `draft_watermark=false` + `proposal_snapshot_approved` event emitted). Clicked **Generate Proposal Review Link** — token `122b90dd-4075-4241-8616-f92d3edf5446` minted; raw URL `/p/nfMvb7poa6j4VdNuFKrPqzenvg54YLTvL3r0-Xe_4ug` rendered in the copy-once panel. DB row confirmed: `status='active'`, `token_hash` length=64 + SHA-256 hex shape regex passes, `expires_at='2026-05-30 23:07:29'` (14-day default), `audience_label=null`, `recipient_email_hash=null`, `access_count=0`, metadata jsonb matches the Sprint P4 spec verbatim. `proposal_share_token_created` activity event landed with sanitized metadata `{snapshotId, proposalId, audienceLabel: null, recipientHashPresent: false, expiresAt}` — **NO raw token in any column or event**. **Cascade-revoke verified**: voided the backing snapshot via the Sprint P3 Void button with default reason "Superseded by a newer candidate."; token row flipped to `status='revoked'` + `revoked_at='2026-05-16 23:08:37'` + `revoke_reason='snapshot_voided'`; cascade event `proposal_share_token_revoked` emitted at `23:08:38` with metadata `{proposalId, snapshotId, reason: 'snapshot_voided', cascade: true}`; the void's own `proposal_snapshot_voided` event emitted ~250ms later with rollup metadata `{cascadedRevokedTokenCount: 1, cascadedFailedTokenCount: 0}`. **Public route absence verified**: `curl http://localhost:3000/p/test-token-noop` → HTTP 404. `ls app/p/` → does not exist. **Locked controls verified**: all 5 LockedActionButton instances unchanged; `Prepare SOW Draft LOCKED` + `Send to Client LOCKED` visibly preserved on the proposal page throughout the session. `npm run lint` clean; `NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 npm run build` clean. **27 app routes** (unchanged — no new route surfaces in P4, only an augmented existing proposal page); `/app/engagements/[id]/proposal` grows `8.53 kB / 112 kB` → `9.87 kB / 113 kB` (+1.34 kB for the new client share-link button + copy-once panel + eligibility chips); every other route within ±0 KB of the Sprint P3 baseline. **No package dependency.** Group-B grep clean. No middleware / `next.config.mjs` / API route / public route / storage / PDF library / AI synthesis change. **Backlog carried forward to Sprint P5**: live operator UX for share-eligible audience-label / recipient-email inputs (button currently uses defaults — no `audienceLabel` / `recipientEmail` parameter passing in the UI); per-(tokenId, ipHash) 5-min access debounce + `SLATE_SHARE_TOKEN_ACCESS_PEPPER` env config (mirror the Client Report Link MVP backlog); revoke-from-management-panel UI (the `revokeProposalShareTokenAction` exists but is not yet surfaced in the panel — Sprint P5 share management UX); dev-only short-expiry affordance (`SLATE_SHARE_TOKEN_DEV_BACKDATE` would let the Sprint P5 audit live-fire the expired-flip branch). **Recommended next sprint: Sprint P5 — public `/p/[token]` proposal review route** (anonymous server component under existing `/p/*` middleware exemption + service-role token lookup + render-time eligibility re-check + access logging + render-time expiry flip + generic-unavailable page across every blocked state + sanitized snapshot render via a new `components/proposals/client-proposal-share-document.tsx` that strips operator-only chrome from the existing `proposal-candidate-document.tsx`; **at the END of Sprint P5: `Prepare Client Review` unlocks** to "Generate Proposal Review Link" as the operator-facing primary action). `Send to Client`, `Prepare SOW Draft`, `Prepare Report` remain locked throughout Sprint P5.

## Phase 1B Proposal/SOW Delivery Sprint P3 — internal proposal candidate workflow (2026-05-15)

Sprint P3 ships the operator-only internal proposal candidate workflow per `docs/24` § Proposed Sprint Sequence + the Sprint P3 prompt's expanded scope. **Operator-only.** No public `/p/[token]` route (Sprint P5 scope). No `proposal_share_tokens` (Sprint P4 scope). No SOW Draft renderer (Sprint P6 scope). No `Generate Proposal Review Link` button (Sprint P4 scope). No `Prepare Client Review` / `Prepare SOW Draft` / `Send to Client` / `Prepare Report` unlock — all 5 `LockedActionButton` instances remain locked verbatim. No email / CRM / e-sign integration. No PDF binary / storage bucket. No new package dependency. Group-B confined to the canonical 4 files. **New route `app/app/engagements/[id]/proposal/candidate/[snapshotId]/page.tsx`** — operator-only server component under `/app/*` middleware. `dynamic = "force-dynamic"` + `revalidate = 0` + `fetchCache = "force-no-store"` segment config defeats Next 14's server-component fetch cache (same pattern as Sprint 4D-C's `/r/[token]` route; needed because cookie-bound supabase reads otherwise leak stale "not found" results). Loads the snapshot via `getProposalDeliverySnapshotById`. Defense-in-depth `snapshot.engagementId === params.id` mismatch → `notFound()`. Renders the snapshot-pure proposal candidate document inside the Sprint 4B `.slate-print-light` CSS-variable scope so chart-token colors fall back to the print-safe palette when the operator drives Save-as-PDF. **New component `components/reports/...wait, components/proposals/proposal-candidate-document.tsx`** — snapshot-pure renderer that reads ONLY from `option_snapshot` / `source_context_snapshot` / `commercial_guard_result` / `omitted_content` jsonb. Strips every operator-only-internal surface that would leak in a future client surface: NO internal UUIDs in the visible body, NO claim-guard codes / pattern families / banned-phrase surface (the safety strip surfaces ONLY "Content safety checks Passed" or a sanitized violation count — never the codes), NO reviewer notes, NO operator hint, NO `generated_by_label` initials beyond a single letter, NO audience-label leak, NO "Send to Client" / SOW / e-signature copy. Renders the canon-mandated proposal-side surfaces: operator hint (on-screen only via `print:hidden`), `CLIENT-SAFE PROPOSAL CANDIDATE · OPERATOR REVIEW ONLY · NOT SENT BY SLATE` banner (tone success when approved, warning when unreviewed), identity header (`AdvisoryOps · Proposal · Operator-internal candidate` eyebrow + `Sapient Digital · AI Opportunity Sprint · Proposal discussion draft` H1 + audit metadata strip), `Content safety checks` affirmative-only strip, `Draft candidate · operator review pending` warning whenever `draft_watermark=true`, `Proposal discussion draft` disclosure card with the canon-mandated three-line preamble ("This document is a commercial discussion artifact. It is not a binding quote, not a statement of work, and not a contract. Final scope, pricing, and timeline require written approval."), `Options (N)` list with per-option type tag + `Recommended` chip + best-fit-scenario + scope-summary + timeline ("Proposed range only — not a delivery guarantee.") + deliverables / assumptions / dependencies / risks bullet blocks + pricing-hidden notice when `pricing_review_state='placeholder'` (or "Estimated · subject to final approval. Not a binding quote." when manually/workflow-approved), omitted-content appendix with Group-B canonical entry (`Group-B (gated)` label + canon's `operatorFacingNote`) plus any per-option omissions, mandatory footer ("not a contract, not an executed SOW, not a financial guarantee, not acceptance of work"). **New component `components/proposals/proposal-candidates-panel.tsx`** — operator-only Past Proposal Candidates panel mounted on `/app/engagements/[id]/proposal` between `<ProposalWorkspace>` and the Boundary Reminder card. Server component. Loads snapshots via `getProposalDeliverySnapshotsForProposal`. Per-row surfaces: status badge (Candidate/Generated/Voided), delivery-surface badge, approval-state badge (Unreviewed/Approved/Approval revoked), pricing-review-state chip, Draft watermark chip when applicable, commercial-guard pass/fail icon (ShieldCheck or ShieldAlert with violation count), generated timestamp + generated-by initials, included-option count + selected-option count, proposal status at generation, snapshot id (monospace for operator audit), voided banner with timestamp + reason when status='voided', Open candidate link / Approve candidate button / Void button. Header carries the GenerateProposalCandidateButton + snapshot count. **New client component `components/proposals/generate-proposal-candidate-button.tsx`** — fires Sprint P2's `generateProposalCandidateAction({engagementId, proposalId})` via `useTransition`. Renders inline success notice ("Internal candidate generated. N option(s) included, M omitted. Approval state `unreviewed`; pricing review state `placeholder`. SLATE does not send this artifact to a client.") with Open candidate link + Draft-watermark chip. Renders inline failure notice with eligibility reasons or sanitized violation count (NEVER the banned phrase text — the action layer's `{field, code}` shape is preserved). **New client component `components/proposals/void-proposal-candidate-button.tsx`** — two-step confirm flow (Void → reason input + Confirm/Cancel) mirroring the report-side void button. **New client component `components/proposals/approve-proposal-candidate-button.tsx`** — single-click approval (no confirmation step since approval is a soft state flip that the operator can reverse). Hides itself with an explanatory chip when the commercial guard failed at generation time. **New `voidProposalDeliverySnapshotAction({snapshotId, reason})`** in `lib/proposals/snapshot-actions.ts` — cookie-bound operator action. Validates UUID + auth + already-voided guard. Updates `status='voided'`, `voided_at`, `voided_by`, `void_reason`, AND `approval_state='revoked'` (so a previously-approved candidate cannot keep its approved badge after a void). Emits `proposal_snapshot_voided` activity event with metadata `{proposalId, priorApprovalState, reason}` (reason truncated to 120 chars). Revalidates the proposal page + the specific candidate route. **No cascade-revoke** of proposal share tokens here — `proposal_share_tokens` doesn't exist until Sprint P4; the Sprint P4 commit will extend this action with a `cascadeRevokeActiveProposalShareTokens` helper mirroring the report-side pattern. **New `approveProposalDeliverySnapshotAction({snapshotId})`** — cookie-bound operator action. Validates UUID + auth + not-voided + not-already-approved + commercial-guard-passed (rejecting approval on a guard-failing candidate). Updates `approval_state='approved'` + `draft_watermark=false`. Emits new `proposal_snapshot_approved` activity event with metadata `{proposalId}` and summary "Operator approved the content of a proposal candidate. Pricing approval is independent; Send to Client and SOW Draft remain locked." Revalidates. Critically: approving CONTENT does NOT advance `pricing_review_state` — pricing approval is independent and remains a future commercial-workflow concern per `docs/24` § Pricing / Terms Policy. **New activity event type `proposal_snapshot_approved`** added to `lib/activity/types.ts` (tone `success`) + timeline label `Proposal candidate approved` in `components/activity/activity-timeline.tsx`. **`app/app/engagements/[id]/proposal/page.tsx` extended** — mounts `<ProposalCandidatesPanel engagementId={engagement.id} proposalId={proposal.id} />` between the proposal workspace and the boundary reminder card, persisted-UUID-engagement only. **Live verification against `76097653-fedb-42e5-9ef6-e89a0e97f802`'s proposal `77031092-700b-4da1-b69b-15e3dcd889d6`:** Generated first snapshot `2ff866d0-1d22-42e7-99ea-b21015fa53eb` via the Generate button — DB row inserted with `status='candidate'`, `delivery_surface='client_proposal_candidate'`, `approval_state='unreviewed'`, `pricing_review_state='placeholder'`, `draft_watermark=true`, `commercial_guard_result.passed=true` (scanned 12 fields against 45 patterns), `option_snapshot` carries all 3 proposal options with `includedInArtifact=true` only on the recommended one (`AI Workflow System`), `omitted_content` includes the canonical `group_b_block` entry + per-option `option_not_selected` entries for the 2 non-recommended options, `selected_option_ids` correctly captures `[<recommended-option-id>]`. Opened the candidate route — full operator-internal artifact rendered with all canon-mandated chrome (operator hint, banner, identity strip, content-safety affirmative, draft watermark, disclosure card, options list with Recommended chip and pricing-hidden notice, omitted-content appendix, four-denial footer). Clicked Approve candidate — flipped to `approval_state='approved'` + `draft_watermark=false`; `proposal_snapshot_approved` activity event emitted. Generated second snapshot `1485ccfe-7de2-477d-8d6f-314edb0a67a9` and voided the first one via the two-step Void flow with the default reason "Superseded by a newer candidate."; the previously-approved snapshot flipped to `status='voided'` + `approval_state='revoked'` + `voided_at=2026-05-15 18:14:32` + `void_reason='Superseded by a newer candidate.'`; `proposal_snapshot_voided` activity event emitted. Four activity events landed in correct chronological order: `generated → approved → generated → voided`. All locked controls (`Prepare SOW Draft LOCKED`, `Send to Client LOCKED`) remained visibly locked on the proposal page throughout the live session. **Bug found + fixed mid-walkthrough**: the candidate route initially returned "Snapshot not found" for a freshly-inserted snapshot id because Next 14's server-component `fetch` cache de-duped the cookie-bound `getProposalDeliverySnapshotById` call — same root cause Sprint 4D-C hit on `/r/[token]`. Fix: added `revalidate = 0` + `fetchCache = "force-no-store"` to the route segment config. Lint + build clean. **27 app routes** (was 26; the new `/app/engagements/[id]/proposal/candidate/[snapshotId]` at 184 B / 96.1 kB matches the report-side PDF candidate route shape). `/app/engagements/[id]/proposal` grows `6.55 kB / 110 kB` → `8.53 kB / 112 kB` (+1.98 kB for the Generate / Approve / Void / panel client-bundle additions). All other routes within ±0 KB of the Sprint P2 baseline. **No package dependency.** Group-B grep clean. **No middleware / `next.config.mjs` / API route / public route / storage / PDF library / AI synthesis change.** **Backlog carried forward to Sprint P4**: cascade-revoke proposal share tokens on snapshot void (mirror report-side); audience-label / scope-selection refinement UX (Sprint P4 + Sprint P5 scope when the share-link flow lands); SOW Draft generator + renderer (Sprint P6 scope). **Recommended next sprint: Sprint P4 — proposal share-token foundation** (migration `0016_proposal_share_tokens.sql` mirroring `0014_report_share_tokens.sql` shape + token service helpers + share eligibility evaluator + `generateProposalShareLinkAction` returning raw token once + copy-once Generate Proposal Review Link button on the Past Candidates panel; **`Prepare Client Review` still locked** because the public route lands in Sprint P5).

## Phase 1B Proposal/SOW Delivery Sprint P2 — proposal delivery foundation (2026-05-15)

Sprint P2 ships the operator-only proposal-delivery foundation per `docs/24` § Proposed Sprint Sequence. **Foundation-only sprint** — no public route, no share token, no Generate Proposal Review Link UI button, no internal proposal candidate route (Sprint P3 scope), no SOW Draft renderer (Sprint P6 scope), no email / CRM / e-sign integration. All five locked controls (`Send to Client`, `Prepare SOW Draft`, `Prepare Client Review`, `Prepare Report`, `Export Report` on mock paths) remain locked verbatim. **Migration `0015_proposal_delivery_snapshots.sql`** — new table `public.proposal_delivery_snapshots` with 30 columns mirroring `docs/24` § Snapshot Model Recommendation: workspace/engagement/proposal FKs + `status` CHECK (`candidate|generated|voided`) + `delivery_surface` CHECK (`internal_candidate|client_proposal_candidate|sow_draft_candidate`) + `proposal_status_at_generation` + `generated_by` / `generated_by_label` / `generated_at` + `option_snapshot` jsonb + `source_context_snapshot` jsonb + `commercial_guard_result` jsonb + `omitted_content` jsonb + `draft_watermark` boolean default `true` + `approval_state` CHECK (`unreviewed|approved|revoked`) default `unreviewed` + `pricing_review_state` CHECK (`placeholder|manually_approved|workflow_approved`) default `placeholder` + `selected_option_ids` uuid[] + nullable artifact_* columns + audit columns + `updated_at` trigger. Six indexes (workspace / engagement / proposal / generated_at desc / status / approval_state). Workspace-scoped operator-full RLS policy mirroring `0013` / `0014`. **No `to anon` policy.** Idempotent. Header comment explicitly states "No anon access. No public read. No public route exists yet." Applied to live Supabase via `node scripts/dev/apply-migration.cjs 0015_proposal_delivery_snapshots.sql` → `HTTP 201`. **New TS layer:** `lib/proposals/delivery-snapshot-types.ts` (`ProposalDeliverySnapshot` + `ProposalDeliverySnapshotStatus` + `ProposalDeliverySurface` + `ProposalApprovalState` + `ProposalPricingReviewState` + `ProposalOptionSnapshot` + `ProposalSourceContextSnapshot` + `ProposalCommercialGuardResult` + `ProposalOmittedContent` shapes; exports the `PROPOSAL_GROUP_B_OMISSION_ENTRY` canonical confirmation per canon § Proposal Eligibility Rules item 11); `lib/proposals/delivery-snapshot-mappers.ts` (defensive DB → TS mapper with jsonb validation; unknown enum values fall through to canon-safe defaults — `candidate` for status, `client_proposal_candidate` for surface, `unreviewed` for approval, `placeholder` for pricing review); `lib/proposals/delivery-snapshot-queries.ts` (`getProposalDeliverySnapshotById` + `getProposalDeliverySnapshotsForProposal` + `getLatestProposalDeliverySnapshotForProposal` — all RLS-gated via the cookie-bound client; **no service-role helpers** — those land in Sprint P5 when the public `/p/[token]` route is authorized). **New module `lib/proposals/commercial-guard.ts`** — extends `lib/ai/claim-guard.ts`'s shared 26-pattern scanner (financial 14 + commercial-finality 6 + roadmap-commitment 6) with **18 new `PROPOSAL_FINALITY_PATTERNS`** per `docs/24` § Commercial Claim Guard: `fixed_price`, `final_terms`, `contract_accepted`, `client_has_agreed`, `work_will_begin_on_date` (date-bearing heuristic), `payment_due`, `auto_renewal`, `cancellation_terms`, `cancellation_policy`, `please_sign`, `sign_here`, `signature_required`, `legally_binding`, `guaranteed_delivery_date`, `guaranteed_implementation_timeline`, `i_authorize`, `by_accepting`, `by_signing`, contextual `effective_date_binding` (only flags when paired with `agreement|contract|binding|executed` within 80 chars). `runProposalCommercialGuard({optionSnapshot, sourceContextSnapshot, extras?})` returns a `ProposalCommercialGuardResult` with `passed` boolean + `violations[]` (each tagged with `patternFamily: 'financial'|'commercial-finality'|'roadmap-commitment'|'proposal-finality'`) + `scannedFieldCount` + `patternCount` + `scanDurationMs` + `version: 'commercial-guard.v1'`. Scans the full canon-mandated field set: per-included-option title / bestFitScenario / scopeSummary / timeline / each entry of deliverables / assumptions / dependencies / risks / pricingPlaceholder + proposal-level implementation-credit copy (creditAmountPlaceholder / creditWindow / creditNotes); Sprint P6 SOW hook accepts optional `extras.sowDraftText` + `extras.footerText`. Reviewer notes excluded by default per canon. **New module `lib/proposals/eligibility.ts`** — pure `evaluateProposalDeliveryEligibility(input)` returning `{eligible, reasons[], includedOptionIds, omittedOptionIds, omittedContent[], draftWatermark, pricingReviewState}` enforcing the canon's 13 Proposal Eligibility rules. Returns ALL applicable reasons (not first-fail). 11 reason codes — `engagement_not_persisted` / `proposal_missing` / `no_options_exist` / `no_selection` / `included_options_empty` / `option_blank_commercial_terms` / `commercial_guard_failed` / `reviewer_only_option` / `sow_surface_not_authorized_yet` (rejects `sow_draft_candidate` surface in P2 since Sprint P6 hasn't shipped) / `implementation_credit_pending_approval` (severity `warning`, not blocking) / `unsupported_surface`. Placeholder-pattern detection rejects `TBD` / `TODO` / `<scope>` / `<timeline>` / `<placeholder>` in commercial-content fields. **New action `lib/proposals/snapshot-actions.ts`** — `generateProposalCandidateAction({engagementId, proposalId?, selectedOptionIds?})` cookie-bound operator action. Validates UUIDs + auth + engagement existence. Loads proposal + options via `getProposalForEngagementPersisted`. Resolves included-options set (`selectedOptionIds[]` precedence; falls through to `recommended=true` options; surfaces `no_selection` reason if neither). Builds full `optionSnapshot` (all options captured for audit; `includedInArtifact` flag per option) + `sourceContextSnapshot` (implementation credit verbatim; linked opportunity/roadmap ids deduped from included options; `reportSnapshotId: null` — Sprint P5 hook). Runs commercial guard before eligibility (eligibility consumes the guard's `passed` flag). On guard failure → logs `proposal_snapshot_failed` with metadata `{proposalId, failureReason: 'commercial_guard_violation', violationCount}` (NEVER violation text — would smuggle banned phrases into the activity feed) + returns `{ok: false, error: 'commercial-guard-violation', violations: [{field, code}]}`. On eligibility-blocker → logs `proposal_snapshot_failed` with `failureReason: 'eligibility_blocked'` + blockerCount. Happy path → inserts row with `status='candidate'`, `delivery_surface='client_proposal_candidate'`, `approval_state='unreviewed'`, `pricing_review_state='placeholder'`, `draft_watermark=true`, prepends `PROPOSAL_GROUP_B_OMISSION_ENTRY` to the omitted_content array; logs `proposal_snapshot_generated` activity event with `{proposalId, deliverySurface, draftWatermark, approvalState, pricingReviewState, includedOptionCount, omittedOptionCount}`; revalidates proposal + engagement detail routes; returns `{ok: true, snapshotId, deliverySurface, draftWatermark, approvalState, pricingReviewState, includedOptionCount, omittedOptionCount}`. **New activity event types** — 3 added to `lib/activity/types.ts` (`proposal_snapshot_generated` tone `success`, `proposal_snapshot_failed` tone `risk`, `proposal_snapshot_voided` tone `neutral`) + new `proposal_delivery_snapshot` entity type. Timeline labels added to `components/activity/activity-timeline.tsx`: `Proposal candidate` / `Proposal candidate failed` / `Proposal candidate voided`. **No UI button mounted** in Sprint P2 — the action is callable from server-action contexts only; Sprint P3 will wire it into the proposal page surface. **No public route** — `app/p/[token]/` does not exist. **No share token table** — `proposal_share_tokens` is Sprint P4 scope. **Live validation deferred to Sprint P3** consistent with the P2 prompt's escape clause ("If no safe invocation path exists, statically verify and defer live generation to P3 when UI route exists.") — auto-mode classifier blocks the direct service-role insert that would otherwise let the audit fire the action without a UI button. Static verification: 4 modules + 1 migration + 1 mappers/queries set + 1 action all type-check + lint clean; the migration applied successfully and `proposal_delivery_snapshots` is queryable from the cookie-bound server client (verified via the migration apply HTTP 201 + the type signature of `getProposalDeliverySnapshotsForProposal` compiling against the live schema). `npm run lint` clean; `NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 npm run build` clean. **26 app routes** (unchanged from post-`docs/23`); every route's First Load JS byte-identical to the post-Client-Report-Link baseline — `/r/[token]` 152 B / 87.4 kB · `/app/engagements/[id]/proposal` 6.55 kB / 110 kB · `/app/engagements/[id]/report` 11.8 kB / 115 kB · `/app/engagements/[id]/report/pdf-candidate/[snapshotId]` 181 B / 96.1 kB · `/app/engagements/[id]/roadmap` 5.91 kB / 109 kB · `/scorecard/results` 10.7 kB / 119 kB. **No package dependency** — `package.json` / `package-lock.json` untouched. Group-B grep clean (confined to the canonical 4 files — `components/charts/exhibits/{benchmark-comparison-bars,ai-savings-waterfall,roi-bridge}.tsx` + `app/app/charts-preview/page.tsx`); `lib/proposals/commercial-guard.ts` itself contains the prohibition strings as `RegExp` patterns (which is the documented canon-allowed shape — same as `lib/ai/claim-guard.ts`'s existing 26 patterns) so the grep's matches inside the guard file are pattern-definitions, not violating content. All 5 LockedActionButton sites unchanged. No middleware / `next.config.mjs` / API route / public route / storage bucket / PDF library / AI synthesis change. **Backlog carried forward to Sprint P3**: (1) live validation of `generateProposalCandidateAction` via a real operator-mediated UI surface — Sprint P3's internal candidate route + Past Proposal Candidates panel will be the natural test harness; (2) `voidProposalDeliverySnapshotAction` — deliberately not authored in P2; the spec said "can be deferred to P3 unless simple" and the action layer benefits from being authored alongside the panel that surfaces the void button; (3) banned-phrase live test against the commercial guard — same classifier-deferred path as Sprint 4C-D's report-side banned-phrase test, needs explicit operator authorization for a section-text mutation or a UI edit surface; (4) cascade-revoke semantics for proposal share tokens — Sprint P4 scope. **Recommended next sprint: Sprint P3 — internal proposal candidate route + Past Proposal Candidates panel + `voidProposalDeliverySnapshotAction`**, conditional on this Sprint P2 foundation accepting.

## Phase 1B Proposal/SOW Delivery Canon — Sprint P1 (2026-05-15)

`docs/24_PHASE_1B_PROPOSAL_SOW_DELIVERY_CANON.md` is the canonical governance doc for moving from the operator-only internal proposal workspace toward any future client-facing proposal / SOW delivery surface. **Canon-only sprint.** No source code authored. No schema / migration / API route / package / public route / storage bucket / PDF library / email / CRM / e-sign integration. All three proposal-side `LockedActionButton` instances (`Prepare Client Review`, `Prepare SOW Draft`, `Send to Client`) remain locked verbatim. The report-side Client Report Link MVP is unaffected — `/r/[token]` continues to render the advisory report artifact per `docs/22` and `docs/23`. **Canon defines** the proposal-side equivalent of `docs/19` + `docs/20` + `docs/22` together: an eight-surface delivery taxonomy (internal workspace [current] → internal preview → proposal snapshot candidate → client proposal review link → SOW draft artifact → SOW approval / e-signature [out of scope] → CRM/email-mediated send [later] → final contract [out of current scope]); strict artifact definitions (Proposal Candidate = client-safe commercial discussion artifact, NOT binding; SOW Draft = formal scope artifact still non-binding until executed; Executed SOW = out of Phase 1B scope unless separately authorized); 13 Proposal Eligibility rules covering persisted UUID + recommended-option requirement + commercial-guard pass + Group-B exclusion + reviewer-notes exclusion + report-snapshot reference by friendly context only; 11 stricter SOW Eligibility rules stacked on top of proposal eligibility (Proposal Candidate must exist and be approved + operator explicitly selects scope/options + pricing manually reviewed + timeline framed as estimated + zero e-signature language + zero binding-acceptance language + mandatory "Draft · not executed" marking); a future `proposal_delivery_snapshots` table recommendation (separate table from `report_delivery_snapshots` — rejected reuse because of different eligibility / lifecycle / risk / guard / approval semantics) with conceptual columns including `delivery_surface` (`internal_candidate | client_proposal_candidate | sow_draft_candidate`), `approval_state` (`unreviewed | approved | revoked`), `pricing_review_state` (`placeholder | manually_approved | workflow_approved`), `selected_option_ids`, `option_snapshot` jsonb, `commercial_guard_result` jsonb, full audit columns mirroring `report_delivery_snapshots`; a future `proposal_share_tokens` table recommendation (separate from `report_share_tokens`, same opaque-256-bit + SHA-256-hex + 14d-default / 30d-max + revocation + access logging + generic unavailable + no anon RLS pattern) with URL recommendation `/p/[token]`; a **commercial claim guard** extending `lib/ai/claim-guard.ts`'s existing 26 patterns (financial 14 + commercial-finality 6 + roadmap-commitment 6) with 15 new proposal-finality patterns (`fixed price`, `final terms`, `contract accepted`, `client has agreed`, `work will begin on <date>`, `payment due`, `auto-renewal`, `cancellation terms`, `please sign`, `sign here`, `signature required`, `legally binding`, `guaranteed delivery date`, `guaranteed implementation timeline`, `I authorize`, `by accepting`, `by signing`, contextual `effective date <date>`) — runs at export time over option titles + scope summaries + timelines + best-fit scenarios + every entry of deliverables/assumptions/dependencies/risks + pricing placeholders + SOW draft text + footer/disclaimer text; reviewer notes excluded by default; mandatory disclaimers (Proposal Candidate header "Proposal discussion draft" + footer "Not a binding quote · Not a statement of work · Final scope, pricing, and timeline require written approval"; SOW Draft header "Draft SOW · not executed" + footer "Requires authorized review and signature · Not binding until fully executed · Pricing and timeline subject to final approval"; universal closing line "not a contract, not an executed SOW, not a financial guarantee, not acceptance of work"); explicit relationship to the Client Report Link MVP (reuse opaque-token / SHA-256-hash / no-raw-UUIDs / generic-unavailable / `noindex,nofollow` / `Cache-Control: no-store` / `fetchCache="force-no-store"` / peppered IP-UA / cookie-bound-revoke / service-role-public-lookup / snapshot-pure-render — but DO NOT reuse domain logic, snapshot table, share-token table, claim-guard configuration, cascade-revoke semantics, disclaimer footer, or audience model); a future `/p/[token]` security spec mirroring `/r/[token]` (anonymous via existing `needsSessionRefresh` middleware exemption, pure server component, service-role public lookup, no raw UUIDs in body, generic unavailable across every blocked state, no e-signature controls, no pricing unless `pricing_review_state ∈ ('manually_approved','workflow_approved')`, no file storage by default, browser print/save allowed); **unlock policies**: `Prepare Client Review` unlocks at Sprint P5 (after `proposal_delivery_snapshots` + commercial guard + `/p/[token]` route + share token + revocation + access log + approved proposal options + disclaimers all land) and the first unlock is `Generate Proposal Review Link` not an auto-send; `Prepare SOW Draft` unlocks at Sprint P6 (after non-voided Proposal Candidate + `approval_state='approved'` + operator-selected scope + SOW renderer + SOW guard pass + pricing review state + "Draft · not executed" marking); `Send to Client` remains locked, last to unlock, requires a separate `docs/25_PHASE_1B_SLATE_DELIVERY_CHANNEL_CANON.md` channel canon if SLATE is ever to send anything, first version operator-mediated copy-link not auto-email; **pricing/terms policy** locks `pricing_placeholder` as informational, forbids AI-generated final pricing, requires "Estimated · subject to final approval" framing whenever a price is shown; **e-signature/contract policy** puts e-sign out of Phase 1B scope, lists future-canon prerequisites (legal model + audit trail + storage model + vendor selection + post-signature workflow); future audit event types defined but not implemented (`proposal_snapshot_generated`/`_failed`/`_voided`, `proposal_share_token_created`/`_accessed`/`_revoked`/`_expired`, `sow_draft_generated`/`_voided`, optional later `sow_share_token_*`, optional `proposal_sent_to_client`/`sow_sent_to_client` for Sprint P8 if it ever runs); 8-sprint sequence P1 (canon, this sprint) → P2 (proposal delivery foundation: migration + types/mappers/queries + commercial guard + eligibility evaluator + operator-only candidate generator) → P3 (internal candidate route + Past Proposal Candidates panel + void action + 3 activity events) → P4 (share-token foundation + Generate Proposal Review Link copy-once button) → P5 (public `/p/[token]` route + access logging + revoke + render-time eligibility re-check + render-time expiry flip → unlocks `Prepare Client Review` at the end as `Generate Proposal Review Link`) → **audit gate** (Proposal Review Link MVP Acceptance Audit mirroring `docs/23`) → P6 (SOW Draft canon addendum if needed + SOW Draft snapshot + internal preview → unlocks `Prepare SOW Draft` as `Generate SOW Draft`) → P7 (SOW Draft share route IF approved, separate decision) → P8 (optional Send to Client unlock after channel canon, copy-link first, no auto-email) → later (e-sign / CRM / contract execution canons); parallel work that does not block P1–P5: the five-item Client Report Link production-hardening backlog from `docs/23` + Benchmark Gate 1 / Financial Gate 1 advancement (Group-B exclusion holds throughout proposal delivery regardless); 12 open decisions enumerated with committed recommended defaults: `/p/[token]` URL shape, shared `proposal_delivery_snapshots` table with surface enum, separate `proposal_share_tokens` table, pricing hidden in MVP until approval workflow, SOW Draft in separate milestone (Sprint P6), browser print/save allowed with no PDF binary, optional recipient email hash, copy-link only no SLATE-mediated email, report-snapshot reference by friendly context only, implementation credit hidden until commercial approval, optional audience-label / signatory identifier capture, cascade-revoke proposal tokens on snapshot void (mirror report side); pointer-forward binding — future Sprint P2–P7 / P8 agents must treat `docs/24` as source of truth, deviations require canon amendment before code. **Sprint P1 authorizes only Sprint P2's scope if accepted; nothing else.** Acceptance criteria: 15 conditions met (docs/24 exists with every required section, artifact definitions clear, eligibility rules defined for both surfaces, snapshot + share-token model recommendations defined, commercial guard pattern set + run-time policy defined, mandatory disclaimer copy defined, three unlock policies defined, pricing + e-sign boundaries defined, sprint sequence defined, open decisions enumerated with defaults, docs/08 + docs/10 updated, no source/schema/API/package/public/storage/PDF/email/CRM/middleware/next.config changes). `git status --short` after this sprint: only `docs/08`, `docs/10`, `docs/24` modified (`docs/24` added). **No source code touched.** Visx plan file remains parked. The recommended next sprint conditional on operator approval is **Sprint P2 — proposal delivery foundation** (migration `0015_proposal_delivery_snapshots.sql` + TS types/mappers/queries + `lib/proposals/commercial-guard.ts` extracted from / extending `lib/ai/claim-guard.ts` + `evaluateProposalDeliveryEligibility` + `generateProposalCandidateAction` server action that returns the snapshot id; operator-only with NO public route, NO Send to Client unlock, NO email/CRM, NO PDF binary, NO Group-B wiring).

## Client Report Link MVP Acceptance Audit (2026-05-15)

`docs/23_CLIENT_REPORT_LINK_MVP_ACCEPTANCE_AUDIT.md` is the formal acceptance audit of Sprint 4D-B + Sprint 4D-C together as the operator-mediated client share surface. **Read-only audit sprint.** No source code modified. No commits authored. Docs-only output. **Outcome — Accepted with notes for production rollout.** Inventory audit confirmed all 12 expected files present at expected sizes (migration `0014_report_share_tokens.sql` 7,453 B; helpers + components + the public route). Migration / DB shape verified statically against the migration source plus the Sprint 4D-B and 4D-C walkthrough's live evidence (consistent with the Sprint 4C-C precedent in `docs/21` — live SQL on the shared DB is gated by the auto-mode classifier; the canonical shape is the migration source itself plus the Sprint 4D-B `HTTP 201` apply confirmation): 17 columns, three idempotent DO-block CHECK constraints (`expires_after_created`, SHA-256 hex shape on `recipient_email_hash` AND `token_hash`), six indexes (unique `token_hash` + 5 secondaries), self-contained `updated_at` trigger, workspace-scoped `to authenticated` RLS policy ONLY (NO `to anon` policy; header comment explicitly states "No anon policy. No public read."). Token security audit: raw token never persisted (grep confirms surfaces only in in-process function args, action return value, copy-once UI local state, doc comments); `hashShareToken` is SHA-256 hex; raw tokens are 256-bit entropy 43-char base64url from `crypto.randomBytes(32)`; no JWT / `jose` / `jsonwebtoken` package dep; no public RLS policy; activity-event metadata uses sanitized key names (`recipientHashPresent` boolean only, never an email; `ipSig` / `uaSig` 128-bit peppered fingerprints, never raw values) that survive the activity logger's `FORBIDDEN_KEY_PATTERNS` strip while staying explicit. Public route security audit: `/r/[token]` is anonymous (middleware exempts it via `needsSessionRefresh` matcher), pure server component (zero `"use client"`), zero cookie-bound Supabase calls (5 service-role call sites only; `lib/supabase/service.ts` is `import "server-only"`), `dynamic="force-dynamic"` + `revalidate=0` + `fetchCache="force-no-store"` segment config defeats Next 14's server-component fetch cache, `Cache-Control: no-store` + `X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow` + `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` headers via `next.config.mjs` headers entry, inline `<meta name="robots" content="noindex, nofollow">` from `generateMetadata`, generic-unavailable page renders identically across every blocked state (unknown 9,171 B vs revoked 9,326 B — both contain only "This report link is unavailable" + four-denial footer; the small byte delta is from cookie / RSC framing not content), zero internal UUID leak in the rendered body. Client artifact content audit on the valid-token render screenshot `ss_2689hs37x`: title strip (Saipien Labs · Client Report eyebrow + Client Report H1 + Generated May 14, 2026) + affirmative-only "Content safety checks passed" strip + Sections (1) Executive Summary with sanitized labels ("Detail" not "Draft preview"; "Supporting notes" not "Evidence notes") + Visual Exhibits source-summary card for Roadmap 90 Day Sequence (no live SVG) + mandatory four-denial footer all present; ZERO internal UUIDs / token hash / reviewer notes / operator hint / Draft Candidate / void banner / claim-guard codes / `generated_by_label` / audience-label leak / Send-to-Client copy. Access logging audit: two consecutive curl visits incremented `access_count` from 0 → 2 and updated `last_accessed_at`; activity event `Share link accessed` (info-tone, system actor) emitted with sanitized summary "Public share link rendered. SLATE recorded an access event with peppered request fingerprints only."; raw IP / UA never persisted; `SLATE_SHARE_TOKEN_ACCESS_PEPPER` unset in dev → `hashesOmitted: true` rather than low-entropy un-peppered hashes (carry-forward item 1). Revocation audit: two live revokes executed in this audit session against tokens `dpIaT5xx…` and `jKOLP67Bqo-…`; both flipped status atomically, emitted `Share link revoked` activity events with metadata `{snapshotId, reason}`, and subsequent curl returned the generic-unavailable page at 9,326 B with `grep -E "Executive Summary|Content safety checks passed"` returning zero matches. Expiry audit static-verified via code review (`evaluateShareTokenPublicAccess` returns `{status: "expired", shouldFlipExpired}` for past-expiry active tokens; `flipShareTokenExpired` UPDATE is guarded by compound `WHERE id = $1 AND status = 'active'` so concurrent flips/revokes race-safely; `report_share_token_expired` activity event emits on success); live-firing deferred to Sprint 4D-D backlog (carry-forward item 3 — needs dev-only short-expiry affordance because raw-SQL back-date is classifier-blocked). Eligibility audit: eligible state confirmed via live render of snapshot `9068f58f-…` (candidate / client_pdf_candidate / draft_watermark off / claim_guard passed / Group-B omission present / age < 1 day); ineligible states verified — voided snapshot `580db834-…` shows "Share disabled" chip with two reasons in the Past Candidates panel (live-verified yesterday); stale-accepted / old-snapshot / claim-guard-failed states are not naturally available in the test set and are static-verified via evaluator code review. Locked controls / boundary audit: `grep -RnE "<LockedActionButton|LockedActionButton label" app components` returns exactly 5 usage sites (Prepare Report on roadmap + Prepare Client Review on proposal + Export Report on report mock paths + Prepare SOW Draft + Send to Client on proposal-workspace); zero email/CRM/SOW/e-signature/PDF-binary deps (`grep -iE "puppeteer|playwright|react-pdf|pdfkit|pdf-lib|jspdf|sendgrid|nodemailer|salesforce|hubspot|docusign" package.json` returns zero); Group-B exhibits confined to the canonical 4 files. Build / lint / route audit: `npm run lint` clean; `NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 npm run build` clean. **26 app routes** (unchanged from Sprint 4D-C). First Load JS: `/r/[token]` 152 B / 87.4 kB (shared-chunk only — zero route-specific JS); `/app/engagements/[id]/report` 11.8 kB / 115 kB; `/app/engagements/[id]/report/pdf-candidate/[snapshotId]` 181 B / 96.1 kB; `/app/engagements/[id]/proposal` 6.55 kB / 110 kB; `/app/engagements/[id]/roadmap` 5.91 kB / 109 kB; `/scorecard/results` 10.7 kB / 119 kB — all unchanged from Sprint 4D-C baseline.
