Validates UUIDs + auth. Loads the approved source Proposal Candidate snapshot. Runs `runSowDraftCommercialGuard` (or extended `runProposalCommercialGuard`) before the eligibility evaluator. Builds the `sowDraftSnapshot` jsonb shape under `source_context_snapshot.sowDraft`. Inserts the row with `delivery_surface='sow_draft_candidate'` + canon defaults (`status='candidate'`, `approval_state='unreviewed'`, `pricing_review_state` copied from source, `draft_watermark=true`). Logs `sow_draft_generated` activity event with sanitized metadata (count + reason codes only on failure paths — NEVER violation text). Revalidates the proposal page.
4. **3 new activity event types** in `lib/activity/types.ts` (`sow_draft_generated`, `sow_draft_failed`, `sow_draft_voided`) + timeline tones/labels in `components/activity/activity-timeline.tsx`. Optional `sow_draft_approved` if Sprint P6-C chooses to ship SOW-side approval as a separate state.
5. **NO migration in P6-B** — reuse `proposal_delivery_snapshots` with `delivery_surface='sow_draft_candidate'` per `docs/26` § SOW Snapshot Model. The Sprint P2 schema already accepts this CHECK value.
6. **NO public route in P6-B.** NO `Generate SOW Draft` button in P6-B. NO `<PastSowDraftsPanel>` in P6-B. Those land in Sprint P6-C. Sprint P6-B is foundation-only — operator-callable via server-action context only; the action is callable but has no UI surface yet. Live validation of the action is deferred to Sprint P6-C when the UI surface lands, mirroring the Sprint P2 → P3 cadence.

**Sprint P6-B remains operator-only.** No public route. No client delivery. No SOW Draft renderer yet. `Send to Client`, `Prepare SOW Draft`, `Prepare Client Review` (mock paths only — unlocked for persisted UUID engagements per Sprint P5), `Prepare Report` all stay locked verbatim. No email/CRM. No PDF binary. No new package dependency. No Group-B wiring.

These items apply to both the report-side `/r/[token]` and the proposal-side `/p/[token]` surfaces so the hardening sprint covers both lanes.

For reference, the Sprint P5 scope just shipped was:

1. **Status** — date / branch / commits audited (`647b664` P4 + the upcoming P5 commit) / outcome (Accepted / Accepted with notes / Blocked) / scope (Sprint P2 → P5) / locked controls verification.
2. **Executive Summary** — operator-mediated proposal share works end-to-end; `Prepare Client Review` unlocked; `Prepare SOW Draft` + `Send to Client` + `Prepare Report` still locked.
3. **Inventory Audit** — verify the 12 files from Sprint P2 / P3 / P4 / P5 (P2 foundation = 7; P3 candidate workflow = 5 new + 4 modified; P4 share-token foundation = 7 new + 4 modified; P5 public route = 3 new + 2 modified including the unlock).
4. **Migration / DB Audit** — `0015_proposal_delivery_snapshots` + `0016_proposal_share_tokens` shape verified statically against the migration source + live evidence (the Sprint P3 / P4 / P5 walkthrough notes; Supabase MCP-read availability has been intermittently classifier-allowed and intermittently denied this session, so the audit should follow the `docs/23` precedent of static + walkthrough corroboration).

6. **Public Route Security Audit** — anonymous `/p/[token]` middleware exempted; pure server component; zero cookie-bound Supabase; service-role only; `fetchCache="force-no-store"`; identical generic-unavailable across all blocked states; security headers via `next.config.mjs` + inline robots meta.
7. **Client Artifact Content Audit** — proposal-side mandatory four-denial footer present; ZERO internal UUID / token_hash / reviewer note / operator hint / commercial-guard code / `generated_by_label` / audience-label leak; pricing hidden when `pricing_review_state='placeholder'`.
8. **Access Logging Audit** — `access_count` increments + `last_accessed_at` updates + `proposal_share_token_accessed` event emits with sanitized metadata; raw IP / UA never persisted.
9. **Revocation Audit** — operator revoke + cascade-revoke-on-snapshot-void both live-verified; revoked tokens render generic-unavailable.
10. **Expiry Audit** — static-verified (raw-SQL back-date classifier-deferred, same as `docs/23`).
11. **Eligibility Audit** — voided / draft-watermark / un-approved / SOW-surface / claim-guard-failed / over-age / no-included-options all reject correctly.
12. **Locked Controls / Boundary Audit** — `Send to Client` + `Prepare SOW Draft` + `Prepare Report` + `Export Report` (mock paths) all remain locked verbatim (4 remaining `LockedActionButton` sites — was 5 before Sprint P5's `Prepare Client Review` unlock).
13. **Build / Lint / Route Audit** — 28 app routes; First Load JS sizes captured.
14. **Visual Artifacts** — Chrome MCP screenshots under `artifacts/walkthroughs/proposal-review-link-mvp-acceptance/` mirroring `client-report-link-mvp-acceptance/`.

For reference, the Sprint P5 scope just shipped was:

Sprint P5 scope per `docs/24` § Client Proposal Route Security + the Sprint 4D-C precedent on the report side:

1. **`app/p/[token]/page.tsx`** — Next.js App Router server component. NOT under `/app/*` middleware (the existing `needsSessionRefresh` matcher in `middleware.ts` only refreshes `/app/*`, `/login`, `/auth/*`; `/p/*` falls through identically to `/r/*` from Sprint 4D-C). `dynamic = "force-dynamic"` + `revalidate = 0` + `fetchCache = "force-no-store"` segment config (same Sprint 4D-C precedent — defeats Next 14's server-component fetch-cache de-dup that would otherwise serve stale "not found" reads). Resolves `sha256(rawToken)` → `proposal_share_tokens` row via a **privileged server path** in a new `lib/proposals/share-token-public.ts` module that uses `createSupabaseServiceClient()`, NOT anonymous Supabase. Re-runs `evaluateProposalShareEligibility(snapshot)` at render time as defense-in-depth — even if a token was minted against an eligible snapshot, expiry / void / cascade re-evaluation must fail closed if the snapshot has since been voided.
2. **Generic rejection page** — single shared component returning the same generic copy ("This proposal review link is no longer available. Contact the sender for an updated link.") for every failure mode: unknown token, expired, revoked, voided snapshot, snapshot un-approved, claim-guard fail, missing Group-B omission. Never leak which condition failed. Identical body shape across all blocked paths so size-based diffing cannot infer state.
3. **`noindex,nofollow` + `Cache-Control: no-store` + `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer`** — `generateMetadata` on the route surface inline `<meta name="robots">` + `next.config.mjs` `headers` entry for `/p/:token*` mirroring the existing `/r/:token*` block.
4. **Snapshot-pure render via new `components/proposals/client-proposal-share-document.tsx`** — strips every operator-only surface from the existing `proposal-candidate-document.tsx`: NO internal UUIDs, NO `generated_by_label`, NO commercial-guard codes / pattern families / banned-phrase surface (affirmative "Content safety checks passed" only), NO draft watermark, NO operator hint chrome, NO "operator-only" / "not sent by SLATE" framing (those are operator-internal disclaimers). The proposal-side mandatory disclaimers from `docs/24` § Required Disclaimers / Markings — "Not a binding quote · Not a statement of work · Final scope, pricing, and timeline require written approval" + the closing four-denial line "not a contract, not an executed SOW, not a financial guarantee, not acceptance of work" — are present on every render. Pricing remains hidden whenever `pricing_review_state='placeholder'`; surfaces with "Estimated · subject to final approval" framing when `manually_approved` or `workflow_approved`.
5. **Access logging** — debounced per `(token_id, ip_hash)` at 5-min intervals; IP + UA hashed with server-side pepper from `SLATE_SHARE_TOKEN_ACCESS_PEPPER` env var (never logged raw). Writes `last_accessed_at` + increments `access_count` + emits `proposal_share_token_accessed` activity event with sanitized `{ipSig, uaSig, hashesOmitted, snapshotId, proposalId}` metadata (deliberate short key names that survive the activity logger's `FORBIDDEN_KEY_PATTERNS` strip — same pattern that worked for the report-side `recipientHashPresent` + `ipSig` keys).
6. **Rate limiting** — per-IP rate limit (suggested 30 req/min) + per-token render limit (suggested 100 renders/day) using a lightweight in-memory or KV-backed counter mirroring the Sprint 4D-C plan; rate-limit failures return the same generic rejection page.
7. **Render-time status-flip on expiry** — when the route encounters a token whose `expires_at < now()` AND `status='active'`, the privileged server path flips it to `status='expired'` via compound `WHERE id = $1 AND status = 'active'` (race-safe) and emits `proposal_share_token_expired` activity event. Background expiry sweeper deferred — render-time flip is the canon-default for the first implementation.
8. **`Prepare Client Review` unlock at the END of Sprint P5.** The proposal page's existing `<LockedActionButton label="Prepare Client Review" lockedNote="Locked" />` is replaced for persisted UUID engagements with a new `<GenerateProposalShareLinkButton>`-style affordance (or the existing button can be reused if the panel-mount UX is enough). The proposal-workspace-side `Prepare SOW Draft` + `Send to Client` `LockedActionButton`s remain locked verbatim — `Prepare SOW Draft` is Sprint P6 scope; `Send to Client` is Sprint P8 scope and requires a separate channel canon.
9. **Audit gate after Sprint P5**: a Proposal Review Link MVP Acceptance Audit mirroring `docs/23` for the report-side MVP. Required before Sprint P6 (SOW Draft) begins.

**Sprint P5 is the first sprint that exposes any client-accessible proposal surface.** SLATE does not email or send anything in Sprint P5 either; the operator copies the URL from the Sprint P4 button and hand-delivers it.

For reference, the Sprint P3 scope just shipped was:

Sprint P3 scope per `docs/24`:

1. **`app/app/engagements/[id]/proposal/candidate/[snapshotId]/page.tsx`** — operator-only internal proposal candidate route. Server component under `/app/*` (RLS-gated). Loads the snapshot via `getProposalDeliverySnapshotById`. Defense-in-depth `snapshot.engagementId === params.id`. Renders the snapshot-pure proposal artifact: identity strip (with UUIDs visible to operators — this is the operator-only view), commercial-guard strip (operator-visible "passed" / "X violations" badge), draft-watermark banner when `draft_watermark=true`, included options list, omitted-options appendix with reasons, mandatory operator footer ("Operator-only candidate — no public route exists. SLATE does not deliver this artifact to a client."). Print-friendly via `.slate-print-light` CSS-variable scope. No client send. No share token. No PDF binary.
2. **`components/proposals/proposal-candidate-document.tsx`** — the snapshot-pure renderer (mirrors `components/reports/report-pdf-candidate-document.tsx` but consumes the proposal snapshot shape).

Server component using `getProposalDeliverySnapshotsForProposal`. Per-row: status badge / delivery-surface badge / approval-state chip / pricing-review-state chip / commercial-guard pass/fail icon / included-option count / generated-by-label / Open candidate link / Void button. **The button that invokes `generateProposalCandidateAction` (Sprint P2's action) is also mounted in this sprint** — a `<GenerateProposalCandidateButton>` (similar shape to `<GeneratePdfCandidateButton>`) with the recommended-vs-explicit-selection UX from canon (Sprint P3 may default to recommended-flag selection and surface the operator-selection refinement in Sprint P4 when audience-label / scope-selection UX lands).

6. **Approval-state UI hook** — surface an "Approve candidate" action that flips `approval_state` from `unreviewed` → `approved`. This is the prerequisite for Sprint P6's SOW Draft generation but lands in P3 because the operator needs to flip it during the P3 walkthrough to validate the lifecycle. **`Prepare Client Review` remains locked throughout P3** — approval-state flip alone does not unlock client delivery.
7. **Live validation** of `generateProposalCandidateAction` against snapshot `9068f58f-…`'s parent engagement (`76097653-fedb-42e5-9ef6-e89a0e97f802`) — fire the action via the new UI button, verify the row inserts with `commercial_guard_result.passed=true`, `draft_watermark=true`, `approval_state='unreviewed'`, `pricing_review_state='placeholder'`, the canonical Group-B omission entry in `omitted_content`. Flip approval state and verify the candidate document renders without the draft watermark.
8. **Activity event surface** — the timeline already has tones/labels for the three event types from Sprint P2; P3 just exercises them.

**Sprint P3 remains operator-only.** No public route. No share token. No client delivery. The Generate / Open / Void / Approve controls all live under `/app/*` auth.

For reference, the original Sprint P2 scope authored in `docs/24` was:

Sprint P2 scope per `docs/24`:

1. **Migration `0015_proposal_delivery_snapshots.sql`** — new table `public.proposal_delivery_snapshots` with conceptual columns per § Snapshot Model Recommendation: `id` (uuid pk) + workspace/engagement/proposal FKs + `status` CHECK (`candidate|generated|voided`) + `delivery_surface` CHECK (`internal_candidate|client_proposal_candidate|sow_draft_candidate`) + `proposal_status_at_generation` + `generated_by` / `generated_by_label` / `generated_at` + `option_snapshot` jsonb + `source_context_snapshot` jsonb + `commercial_guard_result` jsonb + `omitted_content` jsonb + `draft_watermark` boolean + `approval_state` CHECK (`unreviewed|approved|revoked`) + `pricing_review_state` CHECK (`placeholder|manually_approved|workflow_approved`) + `selected_option_ids` uuid[] + nullable artifact_* columns + audit columns + `updated_at` trigger + workspace-scoped operator-full RLS policy mirroring `0013` / `0014`. Idempotent. Applied to live Supabase via the existing helper.
2. **`lib/proposals/delivery-snapshot-types.ts`** + **`-mappers.ts`** + **`-queries.ts`** — TS shapes mirroring the table, defensive jsonb mappers, RLS-gated read queries.
3. **`lib/proposals/commercial-guard.ts`** — extends `lib/ai/claim-guard.ts`'s existing 26-pattern scanner with the 15 new proposal-finality patterns per `docs/24` § Commercial Claim Guard. Exports `runProposalCommercialGuard({optionSnapshot, sowDraftText?, footerText?})` returning a `commercial_guard_result` shape compatible with the snapshot column. Reuses `scanForBannedClaims(fields, rules)` from claim-guard.
4. **`lib/proposals/eligibility.ts`** — pure `evaluateProposalDeliveryEligibility(proposal, options, snapshotContext)` returning `{eligible, reasons[], evaluatedAt}` enforcing the 13 Proposal Eligibility rules from `docs/24` § Proposal Eligibility Rules.
5. **`lib/proposals/snapshot-actions.ts`** — `generateProposalCandidateAction({engagementId, proposalId, selectedOptionIds?, surface: 'client_proposal_candidate'})` server action (operator cookie-bound; service-role NOT used here — Sprint P5 introduces the service-role path for the public route). Validates UUIDs + auth. Loads proposal + options + optional report-snapshot reference. Runs eligibility evaluator. Runs commercial guard. Inserts the `proposal_delivery_snapshots` row. Logs `proposal_snapshot_generated` activity event. Failure paths log `proposal_snapshot_failed` with violation counts only (NEVER violation text — that would smuggle banned phrases into the activity log).
6. **Activity event types** — add 3 to `lib/activity/types.ts` (`proposal_snapshot_generated`, `proposal_snapshot_failed`, `proposal_snapshot_voided`) + tones/labels in `components/activity/activity-timeline.tsx`.
7. **NO public route in Sprint P2.** NO Generate Proposal Review Link button. NO `Prepare Client Review` unlock. Sprint P2 lands the foundation that Sprint P3+ builds on; the operator-facing affordance to actually generate a candidate from the proposal page is Sprint P3 scope.

**Sprint P2 remains operator-only.** No public route. No client delivery. No SOW Draft yet. `Send to Client`, `Prepare SOW Draft`, `Prepare Client Review`, `Prepare Report` all stay locked verbatim. No email/CRM. No PDF binary. No new package dependency. No Group-B wiring.

3. Live stale-acceptance test (no naturally stale slots in the current engagement; revisit when stale data appears naturally or with explicit operator approval to back-date a row)
4. Cross-browser pagination capture (Firefox / Edge — only Chrome verified via Chrome MCP)
5. Voided-snapshot history filter / search on the panel if snapshot count grows
6. `report_pdf_candidate_downloaded` event emission (defined but not emitted; needs a download tracking surface if PDF binary storage is ever introduced)

E-signature, CRM integration, and BuildOps remain explicit non-goals at the current boundary. Per `docs/19` § Locked Control Policy + `docs/22` § Send to Client Unlock Policy, `Send to Client` is the **last** control unlocked, not the first.
